*Wai dan girman Allah me mutane suke so su cimma buri a rayuwarsu ne idan sunyi k'azafi? mutum sam baya tunanin makomarshi, duniya dai ba gidan baban masharranci ko munafiki bane da gidan uwayen mahassada, dolen dole zamu barta muje mu karb'i sakamakon abubuwan da muka aikata, ki jini dakyau su o'o, duk yanda kika kai ga son kiga kin dasa k'iyayyata a zuciyar mutane to wallahi Allah baya tasiri kaina, ke albishirinki ma, duk lokacin da kikayi irin haka na rantse da girman Allah billahil azeem k'arin bataliyar masoya nake samu, kinga kenan hassadarki da magoya bayanki ta zamemin jari bakida riba, wallahi ni bance masu yimin gadar zare su fasa ba, amma dai su sani cewa Alhamdulillah wallahi ko anyi hassada a gareni taki take zamemin,😂🤪💃 kuma inada tabbas akan cewa babu mai yimin komai sai dai Allahn da ya tsayamin, kuma abin haushin ma wai wad'anda ka d'aukakesu fiye da kowa sune ke yi maka sharri, to Allah ne madogarata mu zuba aga wa zai kai labari, sharri dai karene,  kuma wallahi gaskiya bata k'arewa kuma bata b'uya, a ko'ina take tana bayyana kanta, duk abinki baki isa hana Allah aiwatar da nufinsa kaina ba, in kika kwantar da hankalinki ma komai akwai lokacinsa in hasken da kikejin haushi gareni be disashe ba ai akwai mutuwa da zan tafi in barki gadinta,😎 amma ki sani ko na mutu akwai masu sona dole zasu tunoni kuma zasuyi zancena har addu'a ma zasu yimin abinda ke kuma da magoya bayanki kuka tsana😂 masoyana insha Allah novel d'ina na gaba zakuji irin abinda yake faruwa dani kaina akan kawai ka tsare gaskiyarka da kuma tsarkake niyyarka, hhhh kai duniya ba ruwanka da rayuwar kowama amma kai sai an shiga taka rayuwar.🤔*

                       _page 1 to 5_





*WACECE FATIMA?*

Muhammad asalinsu jajayen buzaye ne, daga agadaz suka taso suka dawo a nijar da zama.


Yana da mata guda d'aya tak mai suna Khadija, Muhammad da Khadija suna da yara biyu, d'aya sunanta Fatima, d'ayar kuma Sadiya.



Fatima tana da shekara shida a yayin da k'anwarta Sadiya take da shekara hud'u.



Wata unguwa suka dawo wadda bata da mutane sosai.



Mahaifin su Fatima ba mazauni bane, yana tafiya Nijeriya ne neman kud'i, wasu lokutan yaje garin kano wasu lokutan kuma katsina, yana neman abinda za'a rufawa kai da iyali asiri,  kuma idan ya tafi yana d'aukar dogon lokaci be dawo ba, dan gaskiya yana yin wata shida ko takwas be dawo ba.



 Gashi d'an abinda ya bar musu ba mai yawa ba,  har yazo ya k'are mahaifiyarsu ta fara neman yanda zata rufawa kanta asiri.



Da mahaifiyar su Fatima taga zama haka fa bazai yiwu ba sai ta fara shiga gidajen masu kud'in unguwar neman aiki domin su samu na abinci, a haka rayuwar tasu ta cigaba da tafiya har ta haifi yaro namiji yaran suka zama su uku kenan.



A lokacin Fatima tana da shakara bakwai a duniya, kuma a wannan lokacin ne mahaifinsu yai wata tafiya wadda ya d'auki dogon lokaci mai tsayi bai dawo ba.



Kuma a daidai wannan lokacin ne 'yar k'aramar yarinya Fatima me k'aramin shekaru ta fara fuskantar k'alubalen rayuwa



Mahaifiyar Fatima tana da k'anwa wadda itama take aure a wannan garin, sunan k'anwar ta-ta Asabe,  wannan kenan.



*YANZU LABARIN ZAI FARA*




Mijin Asabe yana da k'ani sunanshi Manniru, yanzu haka shine zaune a gaban Asabe yana rok'onta ta shige mishi gaba kan neman auren d'iyar yayarta wato Fatima.



Manniru ya kalli Asabe yace "Wallahi Anti ina son yarinyar nan sosai,  kuma da gaske nake zan aureta."



Asabe tai dariya, sannan tace "yanzu saboda Allah manniru me zakai da k'aramar yarinya 'yar shekara bakwai, mema Fatima ta sani a yanzu da kake zancen aure."



Manniru yace "wallahi anti da gaske nake aurenta zanyi, zan raineta har ta girma, zan kula da ita ba tare da komai ya shiga tsakaninmu ba har sai ta girma ta mallaki hankalin kanta."



Anti tai murmushi,  sannan tace "idan kuwa hakane, kuma gaskiya ne abinda kake fad'a to zanfi kowa farin ciki, kuma zan shige maka gaba akan maganar auren."



Manniru yace "to ai yanzu me za'a jira ma,  ai kawai ki tashi kije kuyi magana da mahaifiyarta yanzu."



Anti tace "A'a ka bari ko zuwa gobe ne sai inje muyi magana da yayar tawa, duk yanda ake ciki sai in nemeka in fad'a maka."



Nan fa Manniru ya dage shi lallai sedai ta tashi ta tafi yanzu, to da yake itama tana son abun ya k'ullu sai ta tashi ta tafi gidansu Fatima domin nema wa k'anin mijinta auren d'iyar yayarta Fatima.



Da sallamarta ta shiga cikin gidan, inda Inna ta amsa mata wadda ke tsugunne bakin murhu tana fama da wuta.



D'aki suka shiga da 'yar uwarta suka gaisa,  Fatima ta gaisheta, sannan ta fito tsakar gida ta zauna kusa da k'annenta da keta wasansu.



Asabe ta kalli yayarta tace "yaya kin ganni nazo a k'urarren lokaci yamma har ta gabato ko?"



Mamansu Fatima tace "wallahi kuwa nasan baki saba zuwa a irin wannan lokacin ba, in zakizo da wuri kike zuwa musha hira, Allah dai yasa lafiya?"



Asabe tai murmushi, sannan tace "k'anin mijina manniru ai kin sanshi ko?" Maman Fatima tace "eh na sanshi," Asabe ta cigaba da cewa to shine yaga d'iyarki Fatima yana so," mahaifiyar Fatima ta katseta tace "haba Asabe, wannan 'yar yarinyar me zai ci da ita,  yarinyar da ko k'irgan dangi bata fara ba,  shekararta bakwai fa."



Asabe tace "kash dad'ina dake saurin katse mutum wallahi yaya, ai kya bari in k'arasa maganata ko, in yaso duk abinda zakice sai ki fad'i."



Maman Fatima tace "to ina jinki, cigaba da maganarki," cigaba da maganar Asabe tayi tana fad'in "shine ya hanani sukuni ya tayar dani na taho domin in shaida miki, shidai kawai burinshi a d'aura ta tare, tsoronshi kar a kasashi, harda kud'i ma ya bani yace in kawo miki," ta k'arashe maganar tata tare da kwance gefen zaninta ta ciro kud'i ta mik'a wa mahaifiyar Fatima.



Karb'a tayi tare da irgawa taga kud'i har dubu biyar, zaro idanu tayi, sannan tace duk wad'annan kud'in yace ki kawo min?"



Asabe ta k'ara gyara zama cike da murnar alamun samun nasara,  tace "k'warai da gaske,  ai wannan kad'anne ma,  kuma abin jin dad'in shine yace zai raini yarinyar ne har ta girma, ko ya aureta babu wani abu da zai shiga tsakaninshi da yarinyar na aure har sai lokacin da ta girma ta mallaki hankalin kanta, kinga wannan ma ai abun farin cikine,  kuma kinga kema d'awainiyarsu su uku zata ragun miki ai."



Bayan maman Fatima ta gama jin dukkanin bayanin da Asabe tazo mata dashi sai tace ta bata lokaci domin tayi tunani.



A haka sukayi sallama ta tafi, koda ta koma gidan ta tarar da Manniru yana jiran dawowarta domin yaji yanda sukayi, nan ta shaida mishi yanda sukai da yayar ta-ta.



Tun daga wannan rana Manniru yake sintiri wajen zuwa gidansu Fatima, kuma duk zuwan da zaiyi sai ya d'auki kud'i ya baiwa mahaifiyar Fatiman.



Wannan alherin da yake yawan yi mata na kud'i yasa ta amince mishi da auren 'yar ta-ta, kuma ba tare da wani b'ata lokaci ba aka sha biki amarya ta tare a d'akin mijinta.



Fatima bata son auren nan, hasali ma da yarinta sosai a tare da ita,  'yar shekara bakwai fa, to mema tasan zataje tayi, itama ba dan taso ba aka kaita d'akin mijin da bata sanshi ba balle halayenshi.



Kuma tun daga kan neman auren har aka d'aura ta tare mahaifinta beda labarin komai, haka akayi aure har aka kaita ba tare da sanin mahaifinta, ba saboda baya gari, yana can yana fafutuka a kansu.





_Tirk'ashi! ko yaya zata kasance idan mahaifin Fatima ya dawo ya tarar an aurar mishi da 'ya ba tare da ya sani ba? wai yaya ma wannan zaman auren zai kasance ne?🤔 jin dukkanin amsoshin nan sai ku biyoni a shafi na gaba, nayi typing d'in kad'an ne saboda rashin wadataccen caji, amma insha Allah page na gaba zaifi na farkon nan yawa da yardar Allah_

*🇸🇦ZAMAN AMANA WRITER'S*

*Mamie nah, Tawan, Habibty, Blood, My Heenat & My Meera Mumyn Sultan Aunty Hauwa nayi kewarku mutanena*

                        Page 5 to 10




Haka kowa ya watse aka bar baiwar Allahn nan ita kad'ai cikin d'aki, babu abinda take sai kuka.


Ba'a d'auki wani lokaci me tsaho ba sai ga kishiyarta da wasu mata a bayanta sun shigo d'akin, kishiyar ta nuna Fatima dake rakub'e a gefen d'akin tana kuka,  tace "kunga abar da aka kawomin nan fa a matsayin kishiya to ku fad'amin me zan yiwa kishi anan?"


Gaba d'ayansu dariya suka kwashe dashi, suna nuna Fatima suna dariya, sannan suka fice daga d'akin suna surutu akan banda abun namiji me zaiyi da yarinyar da har yanzu 'yar raino ce.


Cigaba da kukanta tayi bayan fitarsu daga d'akin,  gashi wani irin tsoro takeji sosai dan har ga Allah ita bata tab'a irin wannan zaman a d'aki shiru babu kowa a ciki sai ita kad'ai ba, nan da nan kewar gidansu da na k'anninta biyu ta kamata, sake fashewa da kuka tayi, har kanta ya fara ciwo saboda tsananin kukan da tasha.


Tana jiyo lokacin da ango wato Manniru ya shigo cikin gidan, a yayin da yake magana da uwar gidansa, tsoro ne ya k'ara kamata tare da fargaba matsananci.


Tana cikin wannan yanayin sai taji sallamarshi ya shigo cikin d'akin, kallonta yayi yaga yanda ta takure tare da zazzaro idanu, yanayinta gabad'aya ya nuna mishi a tsorace take.


Manniru yai murmushi, sannan yace "kwantar da hankalinki, ni ba cutar dake zanyi ba, soyayya tasa na auroki, ledar da ya shigo da itace ya ajiye gabanta, sannan yace gashi nan kici in kin gama ki kwanta.


K'arasawa cikin d'akin yayi yai kwanciyarsa, ganin haka yasa ta d'auki ledar abincin da ya kawo mata ta fara ci, abinci ne me rai da lafiya, da kaza had'e da kuma lemo, tana ci tana kuka, nan ta sake tuno da k'annenta da suke cin abinci tare.


Anan ta kwanta bayan ta gama bata motsa ko nan da can ba, ta kasa bacci dan gabad'aya ita ji take kamar tana cikin kurkuku.


Bayan wasu lokuta itama bacci ya d'auketa, washe gari wajen k'arfe shida taji ana tashinta, koda ta bud'e ido matar gidan ta gani tsaye a kanta.


Cikin sauri ta mik'e zaune  matar Manniru tace " ranki ya dad'e sai ki tashi kije ki share mana gida kiyi wanke wanke sannan ki d'ora mana abun karyawa, " ba tare da musu ko jayayya ba tace "toh," dan har ga Allah matar tsoroma take bata, saboda dama ruwan masifaffu da kuma kalar marasa mutunci Allah yai mata.


Juyawa b'angaren da Manniru ya kwanta tayi taga wayam baya cikin d'akin, ko tun yaushe ya fita Allahu a'alam.


Tashi tayi taje ta fara da wanke wanke, sannan ta share cikin gidan gabad'aya, bayan ta gama sharan duk ta gaji ta tsaya a tsakar gida tsaye tana tunanin ta yanda zatai ta fara girkin.


"Au tsayawa ma kikayi ba zakiyi kiyi ki gama mana abun karyawa mu karya ba?" abinda uwargida ta fito tana fad'i kenan.


Fatima tace "Mama bansan ya zanyi ba, ban iya girki ba."


Uwargida tace "dan ubanki karki k'ara kirana da Mama, kuma girki babu fashi sai kinyi, koma yaya zakiyi wannan be shafeni ba," tana gama fad'in haka ta juya tai tafiyarta cikin d'aki tabar Fatima baiwar Allah tsaye tana kuka.


Hakanan ta kwakkwab'a takai mata d'aki, tarar dasu tayi sunata karyawa  suna shan tea me kauri, ta k'arasa ta ajiye zata juya ta fita Uwargida ta tsaida ita, bayan ta tsaya ta dudduba abinda Fatima tayi, kokone ta dama kai da ka gani sai a hankali kasan abaun na 'yan koyo ne, dama da gayya ta sakata aikin dan tasan a 'yan shekarun Fatima beci ace ta iya girki ba balle har ta iya komai, tsabar mugunta ne kawai.


D'aukarshi tayi ta juye mata shi a jiki, saboda tsabar jin zafi da gudu ta fita daga d'akin tana ihu.


Inda tai girkin ta nufa da gudu, sauran ragowar gasarar ta d'auka cikin sauri ta juyeta a jikinta, da yake taga haka Mamansu take yi musu idan sunji ciwo sai tasa musu kamu.


Allah ya taimaketa k'unar be tashi ba, da rana ma haka ta sata ta fito ta d'ora musu na rana, kuma dan mugunta tace wai tuwon shinkafa suke so miyar taushe, shima haka ta kwab'a ta kwab'a da ta gama kuma tai mata shegen dukan tsiya.


Duk girkin da takeyi tun daga kan wajen had'a wuta take shan bak'ar wahala sai ta dad'e kafin ta kama, haka zata k'one wajen hurawa da izawa, tazo ta d'ora girkin ta gama bazai ciwu ba saboda bata iya ba, k'arshe ai mata shegen duka saboda tayi kwamacala.


A haka take a cikin gidan kullum gata yarinya k'arama, amma ba imani a tare da matar nan haka zataita sakata ayyuka kala kala, ita zatayi sharar gidan kullum, tai wanke wanke, kuma tazo ta d'ora girki, in ta kwab'a kuma ai mata duka,  sannan dan bak'ar mugunta duk girkin da ake sakata takeyi uwar gidan basa ci ita da yaranta, girki take musu daban me rai da lafiya, amma ita Fatima sai uwargidan  ta tilastata dole sai taci abinda ta girka, su kuma su zubar da nasu, dama tuni sun cika cikinsu, kawai tsabar salon mugunta ne karta barta ta huta, hatta kayan yaranta ita take baiwa ta wanke gabad'aya dai haka ta maida ita tamkar 'yar aiki, bata tab'a yin girki ta zubawa Fatima ba.


A haka rayuwarta ta cigaba da kasancewa a cikin wannan gidan tun bata saba ba har tazo ta saba da bak'ar wahalar da bata tab'a tunanin fuskantarta ba a rayuwarta.


Komai na gidan ita takeyi, shi kuma miji sai dare yake dawowa, bawai fahimtar meke faruwa yake a cikin gidan ba, ko yana fahimta ma baya tsawatarwa saboda ba irin mazajen da sukafi k'arfin gidansu bane.


Fatima duk tayi bak'i ta rame, koda yaushe kuka da tunani ga damuwa sune abokan hirarta.



Yau lahadi, Fatima kwance take a cikin d'aki, tunda ta tashi take jin jikinta ba dad'i, kuma dama haka take kasancewa kusan kullum, saboda bata samun kulawa kamar da, ga wahalhalu,  ga horo da yunwa, duka da mugunta iri-iri.


Yauma haka ta tashi bata jin dad'in jikinta, hakan yasa bata fito tayi komai ba, tananan kwance sai ga uwargida nan ta shigo ta dad'e tsaye akan Fatima ba tare da ita Fatiman tasan da shigowarta ba.


Fita tayi taje ta d'auko ruwa cikin bokiti me shegen sanyi ta shiga d'akin ta juye ruwan nan duka akan jikin Fatima.


Fatima tayi firgigit ta tashi zaune, tana d'agowa taga itace sai ta maida kanta k'asa tana zubar da hawaye, Uwargida ta rik'e k'ugu,  sannan tace "ubanwa kika barwa aikin naki yai miki, waye bawanki a gidan nan iye fad'amin?"


Fatima ta fashe da kuka, acikin kukan ne tace "dan Allah Mama kiyi hak'uri wallahi banda la.............." kafin ta k'arasa abinda take son fad'i taji ta d'auketa da mari, sannan tace "dan buhun ubaki bance karki k'ara kirana da Mama ba?"


Fatima cikin kuka tace "kiyi hak'uri bazan sake ba."


Uwargida tace "kima sake jikinki ne zai fad'a miki, kuma ki tashi ki wuce kije ki kama aiyukan da kika saba yi tun kafin in fusata wallahi in miki rashin mutunci," haka nan ta tak'ark'ara ta tashi tana makyarkyatar sanyi ta fita, Uwargida ta bita a baya tana cigaba da mita "wato kinje kin mik'e k'afa kin kwanta kin bar baiwarki tai miki aiki, ai tun ranar da kikazo ni na huta da wahala nai bankwana da aiki domin ban tab'a kallonki a kishiya ba, wallahi maza ki kama aiki, in ba haka ba tayama za'a bambance matar gidan da kuma 'yar aikin gidan."


Haka ta cigaba da kasancewa cikin k'angi na bauta gwanin ban tausayi, gata yarinya k'arama amma hakan besa wannan baiwar Allahn taji tausayinta ta rangwanta mata akan wasu abubuwan ba.


Zazzab'i ne sosai ya kwantar da ita a haka Manniru ya dawo gidan ya sameta, koda ya shigo kwance ya tarar da ita tana rawar sanyi, ya tab'a jikinta yaji zafi rau, yace "subhanallahi! Fatima meke damunki haka, ba amsa sai kuka da take shima kukan kanshi baya fitowa sosai saboda tsabar wahala da kuma ciwo dake addabarta.


Manniru ya mik'e tsaye, yace "tashi ki daure muje kemis a dubaki."


Fatima ta yunk'ura ya taimaka mata ta tashi, sedai tana tashi jiri ya d'ibeta zata fad'i yai sauri ya tareta, ganin haka yasa ya d'auketa cak ya fito da ita tsakar gida zai kaita kemis d'in dake kusa dasu.


Uwar gidanshi ya gani tsaye a tsakar gida tana rik'e da k'ugu, ta kalleshi tace "dan Allah dubeka, yanzu daka d'auketa ina zaka kaita, tsayama tukun, idan ka kaita asibitin sai kace musu ka kawo wa, matarka ko wa?" tana kaiwa nan a zancenta ta kece mishi da dariya sannan tai shigewarta d'aki.


Shikam baibi ta kanta ba ya wuce domin ya kai yarinyar mutane kemis.


An dubata a yayin da aka tabbatar mishi da tana aiki masu wahala wanda a 'yan shekarun nan nata be kamata ba ace tana wannan wahalar, kum akwao damuwa a tare da ita wanda zai iya haifar mata da hawan jini domin gashinan ya gwada bp d'inta yaga ya fara hawa, sannan tana fama da zazzab'i me zafi da mura, dan haka a kula sosai, nan dai akayi mata allurai, kuma ya basu magungunan da suka dace tasha, Manniru ya biya kud'in suka dawo gida.

Bayan sun dawo ya kwantar da ita ne yace "Fatima, ki fad'a min idan akwai abinda yake damunki kinji?"

Kai ta girgiza cikin rawar jiki tace "ba komai, nidai kawai gidanmu nake so."

Shiru yayi bai sake cewa komai ba, duk da yana ji kamar akwai abinda ke damunta, sai dai yafi tunanin ko rashin sabone ya janyo hakan, dan haka bai sake kulawa da matsalar tata ba.

Duk da tana fama da wannan ciwon to uwargida bata rangwanta mata ba, sauk'inta d'aya da bata da lafiyan nan Mijinsu yakan dawo jefi jefi dubata, domin yana santa hakan yasa ya aureta, kuma ya bartane ta k'ara girma sannan ya maida ita cikakkiyar mata shiyasa ya fita batunta, idan Manniru nanan bata cika yi mata mugunta a gabanshi ba, amma da zarar ya fita ba ruwanta da ciwonta duk abinda taso sata takeyi.


Bayan kwana biyu jikin Fatima yai sauk'i, amma haka ta zama tamkar mara wadatacciyar lafiya, saboda yau ciwone gobe lafiya, ba dan komai ba sai dan rashin kulawa ga wahalhalu da kuma rashin abinci me kyau.


A haka fatima ta tashi a haka ta saba da wannan bautar da wahala har na tsawon shekara d'aya.


Mahaifiyar Fatima kuwa tana can hankalinta kwance, a tunaninta 'yarta nanan tana samun cikakkiyar kulawa, musamman in ta tuno da alk'awarin da Manniru yai mata sai taji ta samu natsuwa, amma duk da haka uwa uwa ce, lokuta da dama tana tunanin halin da 'yarta take ciki, haka ta cigaba da kula da k'annen Fatima.


Mahaifin Fatima kuwa tun lokacin da ya tafi kafin a aurar da Fatima har aka aurar da ita har zuwa yanzu da akayi kusan shekara uku be dawo ba, sedai yana yi musu aike ko yaya ya samu koda babu yawa kuwa zai aiko.

                        Page 10 to 15




Haka Fatima ta saba da duk wata wahala da take fuskanta a gidan mijinta Manniru


Zaune take a d'aki tana hutawa, tai shiru tana tunani, bayan ta gama uban aikin da ya zamar mata dole sai ga Manniru ya shigo d'auke da sallama, bayan ta gaisheshi tace "dan Allah ka kaini gida inga Mamana da k'annaina, " tana fad'i hawaye na wanke mata fuska.



Manniru ya kalleta yace "kiyi hak'uri Fatima, ki bari ki k'ara jin sauk'i sai in kaiki daga nan nima in gaishesu sai mu dawo tare."



"To" kawai Fatima tace daga nan bata k'ara cewa komai ba har ya gama abinda zaiyi ya fice.



Haka kullum taita faman rok'on Manniru ya kaita gida amma yak'i, gashi matar gidan ta mugun sa mata ido ko wane irin motsi tayi idanunta yana kanta,  ta rasa yanda zatayi.



*Bayan kwana biyu*

Mahaifiyar Fatima ce keta kai kawo a tsakar gida tana 'yan aikace aikace, kamar daga sama taji sallamar mijinta wato baban su Fatima.



Amsawa tayi cike da d'oki ta tarbeshi, tare da karb'ar kayayyakin dake hannunshi suka shiga cikin d'aki.



Zama yayi sannan ya kalleta yace "na sameku lafiya?"



Tace "lafiya lau, ya hanya?"



Yace "hanya Alhamdulillah," fita tayi taje ta kawo mishi ruwa me sanyi tare da abincin da ta girka.



Ana haka sai ga yaran sun shigo gida d'auke da sallamarsu, da gudu sukaje suka rungume mahaifinsu, ya d'aukesu d'aya bayan d'aya ya rungume sannan ya zaunar dasu kusa dashi ya kallesu yace "ina kuma kuka baro yayar taku Fatima?"



Sai a lokacin maman taji gabanta ya fad'i, da sauri ta kalli mahaifin su Fatiman kafin yaran su fara kora mishi bayanin batanan tace "ai ba tare dasu take ba," yace "to ita Fatiman tana ina?"



"Kash ni dad'ina dakai gaggawa wallahi, ka gama cin abincin mana sannan muyi magana," amsar da ta bashi kenan bayan yai mata tambaya.



Har ya bud'e abincin zai fara ci amma jin abinda tace sai ya rufe abincin yace "to nama fasa ci har sai naji inda 'yata take."



Tsoron abinda zata fad'a mishi takeyi, dan bata san ya zasu kwashe dashi ba in yaji cewa aure taiwa Fatima ba tare da saninshi ba, wannan tunanin da tayi shi yasa cikin sauri tace mishi "na aiketa ta siyo mana kayan cefane ne nan mak'ota, kuma nasan yanzu zata dawo.



Bud'e mishi abincin tayi,  ta saka mishi cokali a ciki, sannan tace "bismillah."



D'aukeshi da hira ta fara yi duk k'ok'arinta taga ta kauda hankalinshi daga kan maganar Fatiman, tace "ya wajen aikin baban Fatima, Allah yasa dai an samu nasarar abinda aka fita nema?"



Bismillah yayi ya fara kai abinci bakinsa, sannan yace "to Alhamdulillah, kullum cikin godiyar Allah muke tunda kullum Allah be hanamu na rufin asiri ba, kinga kuwa dole in gode mishi tunda yana bani d'an wanda zan aiko muku, kuma dama amfanin nema shine samun na rufin asiri, ba sai dole ka tara ka ajiye ba."



Haka dai taita janshi da hirarraki dan ta d'auke hankalinshi daga maganar Fatima.



Yana k'arasa cin abincin yai alwala yai sallah, sannan ya sake kallon matar tashi yace "wai har ina kika turamin 'ya haka, gashi har naci abinci nai sallah bata dawo ba, kuma yanzu yaci ace duk inda taje ta dawo gida, ki fad'amin inda kika aiketa inbi bayanta, ko kuma ke ki bita ki dubota."



Mama tace "maganar gaskiya aure tayi, kuma ba kowa ta aura ba sai Manniru k'anin mijin k'anwata Asabe, kuma.........................."



Katseta yayi cikin sauri tare da fad'in "dan Allah banason shirme, ki fad'amin inda 'yata take nace," Maman Fatima ta sake nanata mishi tace "na fad'a maka aure tayi wallahi," mik'ewa tsaye yayi cikin tsananin b'acin rai yace "me kike nufi, kin aurar da 'yata bada izinina ba, me kika maidani ne, saboda kinga bana nan zakiy amfani da wannan damar kije ki aurarmin da 'ya kuma ni ban samu arzik'in a sanar dani ba saboda kin maidani hoto koh, meyasa kikai mata irin wannan auren, nasan tabbas kinada manufarki ko dalili, in banda haka taya za'ai yarinya 'yar k'ank'anuwa me k'ananan shekaru ki d'auketa kije ki aurar da ita ga wani Manniru,  fad'amin dalilinki na yin haka?"



Mama duk ta daburce ta rasama me zatace sai inda inda take, ta duk'ar da kai sannan tace "kayi hak'uri dama fa, dama ni kawai dama," Baban yace "kin gani koh saboda bakida dalili gashi kin kasa koda yin magana, nagode da abinda kikaimin, yana gama fad'in haka sai ya tashi ya fice daga gidan gabad'aya, saboda har ga Allah ranshi yai mugun b'aci.



Fita harkarta yayi gabad'aya, koda ya dawo be kulata ba, yai fushi me tsanani da ita, kuma dole ne ya nuna mata kuskurenta dan kartai gigin sake aikata mishi haka a gaba.



Saida sukai kwana biyu a haka, baya kulata, ita kuma duk abin ya dameta, domin tabbas tasan bata kyauta ba, shiyasa ta yanke shawarar samunshi ta bashi hak'uri.



Da daddare bayan sun kwanta Maman su Fatima tace "Baban Fatima," ya amsa da ina jinki," Mama tace "dan Allah kayi hak'uri banyi da wata manufa ba, naga yaron yanada kirki kuma yace ko ya aureta babu abinda zai shiga tsakaninsu har sai ta girma sosai, abinda yasa ya dage ayi auren yanzu saboda wai kar ya rasata a gaba, ni kuma ganin bakanan kuma bamusan ranar dawowarka ba yasa nai haka, amma dan Allah kayi hak'uri."



Baban yace "in be rasata ba ada yanzu zai rasata, " nan taita bashi hak'uri tana rok'onshi kar yai haka, ba dan komai ba sai dan in yace a bashi 'yarshi shi kuma Manniru ya dage musu a bashi kud'inda yaita kashe musu ina sukeda kud'in da zasu bashi, wannan dalilinne yasa taketa rok'onshi akan tunda an rigada anyi yai hak'uri kawai.



Babansu Fatima ya kalleta yace "kin aikata laifi har guda uku a cikin wannan auren, na farko kin aurar da yarinya ba tare da izinina ba, bayan kuma kina sane da cewa inada hakki a kanta a matsayina na uba a gareta, abu na biyu kin aurar da yarinyar da ko kanta bata saniba balle tasan aure da abubuwan cikinsa, na uku kin aurar da ita ba ason ranta ba, dan na tabbatar bada son ranta kika aurar da ita ba, kuma a matsayinta na 'ya tanada hakki a kanki kiji ra'ayinta akan mijin da zaki aura mata, dan haka in har silar auren nan nata ya zama silar shigarta mawuyacin hali ko shakka babu sai taji haushin abinda kikai mata, kuma bazata tab'a mantawa ba, ba laifi bane dan kin zab'arwa 'yarki miji wanda kika yaba dashi, amma babban laifine iyaye suke aikatawa na aurar da yaransu bayan sunsan cewa basusan komai ba game da aure, ke amma tsakaninki da Allah me kika koya mata na zaman aure da zaman gidan miji? kawai kinbi son ranki kinyi abinda kikeso, dan haka itama hakkinta ne ki nemi afuwarta.



B'angaren Fatima kuwa kodayaushe cikin kuka take tana rok'on Manniru ya barta taje gida ta gaishesu ta dawo, amma sam baya saurararta.



Yau dai ta d'auki alwashin guduwa gidan iyayenta, bayan ya fita wajen aiki saida ta bari kishiyarta ta gama kai kawon da take, tana ganin ta shige d'aki itama Fatima ta zari mayafinta tai wuf ta fice daga gidan, gudu take ba na wasa ba tanayi tana waige kar taga Manniru ya maida ita har tai nisa sosai da gidan sannan ta rage gudun da take.



Bata zame ko ina ba sai gidan iyayenta, da sallamarta ta shiga, dukkaninsu suka amsa mata tare da binta da idanu bakinsu a had'e sukace "Fatima!!!".



Saida gaban mahaifiyarta ya fad'i yanda taga d'iyarta yarinya me kyau dan gaskiya Fatima kyakkyawar yarinya ce irin kyawun da duk kallon k'urillanka k'aryane kace zaka hango muninta, duk da kasancewarta yarinya amma kome tayi kyau take, a tak'aice dai komai nata kyau ne,  tanada tsari ga jiki tubarkallah amma yanzu a yanda tazo musu duk ta rame ta lalace.



Zama tayi a gefen d'akin ta yiwa mahaifinta sannu da zuwa sannan ta gaisheshi, bayan sun gama gaisawa ta juya ta gaisheda mahaifiyarta itama ta amsa jiki a sanyaye.



Mahaifin Fatima ya kalleta yace "Fatima kece kika koma haka," Fatima ta fashe da kuka, mahaifinta ya kalli Maman Fatiman yace "kinga irin halinda kika jefa 'yarki da kanki ko," itadai mahaifiyar Fatima kai ta duk'ar, domin kuwa batada ta cewa.



Saida Fatima tayi kukanta ma'ishi sannan cikin kukan tace "ni wallahi bazan koma gidancan ba in na koma matar gidan kasheni zatai watarana."



Mahaifinta yace "yi shiru Fatima daina kuka, babu wanda zai tirsasaki komawa tunda dama anyi auren bada sanina ba, kuma ga halin da na tsinceki a ciki, saboda haka kwantar da hankalinki babu inda zaki koma tunda bakyaso," sai a lokacin hankalin Fatima ya d'an kwanta har sukaga tayi murmushi, mahaifinta ya kalleta ya girgiza kanshi yana jin wani irin tausayin 'yarshi, zuciyarshi na raya mishi Allah kad'ai yasan irin wahalar da tasha a gidan, duk a cikin ranshi yake cigaba da ayyanawa akan koma menene za'ayi 'yarshi ta gama auren Manniru.







_Tirk'ashi! toh ga uba nan dai ya d'auki alwashi akan babu inda yarinyarshi zata koma, ko ya zasu kwashe tsakaninshi da mijinta, shin shi Mannirun ma zai yarda ya rabu da ita kuwa?_

*Mu zamto masu aikata alkhairi da manta sharrin da duk akai maka, ina jin mamakin wad'anda suke alfahari akan wai su sunada rik'o, wasuma wallahi abinda ya faru be taka kara ya karya ba amma sai su rik'e mutum a ransu harda cewa ni in akaimin abu to yana dad'ewa a raina kafin in manta, wasu kuma kaji sunce ai duk dad'ewa ban iya mance abinda akaimin, to ai ba abu me kyau kake aikatawa ba da har kake tutiya dashi domin rik'o babu kyau, Allah s.w.a da kanshi yace kayi hak'uri hak'uri me kyau, kuma yafiya akan dukkanin abinda akai maka da kuma rashin rik'e mutum a zuciya shi kad'ai zai iya zama tsaninka na shigarka aljanna idan mukai la'akari da k'issar bawan Allahn da manzo ya fad'i a masallaci har sau uku cewa yanzu d'an aljanna zai shigo, ba komai yasa ya samu rabon shiga aljanna ba tun yana raye illa baya kwana da wani a ranshi kuma baya tashi dashi, duk abinda akai mishi a take anan yake barinshi ya wuce a ranshi har abada, shin danme ba zamu zama masu koyi da kyawawan aiyuka irin nasu ba? yawanci abubuwan da muka wofintar muka rainasu kuma sune sukan zama tsanin mutum duniya da lahira, mubi duk wani tafarki na koyarwar manzo s.a.w sai muga dai dai a rayuwarmu, Allah kasa mu dace.*






                    Page 15 to 20




Fatima ta saki jikinta ta shiga cikin 'yan'uwanta tana ta wasa dasu, kuma ta murmure in ka ganta kamar ba ita ba.



Gidan Manniru kuwa uwargida taga shiru har rana ta fara fitowa Fatima bata fito ta fara aiki ba, hakan yasa ta fito cike da mugunta fal a ranta.



Turus taja ta tsaya sakamakon ganin babu kowa a d'akin, ta dudduba ko ina amma batanan, dariya tayi, sannan tace "waya gaya miki barno gabas take, taji wahala ta gudu, dadai kin tsaya in ke mayya ce watarana a fitar da gawarki daga gidan, kuma ba uban da ya isa yace nice sanadi," ta kad'a kai tare da ficewa daga d'akin.



Da daddare bayan Manniru ya shigo gida d'auke da sallamarshi, kai tsaye d'akin Fatima ya fara shiga batanan, ya fito ya wuce d'akin uwargida, tai mishi sannu da zuwa tare da yi mishi tarba ta musamman har yana mamaki, domin tarbar yau tasha bamban da ta kullum.



Yana cikin cin abincin ne yace mata "wai ina Fatima ne na lek'a d'akinta ban ganta ba?"



Uwargida tace "nima tunda na tashi ban sata a idona ba, ni nasha ma tare da ita kuka fita," Manniru yana jin haka yasha jinin jikinshi kan lallai yarinyar nan gidansu ta tafi.



Tashi yayi a daren ko abincin be gama ci ba,  be jira ma gari ya waye ba, ya fita ya doshi hanyar gidansu Fatima.



Yana zuwa k'ofar gidan ya doka sallama mahaifin Fatima ya amsa, yana fitowa suka gaisa, Manniru yace "dama na dawo gidane ban tarar da Fatima ba, shine na taho in gani ko nan ta taho?"



Mahaifin Fatima yace "eh tabbas Fatima tazo gida yanzu haka tana ciki, ta dawo mana a wani irin yanayi, kuma ta tabbatar mana akan bata son komawa gidanka, dan haka kayi hak'uri da ita ka aiko mana da takardarta."



Yana gama fad'in haka yai mishi sallama tare da shigewa cikin gida, haka Manniru ya gaji da tsayuwarshi ya kama hanyar komawa gida, shidai yasan bashida matsala tsakaninshi da Fatima, ya dai ga rama a tare da ita, kuma koda ya tambayeta akan ramar ta-ta bata fad'a mishi komai ba, shiyasa yai tunanin ko kewar gida take har yanzu, da wannan tunane tunanen ne ya koma gida.



Kai tsaye d'akin Uwargidansa ya wuce ya zauna sannan ya kalleta yace "wai idan bananan akwai wani abu dake faruwane tsakaninki da Fatima?"



Uwargida ta dafe k'irji tare da fad'in nashiga uku, wani abu taje tace nai mata?"



Manniru yace "bata fad'a ba nine dai nake bincike akai, saboda tace bazata dawo gidana ba bansan dalilinta na fad'in hakan ba."



Uwargida tace "sedai in ta gaji da zama da kaine kawai, amma ni nasan babu wani abu da akai mata, hasalima koda na tashi ban ganta a gidan ba balle ma ace nice nai mata wani abu."



Tun daga ranar Manniru yaita sintiri akan a dawo mishi da matarshi amma ina mahaifin Fatima ya kafe akan 'yarshi ba zata koma ba, shima kuma Manniru ya dage akan bazai saketa ba.



Da Manniru yaga abun fa ba na k'arewa bane sai yaje gidan yayanshi wato mijin k'anwar mahaifiyar Fatiman ya fad'a mishi dukkanin abinda ke faruwa.



Yayan Manniru ya numfasa, sannan yace "ka tabbatar kuma Manniru ba abinda kayi wa 'yar mutane, kodai kana k'ok'arin kwanciyar aure da itane kullum shiyasa taji tsoro ta gudu?"



Manniru yace "haba dai karka manta yaya na auri Fatima ne saboda sonda nake mata, kuma ban manta da alk'awarin da nayi ba a kanta, kuma ai da hakan na faruwa a tsakaninmu da bamu kai shekaru har uku tare da itaba zata gudu, ni kaina na kasa gane dalilin da yasa ta zab'i rabuwa dani."



Yayan Manniru yace "to ya yanayin zamansu yake tsakaninta da abokiyar zamanta, anya bata cutar da yarinyar nan kuwa?"



Manniru yace "na tambayeta ta rantsemin babu abinda ya had'asu, batama san fitarta ba."



Yayan Manniru yaja tsaki sannan yace "sha'anin mata kuma da kishi idan ma tana cutar da ita fad'a maka zatayi," in Allah ya kaimu anjima kazo sai muje, yanzu zan d'an fitane idan na dawo zan kiraka a waya."



Manniru yace "to yaya sai ka dawo," sukayi sallama ya koma gidanshi ya shirya sannan ya fito zai tafi, uwargidan ta fito ta kalleshi tace "andai ji kunya, akan k'aramar yarinya duk kabi ka susuce sai wahalar da kanka kakeyi wajen sintirin zuwa biko," baice mata komai ba ya fita daga gidan, ya wuce wajen aiki da niyyar zuwa anjima yazo su tafi gidansu Fatiman.



Bayan sallar la'asar yayan Manniru ya kirashi a waya yace yazo su tafi.



Da isarshi wajen yayan nashi ba b'ata lokaci suka d'auki hanyar gidansu Fatima.



A k'ofar gidan sukaja suka tsaya tare da doka sallama, sun taki sa'a kuwa mahaifin Fatiman yana gida, ya amsa musu sallamar.



Mahaifin Fatima ya kalli Maman Fatima yace "ina ganin Manniru ne dan baya wuce shi d'in,  tunda shine ya zama bak'onmu na koda yaushe yanzu, bari inje inji da wacce yazo yau."



Mahaifin Fatima na fitowa yaga ashema ba shi kad'ai bane yau, hannu ya bawa yayan Manniru sukai musabaha, sannan Manniru ya gaisheshi, yace musu "ya kamata mu shiga daga ciki muyi magana, ku d'an bani minti biyu ina zuwa, sukace ba damuwa.



Shiga gida yayi da sauri ya fad'awa Maman Fatima zai shigo dasu ciki, hijabi ta saka ta zauna a can k'ofar uwar d'aka, sannan ya fita ya shigo dasu.



Bayan sun gaisa da Maman Fatiman sannan yayan Manniru ya fara koro jawabi "abinda ke tafe damu kun riga da kun sani magana ne akan Fatima, Manniru ya tabbatarmin da cewa yazo ba sau d'aya ba ba sau biyu ba yana rok'o a maida mishi da matarshi amma kun dage akan ba zata koma ba, kuma shi har yanzu ma besan dalilin da yasa ta kafe ba zata koma ba, dan yace a iya saninshi da ita ba abinda ya had'asu, shine mukazo muji dalili, kuma mu rok'i alfarma a barta ta koma d'akinta."



Mahaifin Fatima ya numfasa, sannan yace "ba zance komai ba akan abinda akewa Fatima ta dage ba zata koma ba, amma bari a kirawota kuji ta bakinta."



Mahaifin Fatima ya k'walawa Fatima kira ta amsa sannan ta taho, tsugunnawa tayi ta gaisheda yayan Manniru da kuma Mannirun, sannan ta kalli mahaifinta tace "Baba gani."



Mahaifin Fatima yace "ba tsugunnawa zakiyi ba zauna sosai Fatima, akwai maganganun da nakeso muyi dake."



Fatima tace "to Baba," ta zauna sosai kamar yanda aka umurceta, Manniru sai kallonta yake yaga ta k'ara kyau sosai, ramar da tayi har ta fara raguwa.



Mahaifinta ya kalleta yace "Fatima inaso ki natsu kiyi musu bayanin dalilin da yasa kika dage akan ba zaki koma gidan mijinki Manniru ba."



Nan da nan Fatima ta fara hawaye, sannan bakinta na rawa tace "Baba dan Allah karku maidani gidan nan, matar gidan kasheni zatayi kullum sai ta dakeni, kullum ni nakeyin girki idan na gama kuma ta dakeni kuma ta zubar tayi wani daban suci ita da yaranta," nan dai ta kwashe komai, dama yaro da rik'e abu a k'wak'walwa, nan dai taita karanto musu duk abinda ya faru tana kuka.



Baba yace "yi shiru ki dena kukan haka, ya juya ya kalli yayan Manniru yace " to kunji irin azabtarmin da 'ya da matar Manniru tayi,  kuma ko ba'a fad'aba daga ganin Fatima kasan cewa tana cikin mawuyacin hali, duba da yanda ta dawo mana ta rame ta lalace kamar ba ita ba, kuma itama a maganarta gabad'aya batace kai Manniru kayi mata wani abu ba, matarka ce ta maida ita tamkar baiwar da ta siya da kud'inta, Allah ne kad'ai yasan irin wahalar da ta gana mata, shiyasa tunda ta dawo kullum sai ta rok'eni akan kar mu maida ita gidan nan, kuma dama ni bananan akayi auren, sannan ga abinda yazo ya faru bayan anyi, dan haka kawai Manniru dan Allah kayi hak'uri ka bata takardarta."



Manniru yace "wallahi banda masaniya akan duk abinda yake faruwa, 
tunda idan na fita sai dare, kuma kwana biyu da naga tana ramewa na tambayeta meke damunta tace min bakomai, ni kuma sai tunanina ya bani kawai rashin sabone da kewar gida yasa take ramar.



Mahaifin Fatima yace "to yanzu dai kaji abinda ke faruwa, sai kayi hak'uri tunda ta dage bazata koma ba."



Yayan Manniru yai shiru yana nazari, can sai ya kalli mahaifin Fatima yace "ayi hak'uri dan Allah da abinda suka faru, na tabbatar da Manniru yanada masaniya akai da tuni ya dad'e da taka mata birki, amma dan girman Allah ayi hak'uri ta koma hakan ba zata sake faruwa ba."



Tun kafin mahaifin Fatima yace komai ta fashe da kuka matsananci, tana maimaita kalmar wallahi bazata koma ba.



Mahaifin Fatima yace "Fatima ya isa haka kukan, tashi kije abinki," kallon mahaifinta tayi tace "to Baba an fasa maidani d'in?"



Mahaifin Fatima yace "eh an fasa maida ke, tashi kije."



Share hawayenta tayi cikin farin ciki tare da ficewa daga d'akin, mahaifinta ya kalli yayan Mannirun yace "to ka ganewa idanunka ko, bata son komawa, ni kuma bazan mata dole ba." 


Yayan Manniru yace "tunda ta dage ba zata koma ba ba damuwa,  kai Manniru sai kayi hak'uri ka saketa, tunda kasan ko ta koma matarka ba zata fasa yi mata abinda take mata ba, tunda ba a gabanka take cutar da ita ba balle ka rik'a tsawatar mata."



Manniru yace "gaskiya ni ina son matata, in kuma an dage sai na saketa to na kashe kud'ad'e da dama wajen auren Fatima, dan haka a biyani kud'ina sai in saketa."



Yayan Manniru ya kalli Manniru yace "haba Manniru ya zaka......................."




Mahaifin Fatima ne ya katse yayan Manniru, yace "ka barshi yai magana ai hakkinshi ne, kuma abinda ya fad'i idan anyi ai ba haramun bane, musamman in kayi la'akari da mu muka nemi ya sakar mana 'yarmu, ya juya kan Manniru yace fad'amin nawa ka kashe gabad'aya na aurenta?"



Manniru yace "kud'in sunada yawa,  banda wanda naita bawa mahaifiyar Fatiman, ga kuma na biki kuma..........................."



Mahaifin Fatima ya katseshi da fad'in "ba sai ka yimin dalla dalla ba Manniru, kawai ka fad'amn gabad'aya zai kai nawa abinda ka kashe?"



Manniru yace "gabad'aya abinda na kashe sunkai miliyan d'aya, idan aka biyani sai in saketa."



Mahaifiyar Fatima wadda tunda aka fara magana batace komai ba, tace "wallahi kud'inda ya kashe bekai haka ba, ko rabin miliyan bekai ba balle miliyan d'aya."



Yayan Manniru yace "haba Manniru hidimar auren ne ka kashe miliyan d'aya?"



Manniru yace "eh mana yaya, kaima kasan hidimar aure fa, abinda na kashe mata daga lokacin da na fara nemanta har zuwa aurenmu na fad'i."



Mahaifin Fatima ya kalli Manniru yace "shikenan zan biyaka kud'inka, amma a yanzu banda wad'annan kud'in,  kuimin alfarma sai naje na nemo tukunna zan biya, ada nan da sati d'aya zan koma, amma yanzu insha Allah jibi zan koma wajen neman nawa da zarar na gama had'a maka kud'inka zan dawo in biyaka, amma Fatima ba zata koma gidanka ba, zata zauna a gidana tare damu, in yaso in na biyaka sai ka saketa, ka amince da hakan?"



Manniru yace ya amince, sukayi sallama dasu suka tafi.







_Topah ko yaya Baban Fatima zaiyi wajen biyan wad'annan kud'in ganin yanda ake fafutukar na kaiwa bakima dakyar? yana samu ya biya d'in? idan an biya mannirun yana sakin Fatiman? ku biyoni_

*Shin akoda yaushe kafin mu rok'i Allah buk'atunmu na gaba muna gode masa kullum akan tarin ni'imar da yai mana a baya?????????? to idan bamayi mu gyara wallahi, mu rik'a godewa Allah akan dukkanin ni'imar da yai mana kafin mu rok'eshi wata, mu kwatanta da kanmu mana muga ko mu da muke bayinsa idan mukayi kyauta muna jin dad'i idan aka yaba kuma aka gode mana, ance yaba kyauta tukuici, menene tukuicin kyauta? shine nuna farin cikinka akai, to hakan sai ya k'ara samu muji muna son sake yin alkhairin sakamakon wannan yabawar da akayi akan kyautar da kayi, to mahaliccinmu wanda ya samar damu ya bamu rai, lafiya, k'afafu, hannu, idanu, ya ni'imtamu da arzik'i yai mana ni'ima da abubuwa da dama yana farin ciki matuk'a ya fimu mu da muke bayinsa matuk'ar farin ciki idan ka fara godewa ni'imomin da yai maka masu tarin yawa kafin ka rok'i wata ni'imar a gaba, kuma hakan ke sawa ya amsa maka bukatunka nan take, saboda baka butulce ba, ka kasance kullum cikin godiya akan wad'anda yai maka a baya kullum, Allah ka bamu ikon gyara ayyukanmu👏👏👏👏👏*




        Bismillahir rahmanir raheem




                    Page 20 to 25





A hanyarsu ta komawa gida yayan Manniru ya kalleshi yace "amma Manniru baka kyauta ba, meyasa zaka shararo mak'udan kud'ad'e haka masu yawa kace wai su ka kashe a bikin."



Manniru yai murmushi, sannan yace "wallahi yaya ina sane nace miliyan d'aya na kashe, kuma nasan kud'ina bekai haka ba, ina matuk'ar son yarinyar ne wallahi yaya, bana son rabuwa da ita, shiyasa nace musu miliyan na kashe saboda nasan basu dashi, kuma duk neman da zai tafi yi, nasan da wuya ya had'a hakan, kaga in ya kasa samu zai hak'ura ya dawo ya mayarmin da matata."



Yayan Manniru yace "to idan kuma ya samo kud'in fa, sai kasa hannu ka karb'a duk da kasan cewa kud'inka bekai haka ba?"



Manniru yace "bazaima samo ba, idan kuma har ya samo to iya kud'ina zan cire in mayar musu da sauran."



Yayan Manniru yaita fad'a yana mita akan abinda Mannirun yayi har suka k'araso gidan yayan Mannirun.



Ya dad'e a gidan yayan nashi suna k'ara tattaunawa akan maganar, yana bashi shawara akan ya kamata yaje ya fad'a musu gaskiyar abinda ya kashe, idan har rabonshi ce zai iya samunta ba sai yayi hakan ba, shidai Manniru amsa mishi kawai yake da to, amma cikin zuciyarshi bawai yanada niyyar yin abinda yace d'in ba, shi a ganinshi ba laifi yayi ba, yayine domin ya samu a dawo mishi da matarshi, saboda har ga Allah yana k'aunarta, kuma bayaso ya rasata.



B'angaren su Baba kuwa bawan Allah tun bayan tafiyarsu ya zauna ya zuba uban tagumi yana tunani.



Maman Fatima ce ta fito daga cikin d'aki ta zauna kusa dashi, ta kalleshi tace "Baban Fatima kayi hak'uri duk ni na jawo faruwar haka."



Kallonta yayi sannan yace "bakomai ya riga ya wuce, sedai a kiyaye faruwar haka a gaba, kawai ki tayani da addu'a Allah ya kawo mana mafita," tace "ameen ya rabbi."



Washe garin ranar da abun ya faru mahaifin Fatima yace wa matarshi ta had'a mishi kayanshi in Allah ya nufa gobe zai tafi, ta amsa mishi da to, shi kuma ya fita yaje da 'yan kud'in da suka rage a hannunshi ya siyo musu kayan abinci.



Washe gari da sassafe Maman su Fatima takai mishi ruwa ban d'aki yai wanka ya shirya kuma ya karya, sannan ya kalli matarshi yace "to ni zan tafi nema, sai aimana addu'a," Maman su Fatima ta kalleshi tace "to Allah ya tsare, ya bada sa'a akan abinda aka tafi nema," ya amsa da "ameen."



Juyawa yayi kan yaranshi yacewa k'annen Fatima bayason yawo su natsu a gida, sannan yace suma suyi mishi addu'a, suka amsa mishi da to.



Fatima ya kalla yace "Fatima," ta d'ago kai ta kalleshi tace "na'am Baba," yace "inaso ki natsu ki saurareni dakyau, har yanzu mijinki be sakeki ba, sai Allah ya sake dawo dani gareku sannan zai sakeki ki rabu dashi gabad'aya, dan haka inaso ki natsu ki k'ara rik'e martabar aure, ki sani har yanzu akwai hakkinsa a kanki, dan haka bana son yawo ki kiyaye."



Fatima tace "to Baba insha Allah zan kiyaye abinda kace," yace "Allah yai muku albarka, sake kallon Maman yayi, yace "ki kulamin da yara sosai khadija, musamman ma Fatima kinsan akwai auren wani a kanta, kuma har yanzu yarinya ce sai ana kulawa a kanta sosai" tace "to insha Allah zan kula dasu tare da kiyayewa."



Nan dai yai musu sallama ya fito, ya dad'e tsaye a k'ofar gida ko me yake tunani oho, can kuma sai ya juya ya koma cikin gidan, a tsakar gida ya samesu inda ya barsu, sai kuma yaja ya tsaya yana kallonsu.



Matarshi tace "Baban Fatima ya ka dawo, yau dai kamar baka son yin tafiyar nan, ko kayi mantuwa ne?"



Mahaifin su Fatima yai murmushi, sannan yace "a'a, ban manta komai ba, na dawo ne ince muku ba rakiya?"



Mahaifiyar Fatima tayi murmushi, tace "to har ina kakeso mu rakaka kuma yau?"



Mahaifin su Fatima yace "har tasha mana," tace "to ba damuwa, gaba d'ayansu suka fito aka rufe gidan, suka k'arasa bakin titi suka samu taxi suka hau sai tasha, suna tafe suna hira har suka k'araso tasha.



Koda sukaje da yake safiya ne ana saurin samun fasinjoji, mutum d'aya dama ake nema motar ta tashi, yana zuwa ba b'ata lokaci ta cika, aka tayar suka tafi, yana d'ago musu hannu suma suna d'aga mishi har suka daina hango motar, sannan su kuma suka juyo suka dawo gida.



Mahaifin Fatima ji yake inama yanada matsuguni acan garin da yake zuwa da ayau babu abinda zai hana ya tafi dasu, dan haka kawai yau yaji yana kewarsu fiye da kullum.



*Bayan wata d'aya*

Yau laraba, Mama tana zaune a tsakar gida ita da fatima suna aiki, su kuma sauran yaran suna can gefe suna wasa.



Sallama sukaji kamar daga sama anata kwad'awa, Mama tace "kamar fa nan gidan ake sallama, waye kuma, suka k'ara saurarawa sukaji lallai nan ne ake musu sallamar, Mama tace Fatima cigaba da gyaran bari in lek'a inga waye."



Tana fita taga mutane sunkai wajen goma, sai kuma gefensu 'yan sanda biyu, sannan ga kuma gawa a k'asa a kwance an rufeta da zani, an kuma shimfid'eta akan tabarma.



Nan da nan taji gabanta ya cigaba da bugawa da sauri da sauri, ta kallesu tace "bayin Allah sannunku, daga ina haka?"



D'aya daga cikin 'yansandan nan ne ya fara bayani kamar haka "sunana Insp Abubakar, wannan kuma Sani, wad'annan bayin Allahn kuma abokan sana'ar mijinki ne."



Sai a lokacin da aka nuna mutanen dake tare da 'yan sandan nan ne ma ta lura da jakar kayan mijinta a hannun d'aya daga cikinsu.



Hawaye ta fara yi, tace "kunaso ku cemin mijina ne a kwance anan akan tabarma?"



Shirun da sukayi ne yasa ta tabbatar da zatonta.



K'arasawa tayi a hankali ta tsugunna a gaban gawar hannunta na rawa tana k'ok'arin yaye mayafin domin taga wanene a kwance, zuciyarta na rok'on Allah yasa ba mijinta bane.



A dai dai lokacin da ta yaye mayafin fuskarshi a dai dai lokacin Fatima ta fito k'ofar gidan itama domin taga me ya tsaida Mamansu ta jita shiru har yanzu bata dawo ba, kuma tanata jiyo maganar mutane da yawa.



Tana bud'e gawar sukai tozali da shi, cikin sauri mamansu Fatima taja da baya tare da zama dirshan a k'asa ta fashe da kuka matsananci.



Fatima dake bayansu ma ta rushe da wani irin kuka me matuk'ar ban tausayi, wajen ta nufo itama tana tafiya kamar me koyon tafiya saboda k'afarta da taji tana yi mata nauyi, duk da taga mahaifinta ne a kwance, amma gani take kamar ba gaske ba, ta kasa yarda da abinda idanunta ke nuna mata.



Tana k'arasowa ta tsugunna kusa da gawar itama ta bud'e fuskarshi, ba gizo idanunta ke mata ba mahaifinta ne kwance Allah yai mishi rasuwa.



Kuka ta fashe dashi tare da yin baya luuuu ta fad'i k'asa sumammiya, dole Fatima taji mutuwar mahaifinta sosai, domin ya damu da ita, yana kuma k'aunarta sosai.



Mahaifiyarta duk ta rud'e a tunaninta itama ta mutu ne, mutanen da sukazo da gawar ne suka kama gawar aka shiga da ita ciki, itama Fatima haka, suna cigaba da bawa Maman hak'uri tare da yabon mijinta suna fad'in mutumin kirki ne, sunyi rashi suma.



Sai a lokacin ne sauran yaran suka san abinda ke faruwa suma suka bar wasan da suke suka taho wajen gawar.



An yayyafawa Fatima ruwa ta farfad'o inda ta sake fashewa da kuka tana fad'in, " Mama shikenan yanzu mun rabu da Baba, ba zamu cigaba da ganinshi ba?" 



Ta sake fashewa da kuka, tanayi itama mahaifiyarta nayi cikinsu ba me bawa wani hak'uri.



Ganin kukan da su Fatima suke yasa suma yaran suka fara kuka.




Innalillahi wa inna ilaihir raji'un!!!!



Tashin hankali wanda ba'a sa masa rana, sedai kwatsam aga afkuwarsa.




_Mahaifin su Fatima ya rasu, wayyo rayuwa, Allah sarki Fatima, Allah ya jik'an mahaifinki ya gafarta masa yasa yana kyakkyawan makwanci, Allah ya haskaka k'abarinsa yasa aljanna ce makomarsa, Allah ya k'ara muku hak'urin jure rashi, ya bamu sa'an tarar dasu, Allah ya jik'an dukkanin wad'anda suka rigamu gidan gaskiya, ayi hak'uri da wannan guntun page d'in, nima mutuwar ta tab'ani gaskiya, sai na dawo hayyacina gobe zan cigaba insha Allah._



_To Allah ya rigada ya d'auki ran Mahaifin Fatima? shin ya abun zai kasance? ya zasuyi da aurenta da kuma Manniru? zai hak'ura ne ko kuwa ya abun zai kasance? ku biyoni muje._

*Wanda duk ya sani to anaso ya sanar, ance ka tunatar domin tunatarwa tana amfanar mumini, amma a yanzu babu abinda ake adawa da ita a duniya irin gaskiya, mutane da yawa basa k'aunar jinta, mafi so da soyuwa a cikin zuciyar mutane da dama a yanzu shine cin naman mutane da zaginsu, kuma kana fara tunatar da mutum a yanzu sai kaji ana fad'in ka jishi can sai kace shi yafi kowa ilimi, tabbas baifi kowa ilimi ba, amma ya fika kai meyi da shi amfani da iliminsa tunda yana amfani da koyarwa irin na manzo s.a.w ne, yana fad'ar abinda ya sani daga cikin ilimin da Allah ya bashi, dan haka mu tunatar da abinda muka sani, duk wanda baya k'aunar gaskiya, kuma yake yin gulmar masu bayyanata wato adawa dasu a tak'aice to in ka bincika zakaga shagalalle ne duniyar kad'ai yakeso, sannan shaid'an ya riga da yai galaba a kanshi, kuma imaninshi yai k'aranci, babu wanda zaice bayason gaskiya sai wanda baya kaunar jinta, wanda kuma baya k'aunar gaskiya to asararre ne, dan haka mu jajirce kan tunatarwa da abinda muka sani, duk izgilancin da za'ai mana akai bekai wanda akaiwa manzo s.a.w ba kuma ya jure, daga k'arshe kuma yai nasara, kuma Allah s.w.a yai alk'awari sai ya cika ko'ina da haskensa koda kafirai da mushirikai basa so, a tak'aice dai duk wanda bayason gaskiya kuma yake adawa da ita wallahi tallahi ko kaffara babu asararre ne duniya da lahira, gaskiya haske ce domin tana haska maka hanya me kyau akan dukkanin tafiyarka, kuma wallahi rik'eta shine jagora da cigaban rayuwarmu in bamu sani ba, Allah ya shiryar damu.*





        Bismillahir rahmanir raheem




                        Page 25 to 30





Kuka suke gwanin ban tausayi, mahaifiyar Fatima cikin kuka tace "ashe shiyasa ka nemi da mu rakaka har tasha, ashe ganawarmu dakai kenan."



Abokanan kasuwancinsa keta faman rarrashinta, suna fad'in tayi hak'uri, suma kansu sunyi rashi, domin mutumin kirkine kuma suna jin dad'in zama dashi, yana da hak'uri kuma beda hayaniya."



Duk da dai unguwar sabuwar unguwa ce ba mutane da yawa, amma ba laifi nan da nan mak'wabta suka cika gidan.



'Yan sanda sun juya, inda su kuma abokan aikinsa sukace sai anyi uku zasu tafi.



Har yanzu Mama da Fatima suna tsugunne a gaban gawar suna kuka, anata basu hak'uri sun rage kukan sai na zuci da kuma hawaye da keta wanke fuskarsu.



Abokanan aikin nashi ne sukace ya kamata ta sanarwa 'yan uwansu dake kusa saboda a samu ayi mishi wanka a sallaceshi a kaishi makwanci.



Mama ta umurci Fatima da taje gidan Asabe ta fad'a mata rasuwar, bata dad'e ba sai gasu sun dawo tare harda mijinta ma wato yayan Manniru.



Nan suka had'a ruwan wanka aka taushi zuciyar Mama akace ta daure ta dake zuciyarshi tayi mishi wanka, tace to, sannan ta tashi ta shiga ta fara yi mishi wankan hawaye na wanke mata fuska har ta gama, tabbas tayi jarumta sosai dan babu wanda yai tunanin zata iya.



Aka saka mishi likkafani aka sashi a makara, sannan aka buk'aci da iyalenshi suzo suyi mishi addu'a.



Mama da Fatima tana rik'e da hannun k'annenta suka taho gaban gawar tare da tsugunnawa, sedai sun kasa yin addu'ar sai kuka da kuka.



Hak'uri akaita basu ana tausarsu akan su dake suyi mishi addu'a, sun dad'e akan gawar suna addu'ar suna kuka, daga k'arshe akazo aka d'auki gawar aka fita da ita domin a sallaceta, dama mijin Asabe ya sanar da Manniru rasuwar, kuma ya samu zuwa kafin a sallaci gawar, sedai be samu damar shiga ciki ba, saboda mutane sunyi yawa a ciki.



Ana fito da ita akai mishi sallah, sannan aka d'unguma zuwa kaishi makwancinsa, bayan an kaishi an dawone abokanan aikinsa ke shaida musu cewa hatsarin mota yayi, sakamakon tahowar wata trailer ta bugeshi, muna yi mishi addu'a Allah ya jik'anshi, yasa yana kyakkyawan makwanci, mu kuma Allah ya bamu sa'an tarar dasu.



To Baba dai sa'i yayi an kaishi makwancinshi, inda 'yan'uwa da abokan arzik'i sukaita zuwa gaisuwa.



Bayan wata uku, bayan Mama ta gama takaba kenan, 'yan'uwan Maman  sukace zasu tafi da ita garinsu ta koma can da zama, inda aka yanke shawarar Fatima ta zauna wajen k'anwar Maman wato asabe, hakan kuwa akayi, Mama ta tafi da k'annen Fatima ita kuma Fatima aka barta nan wajen Asabe.



Sam har ga Allah Asabe bataji dad'in dawowar Fatima gidanta ba, hasalima bataso hakan ba, amma da taga mijinta yayi na'am sosai da zancen yasa ba yanda ta iya dolenta ta hak'ura.



A halin da ake ciki yanzu shekarar Fatima goma da 'yan watanni, dan in bamu manta ba tayi aure tanada shekara bakwai, sannan tai shekara uku a gidan manniru, daga gudowarta zuwa rasuwar mahaifinta 'yan watanni ne da bazasu gaza uku zuwa hud'u ba.



*Bayan sati d'aya*

Bayan sati d'aya da yin rasuwar, ta kama ranar alhamis kenan.



Fatima ce kwance a cikin d'akin da aka sauketa a gidan k'anwar mahaifiyar ta-ta, kanta a sama, tana tuno da mahaifinta, hawaye na wanke mata fuska ganin yanda gabad'aya rayuwa ta sauya mata.



Ko kad'an bata jin dad'in zama da Asabe, hantarar yau daban ta gobe daban, gashi itama ta dage da cuzguna mata, burinta kawai ta gaji da abinda take mata ta bar mata gida.



Haka ta cigaba da kasancewa cikin rashin jin dad'i, saboda matsaloli da take fuskanta a gidan, a wajen Asabe, to dama haka rayuwar ta gada, a cikin 'yan'uwanka ma ba kowa bane yake iya jure rik'e maraya dan ya rasa iyayenshi, ita a yanzu gani take ta takura mata tazo ta zauna mata a gida, shiyasa sam bata ganin walwalarta tun daga ranar da ta dawo gidan, sannan komai in tayi domin ta kyautata mata game da aikin gida bata gani.



Ba'a barinta fita ko nan da can saboda har yanzu da auren wani a kanta, kuma shi Manniru har yanzu be cire rai da zata koma gidanshi ba, musamman yanzu da yaga mahaifinta ya rasu, ita kuma har yanzu Fatima babu k'udurin hakan a ranta, har yanzu bata k'aunar komawa gidansa, kuma koda yaushe akan hakan ta kafe, shiyasa take fuskantar wulak'anci kala kala wajen Asabe, ita a dole anzo mata gida an takura mata, ko duba maraicin yarinyar nan bata yi, haka taita cusguna mata da nau'ikan wulak'anci kala-kala, ita kuma bata da abinyi sai kuka, mijinta yai fad'an har ya gaji, akwai ranar da ya goranta mata ma, yace sai kace ba 'yarta ba, ai inda kara d'iyar yayarta d'iyarta ce, ko hakan ta duba ai ta rangwanta mata, amma kamar busa yai mata akan cigaba da abinda takeyi, haka rayuwar Fatima ta cigaba da kasancewa, inda ta d'an samu sauk'i fiyeda zamanta gidan Manniru shine, za'a bata abinci komai k'ank'antarsa ba ruwan Asabe da ta k'oshi ko bata k'oshi ba, sai kuma harara da hantara da binta da k'ananan maganganu masu ciwo, irin su tak'i aure ta zauna mata a gida kamar wata mayya da dai sauran ire iren wad'annan maganganun.



*Bayan wata biyu*

Fatima ce zaune a d'aki tana hutawa bayan ta gaggama aikin da take taya Asabe, duk da ko tayi bata yabawa kullum a zage take amma besa ta fasa ba.



Asaben ce ta shigo cikin d'akin tace "aikin kenan a gincire ana hutawa, ba'a san aje ayi bautar aure ba, ki tashi kije bakin ti-ti ki siyomin kati insa a wayata ga kud'in, ta mik'a mata kud'in tana cigaba da fad'in saura idan kin fita ki dad'e kisa me gidan ya dawo ya fara yimin fad'an da ya saba yimin a gidan nan saboda ke, muguwar yarinya kawai, tunda kikazo gidan nan muke samun sab'ani da mijina," tana gama fad'in haka ta fice daga d'akin.



Fatima ta mik'e jikinta ba kuzari, ta share hawayen dake zubowa akan fuskarta, sannan ta d'auki mayafi ta yafa, sannan ta fita daga cikin gidan domin zuwa aiken da akayi mata.



Wata mota ce ta taho daga bayanta tana antaya mata hon, tai sauri ta matsa amma be wuce ba, k'arshe ma a gabanta aka faka motar, aka sauke gilas d'in motar a hankali, wani babban mutum ne ta gani a ciki, wanda a k'alla shekarunsa zasu kai hamsin.



Itadai Fatima bata ce komai ba, yace " 'yan mata sannu ko," tuno da maganar mahaifinta na k'arshe a gareta tayi, _"kada ki sake ki kula wani namiji in har mijinki be sakeki ba, kiyi k'ok'arin kare martabar aure"_ cikin sauri ta wuce abinta tabar wajen, cigaba da binta yayi da motar a baya har suka k'araso bakin ti-ti yaga ta tsaya wani shagon saida kati ta siya ta juyo zata koma inda ta fito.



Umurni ya baiwa drivernsa akan ya bita duk inda tayi, haka ko sukaita binta a baya har ta k'araso gida ta shige ciki abinta.



Saida suka d'an jima a wajen dan yaga ko zata fito yaji shiru, hakan yasa ya tabbatar da lallai nan ne gidansu, sai ya baiwa driver umurnin su tafi, kuma yace mishi ya tabbatar da ya lura da unguwar nan sosai domin anjima kad'an zai dawo ya kawoshi gidan su yarinyar nan.



Alhaji Hashimu kenan, hamshak'in me kud'i ne, yanada mata biyu da yara, kuma yanzu haka yanada burin k'ara wani auren, sai kwatsam Allah ya had'ashi da Fatima akan hanyarsu ta komawa gidanshi.



Haka Alhaji Hashimu yaita zuwa har sau biyu Fatima bata fitowa, zuwa na ukun ne Asabe ta tirsasata fita wai dolenta ta fita tunda ba su zasu aureta ba.



Fitowa tayi tana kuka, saboda ganin an sata ta sab'a maganar mahaifinta, sedai tunda ta fito batace komai ba sai kuka da take aikin yi, hakan yasa ba shiri ya sallameta ta koma ciki.



Zuwanshi na hud'u ne ya taki sa'a ya aiko a kirata akan idon mijin Asabe, fitowa yayi domin yaga wake nemanta dan yasan da Manniru ne ciki zai shigo.



Yana fitowa yaga wani wanda zaiyi sa'anshi a wata arniyar mota me d'an karen kyau da tsari.



K'arasawa yayi a dai-dai lokacin da shima Alhaji Hashimu ya fito daga motar, hannu suka mik'awa juna suka gaisa, sannan mijin Asabe yace "kaine kake sallama da Fatima?"



Alhaji yace "eh nine, nan Alhaji Hashimu yai mishi bayanin had'uwarsu, da kuma sintirin da yaita yi bata fitowa, sannan ya k'arashe da fad'a mishi k'udurinshi na son aurenta, kuma ya shaida mishi yanada mata biyu da yara.



Yayan Manniru yace " to maganar gaskiya akwai auren wani a kanta, ya bayyana mishi duk abinda ake ciki, daga k'arshe yace kuma mijin nata yace sai an biyashi dukkanin abinda ya kashe sannan zai saketa."



Alhaji Hashimu yace "indai wannan ne ba damuwa ni zan biyashi dukkanin kud'ad'en nashi, kuma in ya saketa zan aureta, amma ku fara tambayarta ra'ayinta akan aurena idan ta amince to, shikenan, in bata amnce ba to sai in hak'ura."



Yace "insha Allah zan tambayeta inji ra'ayinta, nan da jibi in Allah ya kaimu sai ka dawo kaji yanda mukayi da ita."



Alhaji Hashimu yace "to ba damuwa duk yanda kukai naji jibin," suka sake yin musabaha wato suka gaisa, sannan ya shiga mota driver yaja suka tafi, shi kuma mijin Asabe ya koma cikin gida.



Fatima ce zaune a d'aki zugum tana tunane tunane, tunda wadda zatai hirar da ita bata isheta kallo ba, koma ta kalleta to tabbas ba furuci me dad'i zai fito daga bakinta ba, to dawa zatai hira bayan bata ganin fuska a wajenta, da wannan tunanin Asabe ta shigo cikin d'akin ta tarar da ita,  tace "to ta d'aka ki fito kizo mijina yana kiranki," tana gama fad'in haka ta fice.



Fatima ta tashi ta tafi d'akin Asabe anan ta tarar dashi zaune shi da matar tashi yana jiran shigowarta.



Zama tayi a gefe tare da sunkuyar da kai tace "gani kawu."



Kallonta yayi yace "Fatima akwai wani Alhaji da yake ta sintiri anan k'ofar gidan akan yana so ya aureki, kuma yace zai biya Manniru duk kud'in da ya kashe a kanki dan ya sakeki shi ya aureki, shin kin amince zaki iya aurenshi?"





_Rikita rikita, shin zata amince kuwa? Idan ta amince zai biya kud'in? to inma ta amince wane irin rayuwar aure zasuyi dashi a nashi gidan? domin jin yanda zata kasance mu had'u a page na gaba_

        Bismillahir rahmanir raheem




                    Page 30 to 35



_Abinda na manta ban fad'awa masu karatu ba shine, Fatima tai aure tana da shekara bakwai, tai shekara uku a gidan mijinta Manniru shekara goma kenan, daga nan wahala tasa ta gudo, bayan rasuwar mahaifinsu ta dawo gidan Asabe da zama, kuma a k'alla tayi shekara biyu da rabi a gidan sannan Allah ya had'ata da Alhaji Hashimu, kenan shekarunta sun kama goma sha biyu da rabi, a yimin afuwa._
.............................................


Fatima tai shiru tana nazari, ita kad'ai take tunani a ranta tana cewa yanzu idan ya biya kud'in to na huta da auren Manniru na tsira daga uk'ubar matarshi, to amma ai bazai biya ba in yaji nace a'a bazan aureshi ba, idan kuma nace a'a gabad'aya to zan cigaba da zama da auren Manniru a kaina, kuma bazan rabu da zaman k'uncin da nake ciki ba a gidan nan, da wannan tunanin ta yanke shawarar amincewa domin a ganinta zata samu sauk'i a inda zataje d'in.



Fatima ta d'ago da kanta sannan tace "na amince zan aureshi," mijin Asabe yace "indai kinsan bakya sonshi gwara ki fad'amin kar kiji komai game da maganar k'anina, kasancewarshi d'an uwana bazan tilasta miki komawa gidanshi ki cutu ba, ni ina bayan gaskiya ne, dan haka kije kiyi shawara akai nan da gobe sai ki fad'amin abinda ke ranki," Fatima tace "to," sannan ta tashi ta fice daga d'akin, dan dama duk ta k'osa ta tashi ganin yanda Asabe taketa faman binta da harara a duk lokacin da suka had'a idanu.



Tana komawa d'aki ta zauna sai ga Asabe ta fad'o ta kalleta tace "wallahi gwara ma ki amince da auren mutumin nan yarinya, atoh dan ni bazaki cigaba da zama min a gida ba da auren wani a kanki, kuma dai kinsan daga ni har uwarki babu mai iya biya miki kud'in da Manniru ya kashe a kanki, dan haka gwarama ki rufawa kanki asiri, " tana gama fad'in haka ta fice daga d'akin abinta.



Allah sarki Fatima tunani takeyi tama rasa na kamawa, ta tuno da mahaifinta na raye da komai yazo mata cikin sauk'i, nan hawaye ya zubo mata akan fuska, har ga Allah tana tsoron auren me mata, bama me mata kad'ai ba ita auren tsoro yake bata dan batasan wane hali zata shiga ba a gaba.



Washe gari ko da aka sake tambayar ra'ayinta tace ta amince, dan bata da wani zab'in da ya wuce hakan, dan ta ko'ina ba gwara, dama-dama ma ta amince d'in taji da abinda zata tunkara a gaba kuma.



*Bayan kwana biyu*

Kamar yanda suka alk'awarta da Alhaji haka kuwa akayi, ya dawo aka shaida mishi ta amince da aurenshi, Alhaji yai farin ciki sosai, inda yasa a kirawo mishi Manniru domin ya biyashi kud'inshi.



Yayan Manniru ya kira Manniru aka damk'a mishi kud'ad'en da yace ya kashe, inda ya nuna ai basu kai haka ba, shi ya k'ara yawansu saboda kar a rabashi da Fatiman, Alhaji yace ya d'iba iya nashi yaje ya rik'e sauran ya bashi kyauta, nan dai Manniru ya saki Fatima ba dan ranshi yaso ba, amma ya samu sauk'i tunda kud'ad'enshi sun dawo.



Nan da nan ba tare da b'ata lokaci ba aka saka ranar auren Fatima da Alhaji Hashimu.



Saboda babu wani abu da ya tab'a shiga tsakaninta da tsohon mijinta hakan yasa idda bata kanta.



Duka-duka wata d'aya aka sa rana, dan Alhaji hamshak'in me kud'i ne dan tashi ma bek'i asa sati guda ba, Yayan Manniru wanda ya zame mata kamar ma shine dolenta, shi ya kafe akan adai bari wata d'ayan tayi ai kamar yau ne, sannan ya hak'ura.



*Bayan wata d'aya*

Tun daga ranar da Fatima ta amince da auren Hashimu Asabe ta sakar mata fuska, sai haba haba take da ita kamar ba ita bace me hantararta kullum dare da rana, amma yanzu kamar ba ita ba.



Biki aka shirya na gani na fad'a, inda 'yan uwan mahaifiyar Fatima suka zo daga can garin nasu domin ai biki dasu sannan su koma.



Dama ance rana bata karya to ga ranar Allah ya kawomu, inda a yau talata aka d'aura auren Fatima da Hashimu kan sadaki dubu d'ari da hamsin.



 Bakin Asabe kamar gonar audiga saboda tsabar farin cikin Fatima zatai aure ta tafi tabar mata gida.



Da yamma tayi aka kawo motoci guda goma na d'aukar amarya.



Wani k'erarren gida aka shiga da ita me kyau da kyan tsari, gashi babba kamar wata 'yar k'aramar unguwa, dan in bak'o ya shiga wani wajen a cimin gidan sai an fito dashi.



'Yan uwan Fatima kuwa saida suka shiga ko'ina suka baiwa idanunsu abinci.



Sedai abin mamaki ance musu akwai kishiyoyi guda biyu harda yara amma basu ga ko d'aya ba balle yaran.



Hakan yasa Asabe ta kira mijinta a waya take fad'a mishi, anan ne shi kuma ya shaida mata cewa ai gidanta daban dama yace zai ajiyeta.



Nan suka cigaba da kallon gida da dukiyar da aka narka mishi suna tofa albarkacin bakinsu.



Bayan sun gama ganin ko ina suka yiwa amarya sallama sannan suka fito suka shiga motoci aka maida su gida.



Fatima dake kan tsakiyar wani k'erarren gado tana lullub'e da mayafi, banda fad'uwar gaba babu abinda takeji, ba komai take tsoro ba face abinda zata fuskanta kuma a cikin wannan gidan.



Sallama taji anyi, Alhaji Hashimu ne ya shigo, wani irin fad'uwar gaba ta sake ji wanda ta rasa dalilin jinsa, zama yayi kusa da ita tare da yaye mayafin dake kanta yana zuba uban murmushi.



Ledar da ya shigo da ita ya jawo, ya fito da lemukan kwali kala uku, sai dank'ara dank'aran kaji suma guda uku, sannan yai mata bismillah, ganin bata da niyyar ci ne yasa yaci nashi yabar mata nata yace taci zai shiga yai wanka in ta gama ci sai itama ta shiga.



Tana ganin ya shiga cikin ban d'akin ta kuma tabbatar da ya fara wanka ta jawo kazar da sauri ta fara ci, baiwar Allah ashe tana jin yunwa kawai kunya ne da kawaici yasa ta kasa ci a gabansa, yanzuma turawa kawai takeyi so take ta gama kafin ya fito.



Tana jin alamun ya gama wankan tai sauri tasha lemun sannan ta goge bakinta tare da gyara zama.



Alhaji Hashimu ya fito daga band'aki d'aure da k'aramin tawul, nan da nan Fatima ta duk'ar da kai tare da runtse idanu, domin ganinshi da tayi a haka.



Alhaji Hashimu yai murmushi, sannan yace "tashi kije kiy wanka ki fito kizo mu kwanta."



Ba musu ta yunk'ura ta tashi taje ta shiga band'aki, a ciki ta cire kayanta, sannan tai wanka duk da ta dad'e kafin ta gane inda ake kunnawa ruwan zafi ya zubo, nan dai tai wanka ta gama sannan ta d'aura zaninta a k'irji, ta yafa mayafinta, amma duk da haka jinta take kamar a tsirara take, hakanan dai ta fito tana k'ank'ame jiki.



Alhaji Hashimu ya ciro mata wasu arnakun kayan bacci yace tasa, karb'a tayi tana kallonsu, tare da tunanin yanda za'ai ta saka wannan kayan a jikinta.



Ganin ya nufota yana cewa ta kawo ya sa mata yasa ta shige bayi da sauri, ta dad'e tsaye tana tunanin yanda za'ai ta saka kayan nan kuma ta fita dasu a haka, ji tayi yana fad'in "Baby in bakiy sauri kin saka ba zan shigo in sa miki da kaina, " jin abinda yace ne tayi sauri ta saka kayan ta fito kanta a k'asa tana sunkuyar da kai.



Shi kuwa Alhaji Hashimu ganin kyakkyawar sura da Allah yaiwa Fatima yasa nan da nan ya juye kamar wani mashayi, yasha mamaki, duk da kasancewarta yarinya amma Allah yai mata kyau da kyan sura.



Jawota yayi jikinshi da k'arfi ya rungumeta, nan ta fara kiciniyar k'wacewa saboda abubuwan da ya fara yi mata, da k'yar da sid'in goshi Allah ya bata sa'a ta k'wace tai cikin bayi da gudu ta kulle ta ciki.



Tun daga ranar da aka kawota haka sukeyi kullum har sati d'ayan da zaiyi ya k'are ba tare da auratayya ta shiga tsakaninsu ba, hakan ba k'aramin haushi ya baiwa Alhaji Hashimu ba.



Kuma daga ranar da kwana bakwan ta cika dolenshi ya raba kwanaki, inda ya raba zai rik'a kwana biyu a d'akin kowacce.



Fatima tsabar tsoro ne yasa ta kasa bashi hakkinshi na aure, haka sukaita yi har tsawon wata d'aya.



A yau laraba, ta kama watan su d'aya da kwana d'aya kenan, Fatima ta tashi da ciwon mara matsananci, ashe period ne, da yake yau ta fara kuma shine na farko, hakan yasa ta wahala har saida aka kaita asibiti aka dubata sannan aka dawo da ita gida.



Alhaji Hashimu kuwa ganin Fatima yarinya ce k'arama yasa yake lallab'ata yana binta a hankali, amma a yanzu jira yake ta kammala period d'inta, a ranshi ya shirya koma menene zai faru sedai ya faru amma babu abinda zai hanashi cimma burinshi a kanta, jira kawai yake ta kammala period d'inta.





_Ayi hak'uri da wannan k'arfin hali kawai nakeyi wallahi banjin dad'in jikina._

        Bismillahir rahmanir raheem




                    Page 35 to 40





Bayan sati d'aya Fatima ta gama period d'inta, wato jinin al'adanta ashe ita bata saniba Alhaji Hashimu yananan yana ankare da ita, jira yake yaga ta fara sallah ya far mata.



Yau laraba, hausawa sukace ta bawa ranar samu, to haka ta kasance ga Alhaji Hashimu, kuma a ranar sai Allah ya taimakeshi a d'akin Fatiman yake, Allah-Allah kawai yake dare yayi ya shiga d'akin.



Ko da dare yayi har Fatima ta fara bacci, kuma saboda ta saba yanzu Alhaji baya shigowa d'akinta tunda ta fara al'ada shiyasa bata wani damuwa da sai ta sakawa k'ofarta makulli, har bacci ya fara d'aukarta taji kamar wani abu yana binta a jiki, kamar alamun waiwayi, hannu takai da niyyar sosa wajen kawai taji hannun mutum yana tab'ata.



Zumbur ta tashi a razane tare da yunk'urin zunduma ihu, Alhaji yasa hannu ya toshe bakinta, yace "ke nine, nan ta fara rok'onshi ya k'yaleta amma ina in za'a kasheshi ma besan me take cewa ba, shidai burinshi kawai ya biya buk'atar gabanshi da ita.



Ganin ko sauraronta yak'i yi balle yaji rok'on da take yi mishi yasa ita kuma tak'i bari ya cimma burinshi a kanta, sai faman kiciniya suke kamar 'yan dambe, haka taita tare farmakin da yake kawo mata da hannayenta da k'afafuwanta a ranar su suka zame mata kariya.



Duk ya had'a gumi ya gaji, ranshi yai mugun b'aci, tsayawa yayi yana kallonta, zuciyarshi na fad'in wannan wace irin yarinyace da shegen kafiya ga k'arfi kamar namiji, nan ya cigaba da kiciniyar biyawa kanshi buk'ata ta ko wane hali koda ma bataso, ita kuma ta kafe tak'i yarda saboda tsabar tsoron dake ranta.



Haushin hakan yasa ya wanka mata mari lafiyayyu har uku, sannan ya bita da duka ta ko'ina, saida yai mata shegen duka saboda haushin ta hanashi biyawa kanshi buk'ata, nan ya tsallaketa yabar d'akin yaje ya canza kaya ya fita yabar gidan, a ranar ma ko gidan be kwana ba.




_Hattara magidanta maza, idan har akwai masu irin wannan halin to wallahi suji tsoron Allah su daina, domin ba haka aka koyar a yiwa mace idan za'a kusanceta ba, duk abinda mutum be saba dashi ba dole sai da rarrashi da wasanni, da  kuma kyakkyawan kalami masu kwantar da hankali, balle ita da take yarinya ma ga k'uruciya ga kuma tsoro da ya kamata, sannan shi yaje mata a wani irin yanayi wanda sam be dace ba yin hakan, anaso miji ya kusanci matarsa da yardarta saboda samun ingantacciyar gamsuwa, bayan ya samu amincewarta da farko sai yai mata wasanni had'e da kalamai masu dad'i da narkar da zuciya wad'anda zaisa itama taji shauki a kanshi in yaso daganan koma menene sai ya biyo baya, ya kamata masu irin wannan halin gaskiya su gyara in ba hakaba in kuka cigaba da fad'awa matanku ba wata 'yar soyayya ba wasanni to tabbas duk in zakuyi kwanciyar aure dasu sai sunji tsoronku kuma har a fuskarsu sai kunga canji alamun basaso ku kusancesu, saboda kune kad'ai zakufi morewa banda su, sabida ba d'an wani wasa wanda zaisa suji suma lallai suna cikin wani yanayi na buk'atar hakan a dai-dai lokacin, a'a kowane miji yanzu yawanci su kawai kansu suka sani, burinsu su fad'awa mace ko ta yaya ne, sudai burinsu suji sun samu natsuwa, dan haka wannan babban kuskure ne maza suke aikatawa mace ma fa raine da ita yanda kake so ka gamsu itama haka, to meyasa bakwa tsayawa wajen kula da yi musu wasannin da zaisa duk ku samu biyan buk'ata kai da ita cikin kwanciyar hankali? dubi fa yanda Alhaji ya fad'a mata kamar wanda zaiyi fyad'e sai kace ba matarshi ta sunna ba, wallahi da yawan maza yanzu basu san yanda zasu bi da matansu ba wajen kwanciyar aure, don girman Allah masuyin irin wannan su gyara._



Cikin kuka taja jikinta ta koma kan gadon ta rab'e gefe guda tanata kuka, jikinta duk yai tsami ga ciwuka da ya ji mata na bugun da tasha, hakanan ta kwanta cikin kuka da rashin jin dad'in jikinta, haka ta kwana da zazzab'i jikinta duk ba dad'i.



Shi kuwa Alhaji cewa yayi gida biyu maganin gobara, yana barin gidan gidan uwargidanshi ya wuce acan ya kwana.



Washe gari haka ta tashi jiki ba k'wari amma da sauk'i, saboda ta iya yunk'urawa ta samu tai wanka ta gyara d'akin da ta kwana ciki, nan ne kad'ai dama take gyarawa, saboda gidan akwai ma'aikata masu aiki a ciki ta ko'ina.



Haka ta tashi ta wuni Alhaji ko lek'e saboda haushinta da yakeji, abinci dama batada matsalarshi, dan akwai 'yan aiki, taci na safe taci na rana taci na dare, sedai har dare yayi bataga Alhaji ba.



Ita kad'ai ta kwana a gidan, washe gari kuma a ranar ne dama zai koma wajen abokiyar zamanta dama kwana bibbiyu yakeyi, jikinta yai kyau ta warware ta cigaba da hidindimunta, Fatima tun ranar da Alhaji Hashimu ya daketa bata k'ara ganinshi ba saida kwanakin girkinta suka sake zagayowa kanta.



A ranar da girki ya sake zagayowa kanta ma a ranar ma wata sabuwar artabun sukayi, inda ya sake yi mata shegen duka saboda tak'i yarda ya kusanceta, ita kuma har ga Allah tsorone ya hanata yarda, shi kuma yak'i bi da ita ta yanda ya dace.



Haka suka cigaba da kasancewa duk ranar girkin Fatima in Alhaji Hashimu yazo zai kusanceta bata yarda shi kuma sai yai mata dukan tsiya



A tak'aice dai saida Fatima ta cika rabin shekararta a gidan, dama ta auri Alhaji Hashimu tanada shekara goma sha biyu da rabi, a gidanshi ta cikashe rabin ya zama shekararta goma sha uku.



Kuma a wannan lokacin ne Alhaji Hashimu ya rage dukanta, saboda ya fara tunanin kar wataran yaje ya kashe musu 'ya da duka gwara kawai ya rabu da ita tunda bazai iya ganinta ya k'yaleta ba, in kuma ta hanashi kanta zuciya tana d'ibarshi ya doketa, to kar wataran ya kasheta gwara kawai su rabu, kuma kanme ma zai tsaya yarinya k'arama tana wahalar dashi, tunda yanada kud'i ai kowace kalar mace yake son aura ba zata yi mishi wahalar samu ba, da wannan shawarar ya tsaya a kai.



Yau ta kama Alhamis, Fatima ce zaune a falo tana kallo sai ga Alhaji Hashimu ya shigo cikin d'akin, Fatima ta gaisheshi ya amsa, sannan ya mik'a mata wata farar takarda a ninke, ta karb'a, sannan yace "wannan takardar dake hannunki takardar sakinki ce na sakeki saki d'aya, ki shirya zansa driver ya maidake gidanku, tunda zaman gidan naku kika zab'a kuma kikafi so," yana gama fad'in haka ya fice daga d'akin.



Alhaji yasa driver ya d'auki Fatima ya nufi hanyar gidansu da ita, shi kuma Alhaji ya wuce wajen aiki abinshi, an godewa Allah da shi Alhaji bece a biyashi komai nashi da ya kashe ba, da harda million d'ayan wajen Manniru sai sun biyashi, to sai bece a biyashi komai ba, tunda yanada su ko girgiza dukiyarshi batayi ba dan kawai ya tab'a 'yan kud'i kad'an aciki yai aure, shiyasa be bi ta kansu ba.



Driver yana zuwa k'ofar gidan ya faka mota, Fatima ta fito daga cikin motar ta shiga ciki, shi kuma yaja motar ya k'ara gaba.



Fatima ta dad'e tsaye a zaure tana tsoron abinda zai biyo baya idan Asabae taji abinda ya dawo da ita cikin gidan nan.



Gabanta banda fad'uwa babu abinda yake, duk tabi ta damu, amma ba yanda ta iya dole ta shiga dan a garin kaf bata da gidan da ya wuce nan d'in.



Ji tayi kamar za'a fito sai tayi sauri ta cigaba da tafiya, caraf suka ci karo da Asabe a hanyar shigowa, Asabe tace "wai wai wai wai wai, kaga 'yar banzar yarinya so kike ki jimin ciwo, jiya na gama maganarki ni da mijina ina cewa zanzo gidan jibi ashe kina tafe, irin wannan ai sanarwa zakiy zakizo ko a waya ne tunda mijinki yanada lambar mijina, amma yanzu da kika kwaso jiki kika taho da bananan kuma na fita unguwa fa."



Da alamu da fita ma zatayi, ganin Fatiman yasa ta juya suka koma ciki, ita dai Fatima batace komai ba, dan ta lura Asabe ta d'auka tazo gaishesu ne, d'akin Asabe suka shiga, Asabe ta zauna a kujera, Fatima ta samu waje itama daga gefe ta zauna, sannan ta gaishe da Asabe.



Asabe ta amsa gaisuwar,  sannan ta mik'e taje ta bud'e pridge ta d'aukowa Fatima ruwa, ta dawo ta ajiye gabanta, sannan ta koma ta zauna, tace "ya mijin naki, ina fatan dai kuna zaune lafiya koh?"



Fatima gabanta na fad'uwa ta mik'awa Asabe takardar da Alhaji Hashimu ya bata ba tare da tace mata komai ba, Asabe ta karb'a,  sannan tace "wannan takardar ta mecece kuma?"



Fatima bakinta na rawa tace "Alhaji ne ya bani yace wai takardar sakina ce ya sakeni saki d'aya."



Asabe cikin sauri ta mik'e tsaye tare da zaro idanu tana sallallami da k'arfinta, tace "yanzu Fatima wannan auren ma saida kika kasheshi hankalinki ya kwanta ko, wato kinji dad'in zaman gida bari ki dawo mu jeru muna had'a kafad'a ko."



Fatima ba abinda take rbanda kuka, haka Asabe taita bambaminta ta gaji, ruwan da ta kawo mata ne ta d'auke tana fad'in harda kawo miki ruwa da sauri ni a zatona zan tarbi bak'uwa ashe ba bak'unta akazo yi ba anzo zama ne, mayar da ruwan tayi cikin pridge ta cigaba da fad'in tunda zama kikazo yi ai kinsan inda komai yake ai ba sai na tarbeki dashi ba."



Duk hakan be mata ba ta kira wayar mijinta yana d'agawa ba sallama ba gaisuwa ba komai tace yazo gida yanzu ba lafiya.



Nan da nan ko ya taho a firgice hankalinsa a tashe, yana zuwa Asabe ta kwashe komai ta fad'a mishi tare da mik'a mishi takardar da aka ba Fatiman.



Karb'a yayi ya karanta, sannan yaja tsaki, yace "yanzu akan haka kika tayarmin da hankali haka, in Allah ya kawo wa'adin abu mu mun isa dakatar dashi ne ko mu hana afkuwarsa?"



Asabe tace "au hakama zakace, yarinya ta kaso aure kace ba wani abu ba," mijin asabe me suna Tanimu yace "ba yanda za'ai ta kashe aurenta da kanta sedai in k'addara ta hukunta faruwar hakan, ya juya kan Fatima yace ke Fatima me ya had'aku ya sakeki?"



Fatima cikin kuka tace "wallahi ina zaune ina kallo yace in shirya a kaini gida ya bani wannan takardar yace ya sakeni saki d'aya."



Tanimu yace "kinji ko, to munji nata duk da bazan yarda da zancenta ba kai tsaye, amma duk da haka bari in kira shi Alhajin muji shi kuma me zaice "aka kira Alhaji, Alhaji yace kawai abar maganar tunda Allah ya hukunta rabuwarsu, Tanimu be matsa mishi sai yaji ko menene ba yace shikenan sukayi sallama, ya kashe wayar.



Tanimu ya juya ya kalli matarsa Asabe yace "kinji koh, ya kasa cewa komai, ni dama tunda naji yanada mata naji tsoron mu bashi ita, Allah kad'ai yasan me suke mata shi da matanshi, tunda yarinyar hak'urarra ce ga kuma zurfin ciki ba fad'a mana zatayi ba ko ta kawo k'ara, Asabe tace "uhum komai a d'auki alhakin mata, ita waliyyiyace da bata laifi kenan ko me."



Tanimu yace "koma dai menene ya riga da ya faru, Allah ya fito mata da wani mijin nagari, ni zan koma wajen aiki dan meeting ma muke nayi bak'i kikasa na basu hak'uri nace su jirani na tsallakesu na taho, ke Fatima tashi ki koma d'akinki ki zauna kinji, Allah ya dubi maraicinki ya tallafawa rayuwarki ya baki miji nagari, Fatima ta mik'e ta fita daga d'akin inda Asabe ta rakata da harara, shima mijin kallo yabi Fatiman dashi, sannan ya kalli matarshi yace "wallahi Asabe yarinyar nan tausayi take bani matuk'a, wallahi banda mugun bak'in halin matar Manniru da shi zansa ya sake aurenta tunda yana k'aunarta.



Itadai Asabe bata ce uffan ba, daga uhm, sai um, sai hum, taketa cewa,  ganin ta barshi yanata magana yasa ya kalleta ya girgiza kai kawai ya fice yabar gidan domin komawa wajen aikinshi.
