                    Page 40 to 45





Haka rayuwar Fatima ta cigaba da kasancewa a gidan su Asabe, da dad'i ba dad'i, yau fari gobe bak'i a haka ake.



Abinda Asabe take mata in da sabo yaci ace ta saba, shiyasa yanzu duk wata tijara da zatai mata baya damunta sosai kamar da.



Bayan fitowarta daga gidan Alhaji Hashimu da wata d'aya mahaifiyarta tazo harda k'annen Fatiman, suma sun k'ara girma, Fatima taji dad'in ganinsu sosai, tayi matuk'ar farin ciki mara misaltuwa.



A d'akin Fatima suka sauka, inda Fatima ta shaidawa mahaifiyarta irin halin da take ciki anan, mahaifiyarta ta bata hak'uri tace mata ta k'ara hak'uri wata rana sai labari, itama can hak'urin take balle ta d'auketa su koma can gabad'aya, kuma yanzu tana cewa ma zata d'auketa wata maganar ce zata taso mara dad'i daga wajen Asaben tace dan ba ita ta haifeta ba,  ba mamaki ma hakan ya zama sanadin tab'a zumuncinsu, da wannan maganganun ne mahaifiyar Fatima ta samu ta k'ara tausar zuciyar 'yarta.



Satinsu d'aya a gidan sannan suka koma can garin mahaifiyar ta-ta wato Agadez, inda suka bar Fatima cikin kewarsu.



*Bayan shekara hud'u*

Zuwa yanzu Fatima an fara girma an mallaki hankalin kai, duk da dama can koda take yarinya halayenta duk na manya ne, akwai natsuwa da kuma hak'uri.



Yanzu shekarun Fatima Goma sha bakwai.



Fatima kyakkyawar yarinya ce, tana k'ara girma kyawunta na k'ara fitowa, doguwar yarinya ce, kuma tana da jiki matsakaici, ita ba me k'iba ba kuma ba ramammiya ba, tana da manyan idanu da dogon hanci, gashi kuwa hum wayyo Allah har gadon baya, Fatima kyakkyawa ce acikin sahun farko na kyawawa, kuma itace k'arshe a fannin kyau daga kanta an rufe babin kyawawa.



Kamar yanda naketa fad'awa masu karatu ne cewa, Allah ya wadata Fatima da hali nagartacce, ga d'abi'a ta k'warai, bata da hayaniya sam, to dawa ma zatai hayaniyar ita da ta taso ba abokai bata cikin 'yan, uwanta, hak'urarriyar yarinya ce, ga kawaici, masu karatu kuji baiwa irin ta ubangiji, duk da kasancewarta a lokacin bata wani yi karatu me zurfi ba amma ta samu irin wannan baiwar, cikin mutane da wuya yanzu a samu wad'anda suka had'a dukkanin wad'annan abubuwan sai d'ad'd'aiku.



A tak'aice dai kyawun fuska da na jiki gaba d'aya suka k'ara bayyana ga Fatima, dama tun tana yarinya tana da farin jini balle kuma yanzu da Allah ya k'ara k'era halittarta tafi da k'awatuwa.



Samari suka fara yi mata caaaa akai suna sonta, dan babu ranar da bazatai saurayi ba sedai in bata fita ba, amma tana fita samari suka ganta sai an samu wanda ya biyota gida yanaso, sedai wanda yafi kowa naci a kanta shine Mu'az, amma duk bata fitar musu sai ranar da Asabe ta korota dole ta fito ba yanda ta iya.



Mu'az ba laifi kyakkyawane, sun had'u da Fatima a sanadin wani biki da aka gayyaci Asabe d'iyar k'awarta ce zatai aure, sai suka tafi tare da Fatiman, to shi Mu'az yana d'aya daga cikin abokan ango, a tak'aice ma shine babban abokin ango, to a wannan ranar ne yaga Fatima, yayi k'ok'arin yaga sun keb'e dan suyi magana amma bega fuskar hakan ba, bincike yayi a kanta har ya gano inda take shine fa yaketa sintiri a k'ofar gidansu kullum.



Wasu lokutan Asabe take sata dole ta fita, wasu lokutan kuma idan Asaben bata kusa korar yaran take.



Yau Alhamis, Fatima ce zaune a tsakar gida tana yayyanka albasa zata zuba a abinci, sai suka ji sallamar yaro, Fatima ta amsa, yaro yace "wai ana kiran Fatima inji wani a waje."



Fatima ta rage murya dan karma Asabe taji ta masifeta tace "kaje kace mishi batanan."



Asabe dake cikin d'aki tace "kai jeka kace tana zuwa, yaro ya juya domin fad'awa Mu'az abinda akace, Asabe ce ta fito daga d'akinta ta cigaba da magana tace tashi maza ki wuce kije, bani wuk'ar in cigaba da aikina, yarinya kina da farin jini sai shegen bak'in hali irin naki, in ba haka ba ace duk masu zuwa neman aurenki babu wanda zaki zab'a ki aura, kai wallahi barima Tanimu ya dawo ai dama shi ke goya miki baya akan komai," ita dai Fatima batace uffan ba ta zari mayafinta ta fito, har ta fito daga gidan Asabe mita take tana zazzaga fad'a.



Tana fita ya sakko daga kan saman motarshi inda da yake zaune akai yana zaman jiran fitowarta, ya tsaya a tsaye tare da jingina a jikin motar yana kad'a makulli, harda gyara tsayuwarshi, yana kallonta yana zuba uban murmushi, inda tai mishi sallama ya amsa, ta gaisheshi, daga nan kuma bata k'ara cewa komai ba, sai can bayan wasu mintuna Mu'az ya kalleta yace "haba Fatima zuwa yanzu yaci ace kin tsaya kin fahimci manufata na son aurenki mana, idan kuma bakya sona ne sai ki fad'amin.



Fatima ta sunkuyar da kai, sannan tace "ba haka bane, kawai ni maza tsoro suke bani, musamman idan na duba abubuwan da suka faru dani a baya, shiyasa ba wani abu ba."



Mu'az ya saki numfashi,  sannan yace "haba Fatima, ai ba duka aka taru aka zama d'aya ba, kowa da irin halinshi, ki bani dama insha Allah nai miki alk'awarin rik'eki amana da kuma zama dake har abada."



Fatima tai shiru bata ce komai ba, Mu'az ya sake cewa "Fatima kinyi shiru bakice komai ba?"



Fatima tace "ka bani lokaci zanyi tunani akai."



Mu'az yace "to kamar yaushe Fatima?"



Fatima tai shiru na 'yan dak'ik'u, can sai tace "kamar nan da sati d'aya."



Mu'az ya zazzaro idanu yace "gaskiya sati yai nisa, ina laifin kice gobe in dawo," Fatima tai murmushin nan nata dake k'ara fito da kyawun fuskarta, sannan tace "gaskiya banda gobe, sedai nan da sati d'ayan kamar yanda nace maka."



Magiya yaita mata akan ta rage kwanakin da ta d'iba na yin tunani kawai, dole ta rage aka barshi a nan da kwana uku.



Godiya yai mata akan kwanakin da ta rage mishi, sannan tace mishi tanaso ta shiga gida nan sukayi sallama ta shiga cikin gida shi kuma ya fad'a cikin motarshi ya tafi cike da farin cikin yau Fatima ta saurareshi.



Da daddare bayan Tanimu ya dawo ya kira Fatima, Fatima daga d'akinta ta amsa ta taho ta samu gefen d'akin ta zauna sannan tace "gani kawu."



Tunda ta gansu tare da Asabe tasan ta kawo k'ararta ne, Tanimu ya kalleta yace "Fatima shin da gaske ne akwai masu zuwa neman aurenki?"



Fatima ta sunkuyar da kai sannan tace "eh da gaske ne."



Tanimu yace "to meyasa kika k'i ba d'aya daga cikinsu, wanda kika yaba da halinshi dama ya turo a tsaida magana?"



Fatima tai shiru ta kasa cewa komai, ganin hakan yasa Tanimu yace "to ya kamata a cikinsu ki fitar da guda d'aya daga ciki in akwai wanda kikeso, idan kuma babu wanda yai miki a cikinsu to saurarensu bashida amfani sai ki sallamesu mu jira Allah ya kawo miki nagari," Fatima tace "to," sannan ya sallameta ta tashi ta koma d'akinta, haka kawai taji zuciyarta bata mata dad'i, batasan me Asabe tace mishi ba ya tsareta da wad'annan tambayoyin, bayan ita tasan a iya saninta dukkanin masu zuwa wajen nata ba ita take gayyatosu ba, da yawansu ma bata sansu ba, saboda ba saurarensu take ba, banda Mu'az ba wanda ta tab'a saurara, sai kuma wad'anda Asabe ke korota dole ta fito ta kulasu, amma taji yanayin maganarshi kamar akwai tuhuma a ciki, haka  yasa ta fashe da kuka, tare da k'udurtawa a ranta cewa idan Mu'az ya dawo zata bashi dama ya turo.



*Bayan kwana uku*

Mu'az ya sake dawowa domin yaji inda maganarsu ta kwana, bayan Fatiman ta fito sun gaisa ya kalleta yace "Fatima to gani na dawo domin inji shawarar da kika yanke a kaina, shin kin amince dani in turo magabatana?"



Fatima tace "na amince da aurenka, Allah ya tabbatar mana da alkhairi a ciki, in babu alkhairi Allah ya musanya mana da abinda yafi kasancewarmu tare alkhairi."



Mu'az yace "amin masoyiyata, insha Allah aurenmu alkhairi ne ma naji a jikina, kuma nayi farin ciki da amincewa dani da kikayi, bayan haka Fatima mu ba yara bane, inada mata d'aya ke nake fatan ki zama ta biyu, kema kuma kinga kin tab'a yin aure, dan haka ba sai mun tsaya jan dogon lokaci ba, kawai ki bani dama in turo magabatana a tsaida maganar aurenmu dake dan Allah."



A zuciyar Fatima tace ko baka rok'a ba dama inada niyyar baka damar turowa, a fili kuwa kallonshi tayi tace  "shikenan zan sanar da Innata Asabe, idan ta fad'awa Kawuna duk yanda sukayi da Kawun nawa zan sanar dakai ranar da ya tsayar."



Sun dad'e suna hira, inda daga k'arshe sukayi sallama ya tafi gida cike da zumud'i da farin cikin Fatima ta amince dashi.



Bayan Fatima ta fad'awa Asabe yanda sukayi da Mu'az, sai Asabe ta sanar da mijinta, shi kuma ya saka ranar da iyayen Mu'az d'in zasu zo domin saka rana, Fatima ta kira shi Mu'az d'in a waya ta fad'a mishi, shi kuma ya sanar da iyayenshi, iyayenshi sukayi farin ciki, domin sun dad'e dama suna so ya sake yin wani auren, sunyi murna sosai kuma sukai fatan Allah ya kaimu lokacin.



An tsaida ranar biki nan da wata biyar masu zuwa, kafin nan an gama yiwa Amarya shirye shiryenta.



*Wanene Mu'az?*

Mu'az shahararren mai kud'i ne wanda ake damawa dashi kuma ake ji dashi a garin, yana da kyau, kuma ba laifi yana da hali da d'abi'u, shekarunshi talatin da biyar, yanada mata d'aya sedai basu tab'a haihuwa da itaba ko sau d'aya, matarshi irin matan nan ne masu juya mazajensu, to sai muyi fatan Allah yasa wannan auren ma da Fatima zatai ya d'ore.

                    Page 45 to 50




Hausawa sukace rana bata k'arya sedai uwar d'iya taji kunya, sannan suka sake cewa komai nisan jifa dole dai k'asa zai fad'o.



To gashi yau Asabar Allah ya kawomu yau ne aka d'aura auren Fatima da mijinta Mu'az kan sadaki dubu d'ari, bayan an d'aura ba dad'ewa Mu'az yazo da motoci saboda zumud'i har dashi akazo tafiya da matar tashi, ya kasa hak'urin jira akai mishi ita.



Fatima kuwa zaune take a cikin d'akin Asabe, mijin Asaben yana yi mata nasiha, yace "to Fatima na yarda dake da kuma dukkanin d'abi'unki, nasan ba ason ranki bane kike aure ki fito, k'addara ce kawai ta ubangiji, wanda dama duk mai imani yasan da zuwanta a kowane irin lokaci, dan haka ki k'ara hak'uri akan wanda kike dashi, sannan kiyi k'ok'ari ki rik'e mijinki tsakani da Allah, ke yarinya ce k'arama banaso tun kina yarinyarki aita irga miki aure, kiyi k'ok'ari ki kyautatawa mijinki, duk rintsi karki sab'a mishi, kuma kinsan yanzu aljannarki tana k'ark'ashin k'afarsa, sai kin kyautata mishi zaki samu damar shiganta, dan haka karki sab'a mishi in abinda ya saki be sab'a umurnin Allah ba, in kika sab'a mishi ko kika gujewa shimfid'arsa Allah zaiyi fushi dake, sannan mala'ikun Allah zasu tsine miki, dan haka kiyi k'ok'ari ki yiwa mijinki biyayya, Allah yai miki albarka."



Tanimu ya juya ya kalli Asabe yace "in da abinda zaki k'ara akan abinda nace to kiy sauri ki fad'i motoci sunzo za'a tafi da ita."


Asabe tace "ai abinda zan fad'a ka fad'a, taiwa kanta fad'a wallahi ta zauna a d'akin mijinta shine mutuncin kowace 'ya mace, kar taja aita irga mata aure, duk yanda kake k'aunar gidanku gidan aurenka ya fishi, dan dama dole wata rana ka barshi sanadin zuwan dalilai biyu, ko aure ko mutuwa, dan haka aje aita hak'uri muma shi mukeyi, da dad'i da babu dad'i dai muna zaune a ciki."




Ba tare da b'ata lokaci ba aka fito da ita bayan an gama yi mata nasiha, aka shiga da ita cikin motar da tafi kowacce had'uwa, sauran mutanen da zasu rakata gidan mijinta suka shisshiga cikin motar, lokaci guda aka tayar da motocin suka dauki hanya sai gidan Mu'az, k'erarren gida na gani na fad'a, wanda zamu iya mishi lak'abi da aljannar duniya.



Suna shiga suka tarar da Uwar gida Amina da ita da tawagarta suna jiran zuwan Amarya suga kalarta.



Kai tsaye wajen Uwargidan suka nufo da ita, a wulak'ance suka kallesu, tawagar Amarya suka gaishesu sannan d'aya daga cikin Aminan Asabe tace "to Uwargida aita hak'uri da juna, ga Amanar Amarya nan mun damk'a a hannunki, yarinya ce k'arama har yanzu duk abinda kikaga tayi ba daidai ba to ki tsawatar mata sannan kuma............................"



Amina ta dakatar da ita ta hanyar katseta tare da d'aga mata hannu tace "dakata da Allah malama,  indai amana ce kuka kawo min to ban karb'a ba, wannan balagaggiyar yarinyar ce za'a cewa yarinya, ai duk wanda yai aure ya wuce da a kirashi da yaro, dan haka ban karb'i amanar taku ba kuma bazan karb'a ba, sai a tashi a k'ara gaba."



Wata k'awar Asaben ce tace "ikon Allah, nuna kishi k'arara, Allah ya baki hak'uri mu ba rigima ta kawomu cikin gidan ba."



Amina ta kalli tawagarta suka kalli juna suka yi wata uwar shewa, Amina ta kallesu tace "to in baku sani ba ku sani da ban yarda ba babu abinda zai kawo wata yarinya da sunan aure cikin gidan nan, duk abinda Mu'az zaiyi sai da izinina, idan banaso ko menene ya barshi kenan har abada, a yau nace ya saketa wallahi ta saku kuma babu mai sashi ya maida ita har abada, akan me zan nuna kishin abinda ni nasa a aurota, ai ni aka auro mawa tai mini bauta ba komai ba, dan haka ku kiyayeni wallahi."



Jikin Fatima da na 'yan rakiyarta ne yai mugun yin sanyi da jin furucin bakin Uwargida da tawagarta, hakanan suka k'arasa da ita inda yake mallakinta, sannan suka k'ara tunatar da ita akan tayi hak'uri, ta bi mijinta sau da k'afa kuma taiwa abokiyar zamanta biyayya, Amina kuwa suna ganin an shigo da Amarya sai suka dawo Falo, suka cigaba da yada magana son ransu, 'yan rakiyar Amarya basu dad'e ba suka baro gidan saboda yar musu da habaici da 'yan uwan Uwargida suke ta yi.




Bayan tafiyarsu ne wata k'awar Aminan ke cewa "gaskiya k'awata kinyi shirme da kika bari wata ta samu damar shigo miki gida," Amina tai dariya tace "kedai gwara da na barshi yai auren nan in ba haka ba ai sai iyayenshi da 'yan uwanshi su cinyeni d'anya." 



K'awar Amina tace "nifa mamaki kike bani wallahi, ke da kike da ragamar zuciyar Mu'az a hannunki sai inda kika karkatata to akanme wasu dangin miji zasu dameki," Amina tace "hum ba zaki gane ba, amma k'iyayyarsu akwai ciwo."



Da daddare bayan ango ya dawo kai tsaye d'akin Uwargida ya fara wucewa, ganinta yayi a zaune tana tunanin maganganun da sukayi da aminiyarta da kuma shawarwarin da ta bata.



Mu'az yai sallama ya shiga ya zauna kusa da ita, sannan ya kalleta ya sakar mata murmushi, itama mayar mishi tayi, sannan tace "ka dawo?"



Mu'az yace "eh na dawo, amma nifa bana jin dad'in yanda nake ganinki a haka kamar kinsa wannan auren a ranki bayan sai da amincewarki na yishi."



Amina ta tab'e baki, sannan tace "nifa aurenka baya gabana, nasan irin k'aunar da kake min, kuma ni na baka damar yin wannan auren saboda in samu zaman lafiya ni da 'yan uwanka, dan haka karkai wani tunani, nidai burina kawai ka nuna mata matsayina a wajenka karta kuskura tace zata rainani.



Murmushin jin dad'i yayi, sannan yace "indai dan wannan ne angama gimbiyata," ledojin dake hannunshi guda biyu ya mik'a mata guda d'aya, d'ayar kuma yace "bari inje in kai mata in dawo, ki fito falo zamu fito yanzu muma akwai maganar da nakeso nayi daku."



Amina tace "to a fito lafiya," fita yayi ya nufi d'akin Fatima inda ya tarar da ita zaune tana jiran zuwanshi, zama yayi kusa da ita yana murmushi, yace "Amaryata ta kaina, hannunta ya rik'e, sannan yace taso muje falo akwai maganar da nakeso inyi daku dukkaninku."



Fatima tace "to," a falo suka tarar da Amina, tun kafin su k'araso wajen Mu'az yai saurin sakin hannun Fatima kar Amina ta gani ranta ya b'aci.



Fatima ta zauna a kujera two sita inda shi kuma Mu'az yaje kusa da Amina ya zauna, sannan yai gyaran murya ya fara magana.



"Abinda yasa na taraku anan shine ke Fatima wannan matatace ya nuna Amina harda rik'e mata hannu, ba dan bana k'aunarta ba na auroki, a'a zaune muke da ita lafiya, kuma babu wacce nakeso kamarta,  macece me son zaman lafiya, sanadinta ma na aureki dan da bata amince ba sedai mu hak'ura da juna, dan haka ki gode mata sanadinta ne muke tare, kuma ki bata girmanta, da kuma mutunta ta, Amina ga Fatima nan, banda shakku a kanki tunda na dad'e tare dake nasan daga gareki zaku zauna lafiya insha Allah."



Amina cikin yatsina tace "hum Allah ya zaunar damu lafiya," Mu'az yace "ameen," ganin canji a fuskar Aminan ne yasa duk ya rud'e, kallon Fatima yayi yace taje d'aki gashinan tahowa.



Bayan Fatima ta shiga d'aki Mu'az ya d'an rungumo Amina a jikinshi yace "haba abar k'auna, me zaisa ki damu wai bayan kinsan babu yarinyar da zan bawa soyayyar da nake miki wai?"



Amina ta shagwab'e a jikinshi harda su kukan drama, tace "taya bazan damu ba bayan zaka je ka kwana da wata bayan ada ni kad'ai ce mallakinka, ai dole inji wani abu," Mu'az dama yasan za'a rina, dama yasan abinda ke damunta kenan.



Kallonta yayi yace "in dan wannan ne karki tashi hankalinki akai, sai inyi kwanciyata a d'akinki, kwanan d'akinta ma sai kin amince sai da yardarki, tashi muje d'akinki mu kwanta, shikenan dai ko?"



Murmushi Amina tayi cike da jin dad'in yanda mijinta yake fifitata fiye da kowa hatta 'yan'uwanshi, hannu ya mik'a mata ta kama suka shige cikin d'akinta suka yi kwanciyarsu.



Fatima tana zaune tana jiran zuwan ango domin a wannan karon ta d'auki alwashin zata cire duk wani tsoro ta kyautatawa mijinta iya iyawarta, saboda batason tayi abinda Allah zaiyi fushi da ita, kuma mala'iku su tsine mata, wannan k'udurin ne a ranta tana zane tana jiran shigowar ango amma shiru kakeji.



Sak'o ta gani an turo mata a waya, tana dubawa taga Mu'az ne, tana bud'ewa taga yasa wai ta kwanta shi zai kwana d'akin uwargida, amma ta bud'e ledar da ya kawo taci abinda ke ciki kafin zuwa gobe, daga k'arshe yai mata saida safe harda wani ta kula mishi da kanta.



A tak'aice dai a d'akin uwar gidanshi ya kwana.


 _MY MEERA ta kalleni ni 🅱K tace "lallai my bk wannan Mu'az d'in ya cika sunanshi mijin tace, sai nace mata ai my meera wannan ya wuce mijin tace sedai mijin kafin tace😂."_



Haka Fatima ta kwana da fargabar hali irin na wannan mata, tana kuma tunanin anya zata barsu su zauna lafiya ita da mijinta kuwa, da wannan tunanin tai bacci a ranta.



Washe gari da wuri Fatima ta tashi tai wanka tai fes da ita, tasha ado da kwalliya tai kyau sosai, sannan ta shiga kicin domin shirya musu abun karyawa, bayan ta gama tazo ta jera akan dinin d'in dake falon.



Komawa d'akinta tayi tare da nata abin karyawan taje taci ta k'oshi, Fatima ta sawa kanta dangana, da kuma juriyar duk abinda zai faru da ita, shiyasa ma ta sakawa ranta hak'uri, kuma ta dena sa komai a ranta, duk da taji ciwon abinda ya faru jiya amma sai ta danne ta maidashi kamar komai be faru ba.



Tun tashin su Mu'az da Amina sallar asuba, sai wajen goma sannan suka tashi suka shiga sukai wanka a tare suka fito suka zab'arwa juna kaya, sannan suka fito falo, sunsha mamakin ganin an shirya dining da abun karyawa, k'arasawa sukai suka zauna suka shiga bawa ciki hak'k'insa, sunayi suna ciyar da junansu.

                    Page 50 to 55



Saida Fatima tai kwana uku a cikin gidan nan ba tare da Mu'az ya kwana a d'akinta ba, tsakaninshi da ita sai in zai bata wani abu ko kuma idan yana nemanta sai ya kirata a waya ko yai mata test.



Yau Lahadi, yau ne kwanakin Amarya ya k'are a cewar Amina, a yau ne kuma ta bashi dama ya shiga d'akin matarshi amma sedai ya raba musu kwanaki bibbiyunsu tunda ranar amarcinta ya wuce.



Hakan kuwa akayi, dan duk abinda Amina tace to Mu'az baya musu akai.



Da daddare, suna zaune a falo shi da Aminarsa sai ya d'auki wayarshi ya kira Fatima, bata d'auki lokaci ba ta fito, ta samu waje ta zauna.



Mu'az ya kalli Fatima yace "abinda yasa na kiraki shine yau za'a raba kwana, kowacce daga cikinku zan rik'a yi mata kwana bibbiyu," Fatima tace "to," sannan ta tashi ta koma d'aki.



Duk da kasancewarta sabuwa kuma bak'uwa a harkar aure amma tasan abinda sukeyi mata sam babu adalci a cikinsa, amma ita nata idone duk wanda yai nagari dan kansa.



Har wajen sha d'aya yana d'akin Amina, Amina ta kalleshi tace "ya kamata ka tashi kaje ku kwanta, Mu'az ya kalleta yace "sai kin yimin alk'awari idan na tafi ba zaki shiga damuwa ba?"



Amina tai dariya sannan tace "nifa na fad'a maka in zaka ajiye mata uku bayan ni bazai dameni ba, muddin baka canza matsayin da ka bani ba, inada tabbas akan cewa na kere kowa a zuciyarka, dan haka ka tashi kaje kawai."



Mu'az ya rungumeta tare da yi mata sallama sannan ya fice daga d'akin.



Fatima har ta fara bacci taji hannu yana tab'ata, idanunta ta bud'e a hankali, ta ga Mu'az a kanta yana mata murmushi, itama mayar masa tayi da murmushin, nan ya fara mata wasanni daga nan kuma suka zarce.



Fatima tasha kuka kamar me, jikinta duk ya b'aci da jini, Mu'az kuwa banda rarrashi babu abinda yakeyi, nan ya d'auketa ya kaita bayi yai mata wanka ya dawo da ita ya cire zanin gadon ya saka wani sannan ya kwantar da ita akan gadon.



Rungumeta yayi yana cigaba da rarrashi, yana matuk'ar k'aunar yarinyar a ranshi, musamman yanzu da ya jita sabuwa kar a leda, mamaki ne cike a ranshi, saboda shi a iya saninshi kuma a yanda aka fad'a mishi ance ta tab'a yin aure har biyu, to amma abun mamakin shine a budurwa da ya sameta, yaso mata tambaya, amma sai ya daure ya bari sai gobe saboda a yanzu kuka takeyi.



Haka yaita shafa mata baya har ya samu tai bacci, shi kuwa ya dad'e kafin yai bacci saboda mamaki da kuma tarin tambayoyin dake cikin ranshi, ga wani irin k'aunar yarinyar daya k'ara shiga ranshi, sedai duk k'aunar da yake mata bazai iya nunawa a gaban Amina ba saboda baya son b'acin ranta.



To Amina dai abinda takewa gudu ya faru, Mu'az dai ya kusanci matarsa Fatima, kuma ya kwana da farin cikin hakan.



Washe gari da asubar fari yai wanka ya shirya sannan ya tashi Fatima wadda jikinta ya gashe da zafi ya had'a mata ruwan wanka ta shiga tayi, sannan ta fito.



Bayan ta fito ta shirya tai sallah, sannan ta koma ta kwanta, can anjima sai gashi ya dawo daga masallaci shima, kicin ya shiga ya had'o mata tea me kauri ya bata tasha, sannan ya b'allo mata magani ya bata tasha ta koma ta kwanta.



Nan da nan bacci ya sake d'auketa, Mu'az ya tashi yaje cikin sauri ya shiga kicin ya had'a musu abun karyawa me sauk'i, yana yi yana lek'owa kar Amina ta fito ta kamashi a kicin yana yiwa Fatima aiki, doya ya soya musu da kwai sannan ya soya Arish, sai ruwan zafi wanda dama akwai a cikin plas wanda zasu had'a tea dashi.



Cikin sauri yazo ya jajjera a dining kamar yanda Fatiman ta saba yi, sannan yaje ya k'ara yin wanka, ya sake sabon shiri lokacin wajen k'arfe biyar da rabi ne, ya fito ya nufi d'akin Amina saboda yasan halinta tsab zata iya tayar mishi da hankali idan ya dad'e a d'akin Fatima be fito da wuri ba.



Koda ya shiga ya isketa tana bacci, sai yaje ta gabanta ya kwanta shima tare da rungumeta, idanu ta bud'e a hankali, sai ya sakar mata murmushi, itama ta mayar mishi, tare da sake shigewa jikinshi suka cigaba da baccinsu a haka.



Wajen k'arfe takwas Fatima ta tashi, koda ta tashi bata ga Mu'az ba, nan da nan zuciyarta ta bata yana can wajen Amina, fitowa falo tayi taga wayam ba kowa, sai ta koma cikin d'aki abinta tai kwanciyarta, saboda har yanzu jikinta na ciwo bata jin dad'insa.



Sai wajen goma suka tashi, tare suka shiga wanka suka fito, bayan sun shirya suka fito falo domin karyawa, kai tsaye dining d'in suka wuce suka zauna.



Mu'az ne ya zuzzuba musu, suka ci abinsu, suka k'oshi, komawa cikin falon sukai suka zauna suna hira, sai wayar Amina ta fara k'ara a cikin d'akin baccinta, ta tashi domin taje taga waye yake kiranta.



Cikin sauri Mu'az yaje wajen dining d'in ya had'a abun bud'a bakin ya shiga ya kaiwa Fatima kawai ganinshi tayi ya shigo da sauri yana waige kamar b'arawo ya ajiye mata abun karin ya fita da sauri.



Yana fitowa ya koma kujera ya zauna, wayarshi ya ciro ya tura mata sak'o wai ta tashi ta ci abinci idan ta gama magani yananan a gefen gado ta d'auka tasha, yana gama tura mata sai ga Aminan ta dawo falon ta zauna kusa dashi suka cigaba da hirarsu.



Bayan sun gama shan hirarsu kuma suka sake sabon shiri, suka fice daga gidan suka tafi anguwa.



Bayan fitarsu ba dad'ewa kamar daga sama Fatima taji ana sallama, koda ta fito taga wata dattijuwa ce wadda a k'alla shekarunta zasu kai hamsin da biyar.



Fatima tace "sannu da zuwa Mama, ga waje nan ki zauna, bari in kawo miki abun sha, tsohuwar nan tana cewa a'a amma sai da Fatima taje ta d'ebo mata lemo na kwali da ruwa ta kawo mata, sannan tazo ta ajiye gabanta ta zuba mata.



Bayan tasha sannan suka gaisa, bayan sun gaisane wannan tsohuwar ta kalli Fatima tace "ni mak'wabciyarku ce, ga gidana nan a kusa daku, naji an kawo amarya ne shine naga yanada kyau in shigo domin in muku nasiha saboda hakkin mak'wabtaka."



Nan tsohuwar nan taita yiwa Fatima nasiha akan su zauna lafiya, tare da bata shawarwari masu kyau.



Fatima taji dad'i sosai da maganganun tsohuwar nan, kuma lokaci guda taji tana k'aunar tsohuwar, saboda dattijuwar k'warai ce, domin sai d'an k'warai ne yake baka shawarar k'warai.



Fatima tai mata godiya sosai, sannan tace insha Allah itama zata shigo taga gidan nata domin su rik'a gaisawa.



Tai mata sallama sannan ta tashi ta tafi.



Tun daga wannan ranar Fatima ta samu abokiyar hira da kuma abokiyar shawara, takan shiga jefi jefi, itama Maman tana shigowa wajenta.



Shak'uwa ce ta shiga tsakaninsu, tana jinta kamar mahaifiyarta, itama Maman jin Fatima take kamar 'yar da ta haifa.



Bayan Mu'az ya keb'e da matarsa Fatima ne yake tambayarta ya akai ya sameta a matsayin budurwa, duk da tambayar ta bata kunya amma dole ta daure ta bashi amsar cewa ba wani abu da ya tab'a shiga tsakaninta da mazajen da ta aura a baya.



Tun daga lokacin da Amina tai rabon kwana in ya shiga d'akin matarshi Fatima ranar girkinta duk da sai can dare yake shiga wajen Fatiman, lokacin da Amina taso ya tafi bayan ya gama yi mata hira, amma hakan baya hanashi kusantar matarshi duk ranar girkinta.



Ita kuma Fatima dama ta d'auki alwashin ba zata tab'a sab'a mishi ba insha Allah, hatta ita Aminan duk garin Allah in ya waye sai taje ta gaisheta.



*Bayan wata biyar*

Watan Fatima biyar a gidan Mu'az, da dad'i ba dad'i tana zaune a haka, ba wanda ta tab'a kaiwa k'arar halinda take ciki.



Yau Fatima ta tashi da wani irin matsanancin ciwon ciki gabad'aya bata jin dad'in jikinta, gashi ta kasa cin komai, duk abinda tai yunk'urin kaiwa baki sai taji amai ya taso.



Gashi yunwa takeji, hakan yasa ta fito domin zuwa kicin ta samu abinda zata ci, tana fitowa ta tarar dasu a falo suna karyawa, nufar wajensu tayi domin ta gaishesu, sedai tana zuwa daf dasu taji amai ya taso mata har ta fara yunk'urin yi, da gudu ta nufi d'akinta domin taje tayi.



Dukkansu binta sukayi da kallo, sannan suka dawo da kallonsu kan junansu, Amina tace "dama matarka batada lafiya amma baka fad'amin ba?"



Mu'az yace "wallahi ban san batada lafiya ba nima," Amina tace "to ya kamata ka kira likitanmu mana ya dubata."



Ba dan komai yasa Amina tace a kira likita ya dubata ba sai dan ta tabbatar da abinda zuciyarta ke zargi.



Mu'az yace "yanda kikace hakan za'ayi gimbiyata, wayarshi ya d'auka ya lalibo lambar likitan dake dubasu ya kirashi, inda likitan ya tabbatar mishi da gashinan tahowa.



Koda likitan yazo har Amina aka bishi har cikin d'akin Fatiman, kuma yau ne ranar farko da ta fara shiga d'akin Fatiman.



Bayan likita ya gaggama gwadata ya d'ago ya kalli Mu'az yace "yallab'ai congratulations, matarka tana da ciki na tsawon wata biyu."



Nan da nan kamannin Amina suka canza idanunta suka sauya saboda tsabar kishi, Mu'az kuwa ana gama fad'in Fatima tanada ciki, Aminan yai sauri ya kalla yaga yanayin da take ciki.



Tabbas Mu'az yana cikin farin ciki wai shine zai samu d'a nashi na kanshi, amma babu damar nuna farin cikin nashi a fili.



Amina ta fice daga cikin d'akin fuuuuu, har Mu'az ya juya zai bita ya tuna yabar likita a ciki, hakan yasa ya dawo d'akin suka fito tare da likitan, saida ya sallami likitan ya tafi sannan ya nufi d'akin Aminan.






_Tatab'urza kenan😂😂😂😂😂_







Muje zuwa

                    Page 55 to 60




Mu'az yana dawowa daga wajen raka likita kai tsaye d'akin Amina ya wuce, inda ya sameta tana hawayen drama.



Kusa da ita yaje ya zauna, ya kamo hannunta zai fara aikin rarrashi, cikin sauri ta fad'a jikinshi tare da sake fashewa da kuka, cikin kukan tace "shikenan ni nasan matarka na haihuwa zaka juyamin baya ka daina k'aunata."



Shi kuwa Mu'az ba abinda yakeyi banda aikin rarrashi, yace "haba Amina meyasa zaki fad'i haka bayan kinsan cewa duk duniya babu wadda nake k'auna sama dake? koda ace Fatima yara d'ari zata haifamin wallahi ba zai tab'a sawa matsayinki ya canza daga gareni ba, babu macen da zanso sama dake, dan haka babu sanadin da zaisa in canza mikima balle har in daina k'aunarki, babu wannan sanadin a cikin duniyar nan."



Haka dai yaita amfani da dad'ad'an kalamai wajen k'ok'arin kwantar mata da hankali, a ranar ma duk dan ya faranta mata be sake komawa d'akin Fatiman ba, kuma a d'akin Aminan ya kwana duk da kasancewar ranar kwanan Fatiman ne.



Tun daga wannan ranar Amina ita da k'awarta sukaita fafutukar neman hanyar zubewar cikin nan, amma ina, duk abinda sukeyi kwata kwata be zube ba yananan daram dam, saima k'ara girma yake.



Dole hakanan suka hak'ura domin ba irin abinda basu yi ba dan suga ya zube amma be zube ba, hakan yasa dolensu suka hak'ura ba dan rai yaso ba.



Tun daga lokacin da Fatima ta samu ciki har zuwa yanzu da cikin yake da wata bakwai kullum Amina cikin fitina take da kuma yiwa Mu'az daru, shiyasa ya kasa sakat gabad'aya ba wata kulawar kirki da Fatima ta samu daga gareshi, Amina ce abun tattalinshi da kuma lallab'awa.



Wannan dattijuwar ce take shigowa akai akai tana bata kulawar da ta dace.



_Kamar yanda na fad'a muku ne idan masu karatu basu manta ba, Amina ta riga da ta gama da Mu'az, ta rabashi da kowa nashi duk da kasancewar tarin 'yan'uwan da yake dasu amma saida ta rabashi da kowa, shiyasa tunda Fatima ta shigo gidan tunda suka zo tana amarya wani nashi be k'ara takowa yazo ba, saboda gudun fuskantar wulak'anci, saboda duk abinda Amina zataiwa 'yan'uwan Mu'az baya iya cewa komai, sedai ma ya tayata a ci zarafinsu, itama kanta Fatiman tausaya mata sukeyi saboda sanin halin Amina._



Yau litinin, Mu'az ne shi da Amina suka fito daga cikin d'akin Aminan da sassafe cikin shirinsu na fita unguwa, hannunsu rik'a da babbar jaka ta kaya, ga dukkan alamu hakan yana nuna tafiya zasuyi.



Bayan fitowarsu falo, hannun Amina yana sak'ale a jikin na Mu'az, kallon k'ofar d'akin Fatima yayi, ganin haka yasa Amina ta jashi tace "Durling ba lokaci fa, ka taho mu tafi kar mu rasa jirgi, idan mun fita a hanya sai ka tura mata da sak'o a waya ka fad'a mata mun tafi," tana jan hannunshi take duk wad'annan maganganun, Mu'az ya kalleta yace "duk abinda kikace haka za'ayi masoyiyata, suna fitowa cikin sauri suka fad'a mota driver ya jasu sai filin jirgi, a cikin motar ne ya turawa da Fatima sak'on cewa sun tafi makka sai sun dawo ta kula mishi da Babynshi, bayan sun isa filin jirgi suna tashi driver ya juyo da motar zuwa gida inda su kuma suka lula zuwa k'asa me tsarki.



Tun biyar saura suka bar gidan, bayan fitarsu da awa d'aya Fatima ta tashi, yau ta makara sallar Asuba, ita da duk rintsi bata wasa da sallarta, cikin sauri ta tashi tayi sallah, sannan tayi azkar, bayan ta kammala ta tashi ta shiga kicin domin shirya abun karyawa domin dama Amina ta fad'a a gabanta a gaban kowa cewa tabar ganin ta samu ciki to wallahi ba abinda zata d'auke mata.



Bayan ta gama shirya abun karyawa sai ta jera musu nasu, duk da yanzu basu cika cin abincinta ba idan tayi tunda ta samu ciki, yanda ta ajiyeshi haka zata dawo ta d'aukeshi, fita sukeyi suje waje su cika cikinsu su dawo, amma duk da hakan Fatima bata fasa ajiyewa ba kullum a cewarta hakkinsu ne, kuma har yanzu su basu ce mata ta daina zuba musu ba shiyasa bata fasa ba.



Wucewa tayi da nata cikin d'aki ta zauna taci tasha tai k'at, sannan ta gyaggyara ko ina yai tsab dashi, wanka ta shiga tayi ta cancad'a kwalliyarta me shegen kyau, ta zauna cikin d'akinta ta jawo wayarta tana dubawa anan ne taci karo da sak'on Mu'az.



Bayan ta gama karantawa taji zuciyarta na wani irin suya, kasa jurewa tayi ta fashe da kuka, tunda take gidan nan abubuwan da ake mata a gidan be tab'a k'ona ranta ba irin na yau, ace Mu'az yana matsayin mijinta amma be damu da yasan halinda take ciki ba, duk da kasancewar yanayin da take ciki a yanzu na lalurar ciki yau lafiya gobe ciwo, kuma wai dan ya tabbatar mata da bata gabanshi wai har ce mata yake ta kula mishi da Babynshi kad'ai banda ita.



Kuka take sosai domin ranta yai mugun b'aci, tana cikin wannan yanayin taji sallamar Mama, sauri tayi ta goge hawayen dake fuskarta, dan bataso kowa ya fahimci halin da take ciki.



Fitowa tayi, inda tayi k'ok'arin tattaro walwalarta dan kar Mama ta gane komai, zama sukayi a falo suka gaisa da Mama, bayan sun gama gaisawa Mama ta kalleta tace "d'iyata meke damunki ne naga kinyi kuka meyafaru?"



Fatima ta sake k'ak'aro murmushi tace "bakomai Mama, ba kuka nayi ba, me kika gani?"



Mama ta girgiza kai, sannan tace "daga lokacin da na sanki zuwa yanzu Fatima ba sai kin bud'e baki kin fad'i cewa ana zaluntarki ba za'a gane haka, ki cigaba da yin hak'uri Fatima, Allah yana sane dake zai tallafi rayuwarki, duk tsananin halinda kike ciki Allah yana sane dake, kuma zai kawo miki d'auki wata rana da yardarsa, dan haka har kullum maganin damuwar nan taki shine cigaba da hak'uri da kuma addu'a, in kika rik'e wad'annan abubuwan guda biyu a rayuwarki zakiji dad'i domin sune matakin nasara na rayuwa."



Fatima ta goge hawayen da yaketa kai kawo akan fuskarta, sannan tace "hakane Mama, nagode da irin gudummawar da kike bani na shawarwari nagartattu, Allah ya saka miki da alkhairi, har abada bazan tab'a mantawa dake ba."



Nan dai Mama ta cigaba da bata hak'uri tare da shawarwari, tana nunar mata da cewa ta cigaba da hak'uri ta cire damuwa a ranta, tai addu'a Allah ya rabata da abinda ke cikinta lafiya shine kawai babban fatanta a yanzu.



Sun dad'e suna hira har Fatima ta rok'i Maman da ta dawo nan gidan da zama, domin ita kad'aice a cikinshi yanzu, masu gidan sunyi tafiya kuma bata san ranar dawowarsu ba.



Mama bata amince ba, tadai yi mata alk'awari kan cewa kullum zata rik'a shigowa tana duba halinda take ciki.



Bayan wata d'aya da tafiyarsu ya kama cikin Fatima watanshi takwas kenan,  jikin Fatima ya rikice sai rashin lafiya take ta yi, amma dai yau abun yafi tsananta, dan koda Mama ta shigo cikin gidan yau a sume ta tarar da ita.



Hakan yasa ta fita domin taje taiwa drivern gidan magana yazo ya kai Fatima asibiti, anan ne me gadi ke shaida mata ai tun ranar da su Alhaji wato Mu'az zasui tafiya a ranar shima Hajiya Amina tace ya tafi gida sai sun dawo sai ya dawo.



Mama bata tsaya ta k'arasa jin zancenshi ba ta fita ta tafi bakin ti-ti ta tsayar da taxi suka shigo har cikin gidan aka fito da Fatima suka tafi asibiti da ita.



Koda suka isa ganin halinda take cikine yasa cikin gaggawa aka wuce da ita emergency, likitoci suka rufa a kanta.



Mama sai kai kawo take tana yi mata addu'ar samun sauk'i



Bayan wasu lokuta likita ya fito da sauri ya nufo Mama, yace "kece kika kawo wannan mara lafiyan ko?" Mama tace "eh nice," likita yace "to tana cikin mawuyacin hali, muna buk'atar jini wanda zaiyi daidai da nata, kuma muna buk'ata da gaggawa domin a k'ara mata."



Mama tace "to likita yanzu ya za'ayi, ba za'a iya samun wanda zaiyi dai-dai da nata ba a wajenku?"



Likita yace "za'a iya samu mana, wata 'yar takarda ya ciro yai rubutu akai, sannan ya mik'a mata yace "gashi kije can wajen,  ya kwatanta mata da hannu, in kinje ki basu wannan takardar ta hannunki, zasu baki irin nata sai ki biyasu ki karb'o yanzu ki kawo mana, ki hanzarta ba lokaci tana cikin mawuyacin hali."



Mama tace "to," ta juya cikin sauri ta nufi inda likita ya nuna mata.



Cikin k'ank'anin lokaci aka shiga k'arawa Fatima jini, a tak'aice saida tai sati biyu a asibitin nan sannan aka sallamesu suka dawo gida, kuma a lokacin cikinta yana da wata takwas da sati biyu.



Tun daga lokacin Mama ta dawo gidan Fatima domin cigaba da kula da ita.



Bayan cikar wata tara da 'yan kwanaki Fatima ta fara nak'uda, inda suka sake tafiya asibiti cikin gaggawa, domin dama likita ya sanar musu cewa in ta fara nak'uda ayi gaggawar dawo da ita asibiti ta haihu anan kar a barta a gida.



Hakan yasa Mama bata tsaya yin k'ok'arin karb'ar haihuwar ta-ta ba suka tafi asibiti.



A asibitin ne Allah ya sauketa lafiya ta haifi d'anta namiji, kyakkyawan yaro kamar d'an indiyawa.



Saida suka kwana washe gari aka sallamosu, Fatima tai waya ta sanarwa Asabe ta haihu, nan da nan ba b'ata lokaci sai ga Asabe ta taho, tun daga lokacin su biyu suke sintiri akan Fatiman da Mama da Asabe, sedai Asabe da dare take komawa tunda ga Mama tana kwana tare da ita, da sassafe take sake dawowa.



Fatima ta fad'awa Asabe irin d'awainiyar da Mama takeyi da ita, Asabe taiwa Mama godiya sosai, Mama tace bakomai ai, Fatima yarinyar kirki ce, kuma d'a na gari na kowa ne.



A d'an zuwan da Asabe take anan ta fahimci komai, inda kuma wasu ta tambayi Fatiman cikin hikima ta fad'a mata, musamman yanda taga anata kai kawo ba uban d'a ba kishiyarta, hakan yasa ta tausayawa Fatiman kuma ta jinjina hak'uri irin nata, dan tasan ita da take tsohuwa ma ta manyanta ba zatai hak'urin da Fatima tayi ba.



Ranar suna tazo gashi babu uban yaro, hakan yasa aka rangad'awa yaro sunan mijin Asabe wato Tanimu, inda suke ce mishi Ameer.



Tanimu yaji dad'i matuk'a inda ya zage yaiwa d'iyarshi Fatima kayan suna da gara me uban yawa, 'yan uwan Mu'az ma sunzo kuma sunyi bajinta domin sun gwangwaje Fatima da kayan suna kala kala, inda suka nuna murnarsu na ganin jinin d'an'uwansu Mu'az.




_Fatima muna yi miki barka, Allah ya raya Ameer ya albarkaci rayuwarsa a duk inda yake, Allah ya amintar dashi a cikin bayinsa na k'warai._

                   Page 60 to 65




Bayan kwana arba'in da haihuwar Ameer kwatsam ranar alhamis sai ga matafiya sun dawo.



Lokacin Mama ta gama yiwa Ameer wanka kenan, a yayin da Fatima ke zaune a kusa dasu, Mama tanata yi mata tsiya akan ta tsaya ta koyi wankan yara dan in batanan ko kuma dan nan gaba in ta sake haihuwa, suna cikin maganar ne sai sukaji sallamar su Mu'az.



Turus sukaja suka tsaya a bakin k'ofa suna mamakin ganin yaro da sukayi, sai yanzu ma suka tuna da sun bar wata baiwar Allah d'auke da ciki a jikinta.



Mama tace "sannunku da zuwa matafiya, ku k'araso ciki mana kuka tsaya daga bakin k'ofa."



Mu'az kuwa kasa b'oye murnarshi da d'umbin farin cikin dake ranshi yayi, ya k'arasa wajen yaron da sauri, lokacin Mama ta ciroshi daga cikin ruwa ta goge mishi jiki da tawul, ganin ya tunkarosu gadan gadan yana son yaga d'anshi yasa Mama ta nannad'eshi da wani tawul d'in daban ta mik'a mishi.



Cikin farin ciki da zumud'i ya karb'eshi, sannan ya kalli Fatima yace "yanzu Fatima kin haihu shine ko labari babu?"



Fatima tace "uhm sannunku da zuwa ya hanya?"



Mu'az yace "lafiyalau Alhamdulillah, nan ya gaishe da Mama sannan ya cigaba da tambayoyin rainin wayau, Mama yaushe ta haihu ne, wane suna kuka sa mishi?"



Mama tace "sunanshi Tanimu ana kiranshi da Ameer, saboda sunan kawun Fatima ne, kuma  yaune sukayi arba'in ma da haihuwar yaron, bayan jinya da tasha kashi kashi a asibiti."



Kallon Fatiman yayi yanaso yace wani abu sai karaf idanunshi suka kai kan Amina wadda ta rik'e k'ugu tana kallon ikon Allah, a tarihin rayuwarsu dashi yaune karo na farko da wani abu ya d'aukewa Mu'az hankali daga kanta.



Mu'az cikin inda-inda yace "Amina kinga ashe Fatima ta haihu, karb'eshi ki gani."



Tunkararta yayi da yaron, sedai tun kafin ya k'arasa wajen ta wuce d'akinta fuuuuuu cike da k'unar zuciya.



Cikin sauri ya juyo ya mik'awa Mama yaron yace "Mama gashi ina zuwa," Mama ta karb'i yaron, shi kuma ya juya yabi matarshi Amina cikin d'akinta da sauri.



Mama da Fatima kuwa binshi kawai sukai da kallo, suna mamakin irin mugun rawar jikin da yake nunawa akan Amina.



Mama tace "ikon Allah, to Allah ya sawak'a ya kawo maka rangwame Mu'az," Mama ta sawa yaron kaya ta mik'awa Fatima shi, sannan ta k'arasa 'yan abubuwan da zata k'arasa mata, taiwa Fatiman sallama ta tafi gida.



Itama Fatiman tashi tayi rik'e da d'anta ta shige cikin d'aki abinta ta barwa masoya falon su sakata su wala, duk da tasan ko a gabanta ne duk abinda sukayi niyya ba fasawa zasuyi ba.



Mu'az yana shiga cikin d'akin Amina ya tarar da ita rik'e da k'ugu sai kai kawo take kamar zata kumbure saboda tsabar b'acin rai.



A tsaye take sai faman karkad'a k'afa take, Mu'az ya k'arasa kusa da ita yace "Amina kinga dan Allah ki saurare.............................."



Cikin sauri Amina ta katseshi ta hanyar d'aga mishi hannu, tace "dakata malam, me zaka fad'amin? kaga kawai ka fitamin daga d'aki."



Mu'az ya rik'o hannunta da niyyar yai mata magana ta fizge hannun, sannan ta kalleshi tace "tun ba'aje ko'ina ba ka fara maidani banza a idanun yarinyar nan saboda ta haifa maka yaro ni kuma bana haihuwa ko, to ka fita kaje wajenta ka cigaba da zama, ka fita naceeeee."



Mu'az ya tsorata matuk'a da ganin yanda Amina ta rikid'e ta zama kamar wata kububuwa saboda tsabar masifa.



Juyawa yayi ya fita jikinshi a sanyaye, saboda yasan duk abinda zai fad'awa Amina a yanzu ba zata saurareshi ba tunda tayi fushi dashi sosai.



Shima kuma sanadin Aminan tana fushi dashi ya shiga fushi da kowa, kowa haushinsa yakeji, ko wajen aiki baya fita, koda yaushe cikin magiya yake, sam baya k'aunar b'acin ran Amina, kuma baya iya musawa umurninta.



Saida yai sati d'aya a falo yake kwana domin itama Fatima fushi yake da ita, a cewarsa duk ta dalilinta Amina ke jin haushinsa, shiyasa shima yake jin haushin Fatiman, kuma duk da yanada d'akinshi amma ya gwammace ya kwana a falo ko Amina zataji tausayinshi ta yafe mishi su daidaita, domin a cewarshi abinda yai mata be kyauta ba, kuma ya cancanci tai mishi fiye da hakan ma.



Tsawon sati d'aya da kwana biyu ya d'auka tana fushi dashi, daga bayane ta huce tare da sharad'in bazai sake yi mata makamancin abinda yai mata ba, kuma babu shi babu shiga d'akin Fatiman,  Mu'az yace duk yaji kuma ya yarda.



Watan Ameer shida a duniya.


A haka aka cigaba da irin wannan zama na zalunci, babu abinda ke had'a Fatima da Mu'az, sai d'ad'd'aikun rana ne ma suke gaisawa idan ta taki sa'a ta tarar dashi a d'akin Aminan in taje gaisheta ko in ta tarar dasu a falo.



Duk da irin wannan yanayin zaman da sukeyi a cikin ran Mu'az yana matuk'ar jin kewar d'anshi Ameer, yanaso yaga yana d'aukarshi yana yi mishi wasa, amma ba hali, saboda yana tsoron shiga d'akin Fatiman Amina ta kamashi.



Sai ya tsiro da in Amina tai bacci sai ya lallab'o ya fito yazo yaga yaron ya koma, hakama da safe yana rigata tashi duk dan yazo yaga yaron nashi ya koma.



Kullum haka yakeyi, k'aunar yaronshi tana k'ara shiga cikin ranshi.



Akwai wata ranar Alhamis da sassafe Mu'az ya tashi, kukan Ameer ne ya tayar dashi, inda ita kuwa Amina baccinta taketa yi, hannunta na sak'ale a jikinshi.



K'ok'arin cire hannun nata yakeyi a hankali, domin yaje yaji menene yaronshi ke kuka.



Cikin sa'a ya cire hannun nashi ya tashi ya fito cikin sand'a kar ya tada Amina ya rasa damar zuwa ganin yaron.



Sedai abinda be saniba shine cire hannun Amina da yayi daga jikinshi yasa ta farka, saida taji alamar ya sauka daga kan gadon sannan ta bud'e idanunta, da taga zai waiwayo sai tai saurin sake rufe idanun nata har ya fita daga d'akin ba tare da ta bari ya fahimci idanunta biyu ba.



Kai tsaye d'akin Fatima ya wuce inda Amina ta biyo bayanshi tana lek'e har saida taga inda ya shiga, bayan shigarshi d'akin itama Aminan ta nufi d'akin da sauri ta lab'e a bakin k'ofa inda taji Mu'az d'in yana tambayar Fatiman me yakewa kuka.



Fatiman ta shaida mishi cewa kawai rigima yakeji, karb'arshi yayi daga hannun Fatiman, ya sab'ashi a kafad'a yana jijjigashi yana kai kawo yana fad'in "yi shiru d'an Baba, yi shiru d'an Abbanshi."



Duk abinda ke faruwa Amina tana lab'e tanaji.



Cikin ikon Allah kuwa kamar yasan mahaifinsa yana fara jijjigashi yai shiru, mik'a mata shi yayi tare da k'ok'arin juyowa zai fito daga d'akin.



Amina na ganin haka da gudun arzik'i tabar k'ofar d'akin ta koma d'akinta.



Wajen sau uku kenan Amina na kamashi yana satar fita ganin d'anshi duk besan ta sani ba, kuma bema san ta ganshi ba.



Amina kuwa ganin haka yasa ta fara tunani in tayi wasa fa uwar yaron nan da yaron kanshi zasu k'wace mata miji daga hannunta, hakan yasa ta fara neman mafita domin kawo k'arshen zaman Fatima a cikin gidan ta kowane hali.



Tun daga wannan lokacin Amina take k'ulla makircin da ya kamata taga tayi domin raba Fatima da gidan gabad'aya, sedai duk wanda ta k'ulla sai taga beyi mata ba sai ta warwareshi.



Hakan yasa ta cewa Mu'az zataje gidan Aminiyarta, yace mata to suje mana daganan sai ya ajiyeta ya wuce wajen aiki.



Hakan kuwa akayi, tare suka shirya suka fice abinsu, babu maganar yiwa Fatima sallama dan dama bata sanin shigowarsu da kuma fitarsu.



Bayan ya sauketa ya wuce wajen aiki, inda ita kuma ta shige gidan.



Tun daga bakin k'ofa take k'walawa matar gidan kira, cikin sauri ta fito tana cewa "haba Amina wannan kiran haka fa, banza kawai salon ki tayarmin da hankali."



Fatima tai dariya, tace "ai gwara ya tashu, kema ai sanin kankine idan hankalina be kwanta ba to wallahi kema naki bazai kwanta k'alau ba."



K'awar Fatima me suna Salame tace "topha kice ba lafiya ba kenan, to mu shiga daga ciki mu zauna sai ki koramin bayani zanfi samun natsuwar fahimta."



Can cikin d'akin baccinta suka shige harda rufe k'ofa, nan Amina tahau korawa Salame bayanin dukkanin abubuwan dake faruwa.



Salame tai shiru tana tunanin mafita, bayan wani dogon lokaci tana nazari can sai tace "akwai mafita me sauk'i ma, kawo kunnenki kiji."



Nan Amina ta matsa Salame ta rad'a mata abinda zata rad'a mata, sukayi wata irin shewa, sannan Amina tace "to yaushe kike ganin ya dace in aiwatar da shirin namu?"



Salame tace "gobe goben nan mana, yo me za'a jira kuma," suka sake yin shewa suka tafa.



Anan ta yini sai da yamma Mu'az ya biyo ta gidan ya d'auketa suka koma gida tare.



Washe gari kyakkyawan shiri Amina taiwa Mu'az na fita wajen aiki, sannan aka bishi da kyawawan lafuza masu kwantar da zuciya.



Tattalin da take mishi had'e da muguwar kissa ta hauka ta fito da ita a ranar, shi kuwa sab'o tumakin sai washare hak'ora yake yana murna ana ji dashi.



Ya kalleta yace "yanda kike tattalina haka sai naji kamar ma kar inje wajen aikin," Amina ta langab'ar dakai tare da shagwab'ewa a jikinsa, sannan tace "nima bana so ka tafi ka barni, dan Allah masoyina karka dad'e ka dawo gida da wuri, ina buk'atar kasancewa dakai a kusa dani kullum, amma na yau yafi na kullum damuna."



Mu'az yace "insha Allah bazan dad'e ba zan dawo da wuri," nan sukayi sallama ya tafi wajen aiki.



Wajen k'arfe hud'u Amina ta fara shirin tuggunta, hargitsa kayanta tayi, saida ta jiyo k'arar motar Mu'az alamun ya dawo, sai tayi sauri ta shafa robb a wajen idanunta, nan da nan ta fara hawaye, ta d'auko k'atuwar jakarta ta zuba kaya, ta d'ora akan gado tare da bud'eta, sannan ta k'ak'aro kuka ta farayi, ta fito da kayan sip d'inta ta fara jerawa a cikin jakar, tana yi tana kuka me k'aran gaske, babu wanda zai gani yai tunanin ba wani abu akai mata ba.



Da zumud'insa ya shigo yana k'wala mata kira yanda kasan ita kad'aice mata a wajenshi, shiru ba amsa, kai tsaye d'akin ya shiga, nan da nan fara'ar fuskarshi ta d'auke ganin halinda take ciki, ya fara jero mata tambayoyi, "Amina meke faruwa ne, ina zakije kike had'a kaya, me akai miki,  Fad'amin mana?"



Bata fasa had'a kayan ba  cikin kukan tace "yau a gidanmu zan kwana ka biyoni da takardata, in kuma ba haka ba wallahi sedai ka zab'a ko ni ko Fatima a gidan nan."



Mu'az ya rikice tare da kamo hannunta ya rik'e gam yana fad'in "haba Amina me kike fad'i haka, wa yace miki zan iya rayuwa in babu ke? fad'amin me yafaru bayan na fita?"



Amina cikin kuka kamar ranta zai fita tace "wai naje zan d'auki yaron nan Fatima ta hanani d'aukarshi, kuma tanata fad'amin maganganu harda gori, wai ni juya ce, harda wai in naji haushin maganarta in haifo nawa, sannan harda cewa wai ko yanzu ka fad'i ka mutu itace keda dukkanin dukiyarta, ni timinin takaba kad'ai zan samu, shine ni kuma jin ta fad'i haka a lokacin kuka ya kub'ucemin nace mata ashe dama dukiyarka take mawa ba kai takeso ba, shine tacemin wai eh ai dukiya abun so ce, wa ke fatan ya mutu a cikin talauci, kawai muna zamanmu lafiya kaje ka d'auko wadda bata k'aunar ganinka a raye, fatanta kawai ka mutu taci gado."



Mu'az nan da nan ranshi ya b'aci fiye da na da, idanunshi suka rine, jijiyoyin jikinshi suka fito rud'u-rud'u, an tab'ashi tunda an tab'a mishi matar so.



Share mata hawayen fuskarta yayi, sannan ya jawo hannunta suka fito falo suka tsaya a tsakiyar falon, k'walawa Fatima kira ya fara yi, "Fatima! Fatima!! Fatima!!!"



Cikin sauri ta fito d'auke da yaronta Ameer a hannunta, yaro d'an wata shida, Ameer yai sauri yasa hannu ya k'wace yaron daga hannunta ya mik'awa Amina, sannan ya sake juyowa ya kalli Fatiman yace "Fatima na sakeki saki uku, kuma na karb'i yarona daga hannunki, ki tattara komai naki ki ficemin daga gida."



Fatima cikin kuka ta durk'usa har k'asa a gabanshi tace "dan girman Allah ka barni na tafi da yarona, nai maka alk'awari idan na yayeshi zan dawo maka dashi."



Tureta yayi ta fad'i can gefe ya tsallaketa ya fice daga gidan abinshi, ashe mota yaje nemowa ta kwashe kayanta, Amina na ganin fitarshi ta kalli Fatiman ta shek'e da dariya, sannan tace "kad'an daga cikin tuggun mace kenan Fatima, na ma yi namijin k'ok'ari da na bari aka samu wannan shegen yaron, sai a kiyayi auren mijin wasu, ta juya ta kalli yaron dake hannunta tace kai kuma dan uwarka sai na zama ajalinka, dan yanda na tsani uwarka haka na tsaneka a rayuwata," tana gama fad'in haka ta dangwarar da yaron a nan falon tai shigewarta cikin d'akinta, cikin sauri Fatima ta rarrafa ta d'aukeshi tana rarrashinsa, domin tunda aka karb'eshi daga hannun Fatiman aka mik'awa Amina yaketa faman kuka.






Muje zuwa

                   Page 65 to 70




D'aukar yaronta Ameer tayi cikin sauri ta mak'alk'aleshi a jikinta tana kuka, ba wani dad'ewa sai ga Mu'az ya shigo cikin falon.



Tsayawa yayi yana huci yana kallon Fatiman ganin ta sake d'aukar mishi yaro, sai ya shiga k'walawa Amina kira, Amina ta fito cikin sauri.



Mu'az ya kalleta yace "ba ke na bawa yaron nan ba, meyasa kika sake bata?"



Amina tace "zuwa tayi zatai dambe dani ta k'waceshi daga hannuna shine ni kuma na sakar mata shi."



Mu'az cikin fushi ya nufo Fatima ya watsa mata mari, sannan ya sake k'wace yaron daga hannunta, ya sake mik'awa Amina shi.



Tasa Fatima yayi a gaba dole sai ta fitar mishi daga gida, saida ya fitar da ita har bakin gate sannan ya dawo, inda yasa yara suka fara fito da kayayyakinta suna sawa a cikin motar da yaje ya nemo domin a kwashe kayan nata.



Fatima ta dad'e a bakin k'ofar gidan nan tana kuka, ba komai take kokamawa ba sai yaronta da bata san irin rayuwar da zaiyi ba, gashi yaro k'arami dan zalunci an rabata dashi.



Tashi tayi ta nufi hanyar gidansu, koda isarta har motar kayan ta rigata isowa.



Bayan Mu'az ya koma gida ya tarar yaron nan Ameer ba abinda yake sai aikin kuka, Amina ta tayar mishi da daru akan itafa ba zata iya rik'e wannan yaron ba, yak'i yarda da ita sai kuka yakeyi, Mu'az yace mata karta damu bari yai wanka zai d'aukeshi ya kaishi gidan Mamanshi sisternshi ta cigaba da kula dashi idan ya girma sai ya dawo gabansu, amma dai ba haka yaso ba, yaso ace yaronshi yana gabanshi saboda yana k'aunar yaron sosai.



Fatima tana shiga cikin gida ta tarar da Asabe da Tanimu a tsaye suna tattauna bak'on al'amarin da sukaci karo dashi yau d'innan, wato suna tattaunawa akan maganarta.



A gabansu ta durk'usa k'asa tana kuka kamar ranta zai fita, Asabe cikin nuna tausayawarta sab'anin da, taje kusa da ita ta kamo hannunta suka shiga d'akin Asaben, Tanimu yabi bayansu, domin yanaso yaji musabbabin wannan tozarcin, domin ga shedar mari nan ya fito a fuskar Fatiman sun gani da idanunsu.



Rarrashinta sukayi har suka samu tai shiru, sannan Tanimu ya kalleta yace "Fatima menene ya faru?"



Fatima cikin kuka tace "wallahi hakanan kawu inama cikin d'akine kawai naji Mu'az ya k'walamin kira, ya karb'e Ameer daga hannuna sannan yace in fita mishi daga gida ya sakeni saki uku, wallahi ba abinda nai mishi kawu."



Asabe tace "haba ba sai kin rantse ba Fatima, tunda ni shaida ce naga irin zaman da kike a cikin gidan nan, ga kuma shaidar mari nan a fuskarki, Allah zai saka miki, amma kema me zaisa kiyta kuka dan kin rabu da zama a cikin wannan gidan da babu shugaba a cikinsa sai mace."



Fatima cikin kuka tace "Inna Ameer nake tausaya mawa, a gabana fa da aka mik'a mata shi wurgar dashi kan kujera tayi tai shigewarta d'aki, wai yanda bata k'aunata shima bata k'aunarshi."



Asabe tace "to menene na damuwar idanma ya zama sanadin kashe d'anshi na cikinshi ai nashi ne, dan haka inma zaki cire damuwa ki cire, mu kece tamu ke mukafi damuwa dake, Ameer d'anshi ne, in yaso yaje yai ganda da namanshi, mugun mutum kawai banda zalunci d'an watanni k'alilan ne za'a rabashi da mamanshi, ai shikenan su da Allah."



Nan dai Asabe da Tanimu sukaita bata hak'uri tare da tausar zuciyarta.



Wannan karon Asabe da kanta ta gyara mata d'aki,  aka shirya mata kayanta a ciki yai kyau yai ras dashi.



Asabe ta canza sosai kamar ba itaba, saboda tana tausayin Fatima yanzu sosai, idan ka gansu a tare yanzu zakasha mamaki matuk'a.



Haka Fatima taita jinya, sanadin damuwa da tunanin yaronta da tunanin halinda yake ciki ya sakata har saida ta kamu da ciwon zuciya saboda yawan damuwa da kuka da tunani da ta saka a ranta.



Ba itace hankalinta ya kwanta ba sai da Asabe tasa akai mata bincike aka tabbatar mata da cewa yaron yana hannun kakarsa wato maman Mu'az d'in, sannan ta kwantar da hankalinta.



Bayan tayi idda jefi jefi takan je gidan Mama acan ne ta sake tabbatar da cewar Ameer baya tare da su Mu'az.



Tanimu ne ya yanke shawarar zai maida Fatima makaranta, ko ba komai zata k'ara ilimi, sannan karatun zai d'ebe mata kewa tare da rage mata lokacin zaman tunani.



Asabe tai na'am sosai da shawarar nan ta mijinta, inda aka kira Fatima domin jin ra'ayinta itama ba tare da b'ata lokaci ba ta amince, kuma ta nuna farin cikinta k'arara a fili.



*Bayan shekaru uku*

Fatima tanada farin jinin masoya sosai, yanda kasan jira suke ta fito su fara yin layi a k'ofar gida, sedai wannan karon bata sauraron kowa bata ma basu fuska amma duk da haka basu fasa zuwa ba, sannan su Asabe basu matsa mata ba a yanzu, sunadai tayata addu'a Allah ya zab'ar mata nagari.



Yau lahadi, Fatima ce tafe akan hanya tana ta sauri domin taga ta isa islamiyyarsu da wuri kar tayi latti, saboda yau ta kusan makara.



Tana zuwa bakin ti-ti nan ma abun hawa zasu yi mata tsiya sai wucewa suke da gudu gata matsoraciya ta kasa samun damar tsallakawa.



Wata had'ad'd'iyar mota ce me shegen kyau da tsada tazo ta wuce, har motar ta wuce sai ta tsaya, sai kuma ta fara dawowa da baya da baya.



Gaban Fatima motar taja ta tsaya, hakan yasa Fatima ta matsa daga wajen, ta sake komawa gefe.



Wani mutumi ne had'ad'd'en matashi ya fito daga cikin motar kai tsaye ya doshi inda Fatima take tsaye, turarenshi me matuk'ar dad'i shi ya fara yi mata maraba, magana yayi cikin taushin murya me cike da d'an karen dad'in sauraro, yace "Assalamu alaikum."



Saida kunnuwan Fatima suka motsa saboda tsabar dad'in sautin muryar wannan bawan Allahn.



Amsa mishi sallamarshi tayi tare da d'ago kai ta kalleshi, sedai cikin sauri ta maida kanta k'asa sakamakon baiwar kyau da ta gani a tare dashi, ga kuma zuciyarya da taji ta fara bugawa da ta d'aga kai ta kallesh, bata sake kallonshi ba tunda ta kalleshi sau d'aya ta duk'ar da kai, sannan sai ta k'ara matsawa bakin ti-tin tana k'ok'arin tsallakawa, dama a tunaninta zaiyi kwana ne sai ta matsa ta bashi hanya ya wuce, yanzu kuma da ta lura sanadinta ya tsaya shiyasa zata tafi abinta.



Shima matsawa yayi kusa da ita a karo na biyu, sannan ya kalleta yace "malama barka da war haka, dan Allah ki taimaka ki bani lambar wayarki ko kuma Adreshin gidanku."



Fatima shiru tayi batace mishi uffan ba, ganin ti-tin motoci sun ragu, kuma zata iya tsallakawa, kawai sai gani yayi ta tsallaka abinta tai tafiyarta ta barshi nan tsaye yana kallonta.



Abinda tai mishi maimakon ya b'ata ranshi a'a, saima wata irin k'aunarta da ya k'ara ji, a zuciyarshi yana tabbatar da cewa lallai wannan yarinyar yana jin yak'inin cewa tabbas ta k'warai ce, kuma me nagartacciyar tarbiyya.



Sawa k'ofofin motarshi key yayi, sannan ya tsallaka shima ya bita da sauri.



Da gudu ya k'arasa har inda take, suka jera suna tafiya a tare, kallonta yayi ya sake cewa "kinsan Allah muddin baki saurareni ba to duk inda kika saka k'afarki nima anan zan saka tawa koda kuwa gidanku kika shiga sai na biki, kuma idan har nai ciwon k'afa wajen bin bayanki to sai nakai k'ararki kotu an bimin hakkina.



Duk da maganarshi tayi abun dariya, amma k'in nuna hakan tayi akan fuskarta, k'ara murtukewa ma tayi, ba tare da ta kalleshi ba, tace "dan Allah malam kayi hak'uri ka k'yaleni ka kama gabanka."



Wannan mutumin yace "in k'yaleki fa kikace, to idan na k'yaleki inyi yaya da zuciyata da take son binki duk inda zakije?"



Fatima tai tsaki, sannan tace "haka kuke, halinku guda ne, haka ma kalaman bakinku, ku nunawa mace k'auna a waje, a cikin gida kuma taga sab'anin haka."



Wucewarta tayi ta shige cikin makarantarsu, domin dama ba nisa sosai.



Tsayawa yayi yana nazarin maganarta, to me take nufi da hakan, tabbas ba mamaki wani mugun ya tab'a yaudarar yarinyar nan, in kuwa hakane zansha bak'ar wahala kafin in samu zuciyarta duk a cikin zuciyarshi yake wannan nazarin.



Da wannan tunanin ya shiga cikin makarantar, kai tsaye office d'in shugaban makarantar aka nuna mishi ya shiga da sallamarshi.



Bayan an mishi izinin shiga ya zauna suka gaisa da malamin, sannan ya fara kora mishi bayani, "Malam dama wata d'alibarku na gani a hanya a bakin ti-ti d'azu, har ga Allah na yaba da hankali da kuma natsuwarta, sannan har a raina ina jin k'aunarta, sedai tak'i saurarona kwata-kwata, shine nazo ko za'a taimaka a fad'amin adreshin unguwarsu domin inje in nemi izini wajen magabatanta?"



Malamin nan yai shiru, sannan can sai yace "eh to kayi tunani me kyau, dama a k'a'ida na shari'ar musulunci iyaye sunada hakki sosai wajen neman izininsu kafin a kula yaransu, hakan ne zai basu damar sanin dawa 'yarsu take tare, kuma neman izinin nan yana d'aya daga cikin abinda ke sawa mace ta gane da gaske namiji ke sonta ko yaudara ce kawai, maganar gaskiya yawancin maza idan mace tace musu suxo su nemi izini wajen iyayenta to har abada ba zata sake ganinsu ba, saboda su sunzo ne da wata manufa bawai manufar aure ba, kaga neman izini kyakkyawar kariya ce ga 'ya'ya mata wadda suke sakaci da ita a yanzu, bayan haka naji dad'i sosai da Allah yasa kai da kanka kayi tunanin ka nemi magabatanta kuyi magana dasu kafin ka fara tsayawa da ita, ba mamaki ma wannan  dalilin ne yasa tak'i tsayawa ta saurareka, dan har yanzu akwai yara masu kima da mutunci wad'anda basa yarda su tsaya da namiji a ti-ti suyi zance koda sun tsaida su, har yanzu akwai irin matan nan sedai basu da yawane a cikin al'umma, dan haka a cikin d'alibanmu d'alibai biyu ne kad'ai na tabbatar da cewa basa kula kowa, hatta a cikin malamai akwai masu sonsu amma basa kulasu, d'aya har gidansu wani malaminsu yasa mukaje nema mishi aurenta amma bata bashi damar hakan ba saboda tayi aure a baya ta cutu da yawa, d'ayar kuma tunda mijinta ya rasu ne ta kasa yin wani auren,  bari insa a kirasu sai ka gani ko akwaita a cikinsu.



Ta farko da tazo ya ganta yace ba ita bace, ta biyu kuwa tun daga sallamarta ya fara murmusawa, tana shigowa sukayi ido biyu da wannan mutumin a office d'in, gabanta taji yana wani irin fad'uwa, inda ta tsinci kanta a wani irin yanayi, sai a yanzu ta k'are mishi kallo, kuma taji wani bak'on yanayi ya ziyartota sakamakon kallonshi da tayi, tabbas shine wanda ya tareta a hanya d'azu, me ya kawoshi nan kuma, zuciyarya keta sak'a mata wannan tunanin.



Malamin nan ya kalli mutumin yace "wannan ce kenan?"



Murmushi ya sakeyi, sannan yace "itace malam, wallahi itace."



Fatima ta fara magana, k'walla har ta tarun mata a idanu tace "wallahi malam ba abinda nai mishi, tareni yayi a hanya nak'i tsayawa, shine yazo kawo k'arata wajenka."



Malam yace "kinga ba sai kin rantse ba nasan halinki Fatima, nasan cewa ba abinda zakiywa babba wanda be kamata ba indai kece saboda na yadda da d'abi'unki, jeki aji abinki kinji."



Fita tayi daga office d'in tana goge hawayen fuskarta, sedai har ta koma aji ta zauna gabanta fad'uwa kawai yake.



Nan malamin ya rubutawa wannan mutumin Adreshin gidan su Fatima ya mik'a mishi, sannan yace "amma ina mai baka shawara akan in har kasan da mugun nufi zaka tunkari yarinyar nan to tun wuri gwara ka hak'ura, domin wallahi ina mai tabbatar maka cewa har abada ba zaka tab'a samun had'in kanta ba."



Mutumin nan yace "wallahi Allah malam da kyakkyawar zuciya zanje gareta, kuma da manufar aure zan tunkareta, banda mugun nufi kwata-kwata a kanta."



Nan dai yaiwa malam godiya sannan sukayi sallama, ya fito ya koma bakin ti-ti can inda ya ajiye motarshi, ya shiga ya tayar da ita ya tafi abinsa cike da farin ciki.







_Anya kuwa a yanda Fatima ta tsani maza suka gundureta anya🤔 to amma dai muje page na gaba muji yanda zata kasance, nidai gani nake kamar akwai tatab'urza a yanzu musamman yanda ta samu d'aurin baya a wajen Asabe😂._







Muje zuwa

*MASOYANA NA GODE SOSAI DA ADDU'AR SAMUN WARAKA*👏😍🥰





               Page 70 to 75




Washe gari cikin zumud'i wannan kyakkyawan matashin saurayin yaci kwalliya yasha ado da shiga ta alfarma kuma ta kece raini, ya fito ya fad'a cikin motarshi ya d'auki hanyar gidansu Fatima cike da nishad'i da kuma farin cikin idanunshi zasu sake arba da wannan kyakkyawar fuskar da a yini d'aya ta hana zuciyarshi samun natsuwa.



Gudu yake a cikin motar, amma gani yake kamar baya sauri, duk ya k'osa ya isa wajen sarauniyarshi, domin ya samarwa da zuciyarshi natsuwar da ta kasa samu tun had'uwarsu ta jiya har zuwa yanzu.



Cikin k'ank'anin lokaci ya k'araso k'ofar gidan, saboda gudun da yasha a hanya wajen ganin ya k'araso da wuri, yana isowa ya faka motar sannan ya fito yana waigen neman d'an aike.



Sedai unguwar shiru yau ba yara, ga dukkan alamu suna makaranta, hakan yasa ya yanke shawarar zuwa k'ofar gidan yai sallama da kanshi.



Kai tsaye k'ofar gidan ya dosa tare da doka sallama, daga ciki yaji an amsa, hakan yasa ya jinkirta yana jira yaga wanda zai lek'o.



Asabe ce ta amsa mishi sallamar daga inda take zaune a tsakar gida tana aiki, Fatima tahau k'walawa kira taji shiru bata amsa ba, hakan yasa ta lek'a d'akin nata taga ashe bacci take, mayafinta ta d'auka ta fita domin taga waye ke sallamar.



Tana fitowa taci arba da bak'uwar fuska, har k'asa ya durk'usa ya gaisheta, domin ya taho da shirin yin kowace irin biyayya indai za'a bashi Fatima to zaiyi koma menene.



_Dama haka Allah ke ikonsa, acikin yini d'aya sai ka had'u da soyayyar da bazata tab'a barin ranka sukuni ba har abada._



Bayan sun gama gaisawa Asabe tace "sedai bawan Allah ban shaidaka ba, wa kake nema ne?"



Sunkuyar da kai yayi, sannan ya hau sosa k'eya yace "wallahi Mama dama, am, nazo wajen Baba ne domin neman izinin fara zuwa wajen Fatima."



Asabe tai jim, tana jinjina maganar a ranta, saboda tasan Fatima yanzu babu zancen da take adawa dashi irin na saurayi, gani take duk mayaudara ne, ta kalleshi tace "to mahaifinta dai bayanan ya fita wajen aiki, sedai in kanaso ka sameshi kazo da yamma ne bayan magrib ko issha'i."



Yace "to insha Allah idan ya dawo zanzo," Mama tace "to Allah ya bada iko sai kazo," ta juya zata koma cikin gida, ganin bashida alamar tafiya yasa ta gane inda ya dosa, juyowa tayi tace "bari in turo maka Fatiman," da har ya shiga damuwar rashin ganin Fatima, amma yana jin abinda mahaifiyarta tace sai ya washe fuska da baki yace "to Mama nagode," harda d'an risinawarshi.



Asabe ta koma cikin gida itama tana murmushi saboda ganin yanda ya nuna farin ciki da zumud'i, zuciyarta ce ke cewa ga dukkan alamu yaron nan yana son Fatima sosai.



Kai tsaye d'akin Fatima ta shiga ta fara tashin Fatiman.



"Fatima, ke Fatima maza tashi kinyi bak'o a waje tun d'azu yana jiranki."



Fatima ta tashi fuska a d'aure, musamman ma da taji akan wani ne aka tasheta daga bacci.



Asabe dake tsaye a kanta tace "tashi kije kinji Fatimana, ba dukkanin mazane suka taru suka zama d'aya ba, wallahi duk inda aka samu mugaye sai kinga ka samu na k'warai, ba ma mazan kad'ai ba hatta matan ma, dan haka kije ki saurareshi koda ba zaki dad'e ba ku gaisa kawai ki dawo kinji."



Fatima ta fashe da kuka mai tsanani, ganin za'a tirsasa mata fita wajen wani wanda batama san wanene ba, mutanen da a idanunta take kallonsu azzalumai marasa adalci a rayuwa, kuma masu shegen son kansu.



Asabe tana ganin haka sai tahau salallami, tace "yanzu daga yi miki magana akan saurayi sai ya zama tashin hankali, to yi shiru daina kuka, koma ki kwanta, bari inje ince mishi kina bacci kawai.



Kwantar da ita tayi akan gadon, inda Fatiman keta faman sauke ajiyar zuciya yanda kasan yarinya.


_Nikam nace su Fatima an samu wuri a wajen Asabe harda su kuka shagwab'a ida a dane yaushe zakiy haka😂_


Asabe ta fito daga cikin d'akin tana tunani a ranta anya Fatima batai gamo da aljanu ba kuwa, k'ofar gidan ta dosa,  inda taiwa saurayin k'aryar cewa Fatiman batada lafiya, nan ya nuna damuwarshi a fili, sannan, yace ai mata sannu, ya shiga motarshi ya juya domin komawa gida jikinshi a sanyaye.



Da dare bayan Tanimu ya dawo suna zaune suna hira, can sai Asabe ta kalleshi tace "yauwa kan maganar Fatima nace nifa tunani nake anya kuwa Fatima batada aljanu mai gida?"



Tanimu ya kalleta tare da maida hankalinshi kanta yace "meyasa kika fad'i haka?"



Nan ta kwashe duk abinda ya faru d'azu ta fad'a mishi, sannan ta cigaba da cewa "ba a kanshi ma kad'ai ba, duk wanda yazo wajenta, idan na matsa mata da rarrashin kan ta fita sai ta hau yimin kuka sosai wasu lokutan har kanta yai ciwo saboda kuka."



Tanimu yace "gaskiya bana tunanin Fatima tana da Aljanu, saboda bata sakaci da azkar, duk da dai su aljanu yanzu da masu yin azkar da kiyaye dokokin Allah da kuma wad'anda basa azkar basa kiyayewa duk shiga jikinsu sukeyi, saboda su suna kishi suga ana bin dokar Allah, sai su fara k'ok'arin wahalar dakai domin basaso suga kana ibada da sauran abubuwa na addini sai suyi k'ok'arin dakatar dakai, wasu kuma ko yaya kad'an yi kuskure kayi sakaci to dama sun dad'e suna neman dama sai sui maza su shige jikinka suita wahalar dakai, amma in ka jajirce sai suyi su gama ba tare da sunyi galaba a kanka ba, amma duk da haka bana tunanin Fatima tanada aljanu gaskiya, nafi tunanin kawai tana duba wahalhalun da ta fuskanta ne a zaman aurarrakin da tayi a baya ne, abunda yafi shine mu dage da tayata da addu'a da kuma yi mata nasiha, da nunar mata da cewa  ba duka maza bane na banza akwai nagari a cikinsu."



Asabe tace "nasiha kam da addu'a muna yi kullum, sedai mu cigaba da yi," nan suka saki wannan hirar kuma suka d'auko wata daban.



Washe gari saurayin nan yazo dai-dai lokacin da akace mishi zai sami ganin Baban Fatiman.



Bayan ya fito yai mishi bayanin abinda ke tafe dashi, Tanimu ya shaida mishi cewa ta tab'a yin aure har uku, sannan ya shaida mishi abubuwan da suka faru da ita a baya, daga k'arshe yace shi daga gareshi ya bashi dama ya rik'a zuwa wajenta, idan har sun daidaita ya sami soyayyarta shikenan, akan haka sukayi sallama ya tafi yana tunanin yanda zaiyi wajen wanke bak'in fentin da maza suka shafa a idanun Fatima.



Saida saurayin nan ya shafe wajen wata guda yana sintirin zuwa k'ofar gidansu Fatima amma bata fitowa.



Duk da haka bai fasa zuwa ba, kullum sammakon fitowa yake domin yaga fitowar Fatima wajen tafiyarta makaranta, da fitowarta da dawowarta duk akan idanunshi takeyi, sedai ko ya tsayar da ita bata sauraronshi.



Duk da haka be fasa zuwa ba, dama ya shirya d'ammarar fuskantar kowane irin k'alubale daga gareta.



*WANENE WANNAN SAURAYIN?*

Sunanshi Anas, haifaffen d'an garin ne shima, sedai ba anan yai karatu ba, bayan ya kammala karatunshi ne a waje ya dawo gida domin yace yafiso yai aiki a k'asarshi.



Mahaifin Anas hamshak'in attajirine a garin, saboda tsabar kud'inshi ne yasa yai fice a gari ba wanda bai sanshi ba, ga kyauta da taimako kamar me, sam abun hannunshi be rufe mishi ido ba.




Iyayen Anas magartattun mutanene hakan yasa shima halinshi ya fita daban a cikin matasan wannan zamanin.



Yana aiki a d'aya daga cikin company d'in mahaifinsa, wanda ya mallaka mishi kyauta halak malak, wanda dama da sunanshi aka gina kuma aka bud'eshi tun yana k'arami, hatta sunan compnyn akwai sunanshi a jiki.



Tunda ya fara aiki Allah ya tallafa mishi, gashi dai matashin saurayi, amma Allah ya bud'a mishi sosai shima ya zama mashahurin mai kud'i .



Zuwa yanzu yanada shekara talatin cif a duniya, sedai abinda bamu saniba shine yanada aure ko bashida aure, bari mu jira muji a gaba.




*Cigaban labari*

Yau Asabar, yau Fatima sunada hadda a islamiyyarsu, kuma kamar yanda Anas ya haddace komai wanda ya zamar mishi farilla a rayuwa to haka ya haddace ranakun da Fatima ke zuwa makaranta, sanin hakan ne ma yasa Anas d'in yaci ado ya bugo sammako kamar kullum ya taho k'ofar makarantar tasu yana jiran yaga wucewarta.



Sedai har wad'anda take rigasu zuwa ma suka zo suka shige amma ba Fatima ba alamarta, hakan yasa yace wata k'ila yau da wuri ta shige bari ya jira yaga fitowarta tunda yau shigewarta ta wuceshi.



Bayan an kammala k'ara musu karatu sun biya anji haddar ta zauna, sai aka tashesu tare da jaddada musu akan cewa suje su cigaba da muraji'a.



A karo na biyu ma babu Fatima ba labarinta har d'alibai suka fara watsewa bata ba alamarta.



Wata k'awarta ce wadda kullum yake ganinsu tare idan an tashi tazo zata wuce ta gaban motarshi, hakan yasa yai saurin fitowa ya tari gabanta da sauri yana cewa "Assalamu alaikum, 'yan mata dan Allah k'awarki Fatima fa, naga yau ban ganta ba a makarantar?"



K'awar Fatima tace "wallahi malam yanzu muka gama waya da ita wai kanta ne ke ciwo sosai shiyasa bata sami damar zuwa ba."



Anas yace "to dan Allah ki taimaka min da lambarta mana in kira nima in  mata ya jiki."



Tace "ba damuwa, kawo wayar in sa maka," "no kawo taki dai in d'auka ba sai kin wahalar da kanki ba," amsar da Anas ya bata kenan.



Abinda yasa ya dage sedai ta bashi ya d'auka da kanshi saboda karta bashi wrong number ko kuma tai mistake d'in wata lambar.



Bayan ta lalibo lambar Fatiman sai ta mik'a mishi wayar, Anas ya karb'a ya ciro tashi wayar ya d'auki lambar, saida ya d'auki tsawon minti biyar yana sake kallon lambar gudunshi kar sai ta tafi yazo yayi mistake, saida ya tabbatar dai-dai ne sannan ya bata wayarta, yai mata godiya ta tafi shi kuma ya shiga mota ya zauna, sannan ya  kira lambar wayar da aka bashi.




Saida yai kira wajen goma amma ba'a d'aga ba, hakan yasa ya rubuta sak'o ya tura mata, ga sak'on kamar haka:


_"""""karki manta dani nafi kowa damuwa dake, a kanki ba abinda zan kasa, indai na furta miki labarin dake cikin zuciyata nasan zaki gasgatani in kin kalli idanuwana, ina rok'onki domin girman Allah ki saurari zuciyata kiji sak'on dake cikinta, ina miki fatan samun lafiya, har abada inasonki ANAS NE nataccen saurayinki, kuma maye a fagen k'aunarki, taimaka ki turo da amsa koda kalmar zagice......."_



Duk kiran dake shigowa akan idanun Fatima suke shigowa, kuma saboda samarin dake mugun damunta da yawan kira yasa ta daina d'aukar kowace irin lamba indai bak'uwa ce.



Can sai ta sake jin k'arar message ya shigo, d'aukar wayar tayi, wadda take a gefenta, ta bud'e sak'on tare da karantawa, saida ta maimaita karanta sak'on sau wajen biyar saboda ya tafi da ita, can sai ta tuno cewa yanzu fa duk wad'annan kalaman duk yaudara ne, ba gaskiya bane, sai tayi tsaki, tare da danno delete har zata share, ko me ta tuna kuma sai ta fasa.



Bayan Anas ya gama tura mata sak'on yaja motarshi yai gaba.



Haka Anas ya cigaba da sintirin zuwa wajen Fatima, duk hanyar da yasan zai ganta ita yake bi, bashi gidansu bashi sintirin sammakon zuwa makarantarsu, idan yaga shigewarta tofa bazai tafi ba har sai an tashi yaga wucewarta.



_Allah kenan mai dasa so a zuciyar bayi kuma cikin k'ank'anin lokacinda ma basuyi tunani ko tsammani ba, shin ko Fatima zata sassauto tsanarta ga maza, shin anya kuwa Anas zai samu nasarar samun zuciyar Fatima a yanayin da take ciki a yanzu?????🤔._







Muje zuwa
