               Page 75 to 80




Tun daga wannan lokacinda Anas ya samu lambar Fatima yake tura mata da sak'wanni kala-kala na soyayya, kuma duk samarin da suka dameta da kira saka lambarsu take a black list, amma shi ta rasa meyasa ta kasa sakashi, koda ta sakashi ma da hannunta take komawa ta cire.



Sak'onnin Anas su suka zama abokan nishad'inta, tana matuk'ar jin dad'in bitar karantasu.



Amma ita duk wad'annan alamomin ko kad'an bata fahimci wai ta fad'a so kenan ba, kuma duk da haka har yanzu Anas ya kasa shawo kanta ta saurareshi koda sau d'aya ne.



Yau talata. sunada karatu a islamiyyarsu, hakan yasa ta zama cikin shiri tana jiran k'arasowar lokaci.



Lokaci na k'arasa cika bayan tayi sallah taiwa Asabe sallama ta fita domin tafiya makaranta.



Tazo dai-dai inda Anas ke tsayawa da motarshi, hakanan kawai taji zuciyarta nason kallon inda yake, tana juyawa domin ta saci kallon wajen taga wayam ba kowa, hakan yasa tahau dube dube, amma bashi ba alamarshi, haka kawai kuma sai ta tsinci zuciyarta da jin ba dad'i.



K'arasawa tayi ta shige cikin makarantar, ta shiga aji ta zauna, sedai a ranar bata fahimtar komai sosai, saboda hankalinta ya tafi kan tunanin abinda ya hana mayen k'aunarta (sunan da ya sawa kanshi kenan) zuwa wajenta.



Hakanan ta dawo gida duk a salub'e kamar mara lafiya, bayan ta dawo dayake wayarta a gida take barinta bata zuwa makaranta da ita, bayan ta dawo ne ta d'auko wayar ko zataci karo da sak'onshi amma abun mamaki yau ba ko d'aya, nan da nan damuwarta sai ta k'aru, tana tunani a cikin ranta anya yana lafiya kuwa.



Sai ta danno lambarshi da niyyar kiranshi sai wani sashe na zuciyarta ya hanata aiwatar da hakan, k'arshe dai ajiye wayar tayima taje tayo alwala tana jira lokaci ya k'arasa tayi sallar magrib.



Tun daga wannan ranar kullum Allah Allah take ta shirya ta tafi makaranta ko Allah zaisa ta ganshi amma ina.



Wayarta kuwa kullum cikin gadinta take, tana zaman jiran sak'onshi, ko makaranta ma yanzu da wayar take zuwa.



Gabad'aya ta rasa meke damunta, tama rasa dalilin da yasa ta damu dashi haka, kuma duk iya k'ok'arinta na ganin ta yakiceshi daga zuciyarta ta kasa, to dama wa ya isa yaja da abinda ya riga da ya shiga cikin zuciyar bawa.



Haka kawai ta tsinci kanta da fara yin danasanin abubuwan da tai mishi, a tunaninta saboda abinda tai mishi ne Allah ya jarabceta da tunaninshi a koda yaushe.



Asabe ce ta shigo d'aki ta tarar da ita tana aikin nata wato tunani, zama tayi kusa da ita, sannan ta dafata tace "Fatima wai meke damunki kwana biyun nan ne, naga duk yanayinki ya canza, meke faruwa ne?"



Fatima tai murmushi, sannan tace "bakomai kawai bana jin dad'in jikina ne kwana biyun nan."



Asabe tace "subhanallah, shine baki fad'a mana ba aje a dubaki, gaskiya ke kam zurfin cikin nan naki yai yawa, meke damunki?"



Fatima tace "bafa wani ciwo bane sosai, ciwon kai ne, sai zazzab'i, kuma naji sauk'i ma."


Asabe tace "sannu Allah ya sauwak'e, to ko zamuje asibiti ne a dubaki?"



Fatima tace "a'a da sauk'i fa, idan nasha magani shikenan ma insha Allah," Asabe ta tashi taje d'akinta ta d'auko magani da ruwa ta tasa Fatima a gaba saida ta shanye tas, sannan tai mata sannu tace ta d'an kwanta ta samu bacci, sai ta fita ta barta tai baccin, itama Fatima ta kwanta domin bin umurni bawai dan zata iya yin baccin ba.



*BAYAN SATI D'AYA*

Sai da Anas ya d'auki tsawon sati guda baya zuwa wajen Fatima.



Yau juma'a, Asabe da Fatima ne a tsakar gida suna aiki, sai yaro ya doka sallama, dukkansu suka amsa, yace "wai ana sallama da Fatima."



Abin mamaki cikin sauri Fatima ta mik'e tare da fad'in "kaje kace ina zuwa," cikin d'aki ta fad'a ta d'auko hijabinta ta saka, sannan ta fito, ta cewa Asabe "Inna sai na dawo bari in lek'a inga ko waye," bata jira taji me zatace ba ta fita da sauri, inda tabar Asabe nan sake da baki da hanci tana matuk'ar mamakin sauyawar yarinyar nan.



Koda Fatima ta fito daga k'ofar gida ta tsaya, saboda tanaso kafin ta k'arasa sai ta tabbatar cewa wanda take tsammanin zuwanshi gareta ne.



Be san da fitowarta ba saboda ya juya baya, yana jingine a jikin wata k'erarriyar mota me d'an karen kyau da tsada, ga d'aukar hankali.



Har da ta juya zata koma ciki, wata zuciyar tace kiyi sallama tukun ki bari ya juyo tukunna ki gani idan ba shi bane sai kiy tafiyarki.



Sallama ta doka mishi, cikin sauri ya waiwayo tare da amsawa.



Wayyo farin ciki wanda take tsammani kuwa shine tsaye a gabanta.



Anas ne tsaye yasha ado kamar sabon ango, sedai ya d'an rame, sai kuma ya k'ara kyau, dogon hancin nan nashi ya k'ara fitowa tare da k'ara k'awata  kyawun fuskarshi,  fuskarshi d'auke da murmushi, itama Fatima tsintar kanta tayi da mayar mishi da martanin murmushinsa.



Anas ya k'ura mata idanu k'yar kamar yau ya fara ganinta, ba shiri ta sunkuyar da kanta k'asa tana cigaba da murmushi.



Anas ya cigaba da kallonta yana murmushi, yace "Fatima, ki d'ago kanki mana ki cigaba da kallona, kina mamakin sake ganina ko, to ba abun mamaki bane, domin k'aunar da nake miki ba ta wasa bace, nasan a tunaninki kwana biyu da bakya ganina kinsha na tafi ne, to ba haka bane rashin lafiya ne ya kwantar dani.



Anas ya cigaba da cewa "dan Allah Fatima kiji tausayina ki karb'i tayin soyayyata, ina k'aunarki fiye da yanda kike zato, dan Allah ki amince dani kinji Fatima na, kinga yanda duk k'aunarki tabi ta ramar dani ko, ki taimakeni ki amince dani."



Fatima ta cigaba da murmushi kawai, Anas yace "dan Allah kice wani abu mana."



Fatima cike da jin kunya tace "tunda kaga na saurareka ai alamu ne na ka samu karb'uwa a zuciyata."



Anas yai wani irin tsalle a gabanta na jin dad'in kalamanta wanda saida ya saka Fatima yin dariya, nan dai suka d'an tab'a hira wanda duk yawanci zancen na Anas ne, ita daga eh sai aa, ko tai murmushi.



Haka suka kasance cikin farin ciki mara misaltuwa daga shi har ita Fatiman.



Bayan wani lokaci sukayi sallama Fatima ta shiga cikin gida, shi kuma ya shiga mota ya tafi.



Tun daga wannan lokacin Anas ya samu kan Fatima, kullum suna tare, idan ya bar wajenta to suna mak'ale da juna ta waya.



Hakanan Fatima take jinta kamar wata sabuwar mace daban a rayuwa, jin zuciyarta take a wani yanayi na musamman, gani take ma kamar bata tab'a kasancewa da wani saurayi ba sai shi, tana matuk'ar k'aunarshi, sun shak'u da juna fiye da zaton mai karatu.



Yau Asabar, yauma kamar kullum suna zaune a k'ofar gida suna hira, idan suna tare ji sukeyi kamar karsu rabu.



Anas ya kalli agogo sannan ya zuba tsaki, Fatima ta kalleshi tace "meyafaru me sona?"



Anas ya k'ara b'ata rai, sannan yace "wallahi mai sona idan nazo wajenki ji nakeyi banaso mu rabu, kuma naga kamar ma idan nazo nan lokaci yafi gudu."



Fatima tai murmushi, sannan tace "kaine dai kawai kake ganin haka saboda kana tare dani.



Anas yace "dan Allah mai sona ki amince mini in turo magabatana suzo nemarmin aurenki."



Fatima tace "to wannan ba maganata bace, zan sanar da Inna buk'atarka, in yaso sai ta fad'awa kawu, shi kuma kawu shine zai sanar damu ranar da ya tsayar suzo suyi magana dashi."



Anas cikin tsantsar farin ciki yace "to Allah ya nuna mana ranar."



Anas yai shiru, dukkansu sukayi shiru na wasu lokuta, sannan ya d'ago ya kalli Fatima yace "Fatima inaso in fad'a miki wata magana amma bansan yanda zaki kalleta ba."



Fatima tace "karka damu ka fad'amin kanka tsaye zanyi maka kyakkyawar fahimta akai insha Allah."



Anas yaja dogon numfashi, sannan yace "Fatima kiyi hak'uri da jinkirin fad'a miki wannan maganar sai yanzu, inada dalilina na k'in fad'a miki tsawon lokacin da muke tare, Fatima inada mata d'aya da yara biyu, abinda yasa ban fad'a miki ba tun tuni saboda karki gujeni, musamman da na samu labarin abinda ya faru dake a baya shine dalilin jin tsoron fad'a miki."



Fatima ta fara ja da baya tana hawaye tace "dama ni na sani da wuya a samu nagartaccen mutum a cikinku maza, kanada mata ka b'oyemin ka bari na saka soyayyarka a cikin zuciyata, to bazan iya aurenka ba muyi hak'uri da juna."



Tana gama fad'in haka ta juya da gudu ta shige gida abinta, Anas yana k'wala mata kira amma ina ko waiwayowa batayi ba har ta shige gida.



A cikin gidan kuwa Asabe da Tanimu ne suke magana akan Fatiman Asabe tace "sai kaga ikon Allah maigida cikin lokaci k'ank'ani Fatima ta saki jikinta, ta amince da maganar Anas kuma."



Tanimu yace "wallahi kuwa, tai mana bazata, to ai hakan yafi, Allah ne ya karb'i addu'ar da muke a kanta, ya saka mata hak'uri da juriya, kuma yanda na lura da ita tana k'aunar yaron nan sosai," Asabe tai dariya, sannan tace ashe ka lura da hakan kaima."



Suna cikin magana sai sukaga Fatiman ta fad'o cikin gidan tana kuka ta fad'a d'aki da gudu.



Asabe tace subhanallahi, meke faruwane kuma," Tanimu yace "kamar muryar Fatima."



Asabe tace "itace ta shigo tana kuka kuma," Tanimu ya mik'e tsaye yace "to muje mana muga halin da take ciki kinja kuma kin tsaya."



Kai tsaye d'akin Fatiman suka nufa, suka shiga ciki suka tsaya cirko cirko, Tanimu ya kalleta yace "ke Fatima, kinga, yi shiru ki fad'amana meke faruwa ne wai kika shigo gida kina kuka?"



Fatima ta sassauta kukanda take, sannan ta fad'a musu abinda Anas ya sanar da ita, su kansu jikinsu yai sanyi, amma hakanan dai suka k'arfafa mata guiwa akan ai ba duka kishiyoyi bane suka taru suka zama d'aya, akwai na k'warai kuma nagartattu, ita dai in banda kuka ba abinda take sai kuma fad'i take "ni bana sonshi, na hak'ura da aurenshi."



Tanimu yace "to shikenan, munji ba zaki aureshi ba, kukan ya isa haka kar kiyi zazzab'i kinji ko."



Sai da suka samu tai shiru sannan suka koma d'akin Asaben suka barta a nata d'akin, inda suka zauna suna cigaba da tattauna lamarin.




_Wata sabuwa inji 'yan caca, to bari dai mu zuba idanu muga yanda zata kasance._

             Page 80 to 85




Haka Fatima ta kasance a ranar cikin tunanin wai ita shikenan masu mata ne zasuita zuwa wajenta, gashi dai duk maneman nata da mazajen da ta aura duk Allah yanata had'ata da attajiran masu kud'ine, sedai kuma dukkansu sunada mata.



Haka ta kwana tunani har garin Allah ya waye.



Shima Anas haka ta kasance a b'angarenshi, yama fi Fatiman shiga damuwa, dan gani yake kamar yana shirin rasata, bayan ya gama gina kyakkyawan tubali me k'arfi na so da shak'uwa a tsakaninsu.



Yaji haushin kanshi na k'in fad'amata tun farko, gashi yanzu shak'uwarsu tai nisa ta yanda yake tunanin daga ita har shi babu wanda zaiso ya rabu da d'an'uwansa.



Haka suka kwana suna tunani a cikin ransu, washe gari bayan Anas ya shirya ya sallami iyalinshi, dama kamar kullum yana biyawa gidan iyayensa ya gaishesu, yauma daga gidanshi kai tsaye can ya nufa.



Bayan ya isa gidan ya faka motarsa, sannan ya fito ya shiga ciki.



A falo ya tarar dasu suna karyawa, dukkansu suka kalleshi tare da had'a bakinsu wajen cewa "yaro lafiya yau da buga irin wannan sammakon haka?"



Anas ya k'arasa kan dining d'in ya ja kujera d'aya ya zauna, sannan ya gaishe dasu dukkansu suka amsa, sannan suka sake maimaita mishi tambayarsu.



Anas yace "Mum, Dad, kawai ku taya d'anku da addu'a, amma farin ciki yana neman yin nesa dani."



Dukkansu ajiye abincin da sukeci sukai tare da maida hankalinsu kanshi, sai jero mishi tambaya suke akan menene ya faru dashi har haka.



Anas ya kallesu duka yace "kundai san a baya ku kuka zab'armin matar da zan aura ko, kuma ba wai dan a raina akwai k'aunarta ba, a'a sedai na amince da itane kuma na nuna mata so saboda ku kuka zab'amin ita a matsayin mata, kuma ni duk abinda kuka zab'amin a rayuwa ina karb'arsa ko menene muddin be sab'awa Allah ba tunda nima kuna k'aunar abinda nake k'auna."



A lokaci guda dukkansu sukace "hakane yaro," Anas yace "to a yanzu haka naga matar da nakeso in aura."



Dad d'inshi ya kalleshi yace "to dan zaka k'ara aure menene na shiga damuwar yaro, bayan kai da kanka ka fad'i muna k'aunar duk abinda kakeso, fad'a mana menene damuwar?"



Anas yace "Dad akwai matsala ne," nan ya fad'a musu abinda ya faru jiya da kuma dalilanta na baya na gudun auren me mata.



Dukkansu sukayi shiru na wasu lokuta, sannan daga baya Dad d'inshi ya kalleshi yace "karka damu yaro, yanzu ka karya ka tashi kaje wajen aiki dan nasan baka tsaya kayi break fast ba, idan ka dawo kaga lokacin nima ina gida sai muje inga iyayenta ko, oya maza had'a abun karyawa."



Anas ya langab'ar da kai kamar wani k'aramin yaro, yace "ni na k'oshi, Dad kawai ka tashi muje yanzu."



Daddy yai murmushi, yace "wai Anas dan Allah sai yaushe zaka girmane, ji wata shagwab'a da kake sai kace mace, maza ka karya ka wuce wajen aiki idan ka dawo sai muje kaji ko."



Mummy tace "k'yaleshi bari in bashi a baki tunda yau shagwab'ar ce ta motsa, haka kuwa akayi, dama yana kusa da mahaifiyar tashi, ita taita bashi a baki har saida yace ya k'oshi, saida suka tabbatar da farin ciki akan yaransu sannan suka samu suka lallab'ashi ya tafi wajen aiki, inda shima mahaifin nasa ya tafi nashi wajen aikin.



Sedai shi Anas kai tsaye hankalinshi da tunaninshi gabad'aya karkatar dashi yayi wajen gidansu Fatima, sai kawai ganinshi yayi a k'ofar gidan, so kenan, da yake hankalinshi da tunaninshi suna wajen Fatima shine zuciyarshi ta karkato dashi wajenta, amma in za'a kasheshi besan ya akai yazo k'ofar gidan ba.



Yaita faman doka mata kira a waya tak'i d'agawa, da yaga haka sai ya tura mata sak'o ta waya, yaja motarshi ya tafi dan yasan yanzu ba zata saurareshi ba tunda tana cikin fushi dashi.



Duk kiran da yake wa Fatiman tana kallo, hasalima wayar tana gefenta, amma sedai ta kalla ta kauda ido tak'i d'agawa, haka taita yi har ta katse.



Can sai ga sak'onshi ya shigo, cikin sauri takai hannunta ta d'auka ta fara karantawa.



_Fatima dama zan wuce wajen aiki ne na biyo ta wajenki domin in ganki, ki sani cewa kece mace ta farko wadda naji zuciyata tana k'auna, kuma Fatima zuciya wawuyace akan duk abinda takeso, sannan takan iya jure komai, sedai takan jefa bawa matsala, ki tausayamin *MAI SONA* ina d'aya daga cikin bayin Allah masu rauni wad'anda damuwa take iya sanadin kwantar dasu, ki gane cewa ba komai zuciyata ke iya jurewa ba, in kuma kinfi so ki rasani shikenan Fatima sai ki cigaba da wofintar dani, na tafi wajen aiki ki kulamin da kanki dan Allah, idan na dawo zan sake zuwa in ganki, amma dan girman Allah idan na dawo ki fito ki saurareni, zan iya jure komai amma banda nisanta kanki dani, ina sonki har abada, ANAS NE MAYEN K'AUNARKI_



Anas yana kan hanya yaji sak'o ya shigo, yana dubawa yaga Fatima ce ta bashi amsa, ai ba shiri ya faka motarshi, ya fara karanta sak'on.


_Ni kasha ina cikin natsuwa ne halan, taya mace zata samu irinka, kuma lokaci guda tai nesa dashi bataji wani abu ba? kuma wallahi ni ka daina yimin maganar wani zan rasaka, in ba haka ba wallahi zaka sani._



Fad'in farin cikin Anas ma ai b'ata baki ne, amsa ya tura mata a tak'aice.



_Sai na fad'i d'in, tafiya zanyi in mutu kawai, tunda kink'i ki kulani._



Fatima na ganin sak'on nashi sai tayi murmushi, sannan a fili tace "hum ka jishi, sai kace mutuwar a hannunshi take wai shi sai yaje ya mutu kawai," ta sake yin murmushi, sannan ta tura mishi da amsa.



_To wallahi ka mutu ka gani ni da kai ne, ni banson zancen mutuwar nan fa, na sani kowane d'an adam sai ya d'and'aneta, kawai muyi fatan  mu cika da imani, amma ba yanzu ba insha Allah, kaja mota ka tafi wajen aiki ranka ya dad'e, kuma kaima ka kula da kanka._



Cikin farin ciki ya kira mahaifinsa a waya, yana d'agawa yace "Dad albishirinka," Dad d'inshi yace "goro fari tas yaro," Anas yace "Fatima ta hak'ura," Dad yace Alhamdulillah, kaga shikenan na huta zuwa yi maka bikon mata," Anas yace "a'a Dad kar kaimin haka dan Allah, zamuje mana a k'ara bada hak'uri, kaga daganan ma kawai sai ka nemamin aurenta."



Dad yai dariya, sannan yace "to sai mun dawo sai muje, ka kula da kanka yarona," Anas yace "to Dadyna kaima ka kula da kanka, " da haka sukayi sallama Anas yaja motarsa ya k'arasa wajen aikinshi cike da walwala da tsantsar farin ciki.



Bayan Anas ya dawo aiki kai tsaye gidanshi ya wuce, wanda yake kusa da na iyayensa, yana shiga da gudu yaranshi suka taho sukai mishi oyoyo, ya d'aukesu d'aya bayan d'aya sannan ya saukesu,  matarshi tai mishi sannu da zuwa, ya amsa, sannan yace ta kai mishi ruwa yai wanka, bayan ya fito daga wanka ya shirya ta kawo mishi abinci, yace tabar abincin tukun zasuje wani waje shi da Dad d'insa idan ya dawo sai yaci.



Yai musu sallama ya fito ya nufi gidan iyayenshi.



Yana shiga falon suka kalleshi dukkansu sukayi dariya, Mummy tace "ka gani ko, dama nace maka ai yanzu zaizo ya tasaka a gaba."



Anas ya kallesu duka cikin shagwab'a yace "Mum Dad,  me kukewa dariya, maganata kukeyi ko?"



Dad yace "eh mana maganarka mukeyi sai gaka ka shigo, tashi muje."



Anas ya tashi, tare da saka hannayenshi a kafad'ar Dad d'in yace "to muje Dad."



Daddy yace "a haka zamuje neman auren?"



Anas ya cire hannunshi yana dariya yace "to muje na cire," suka yiwa Mummy sallama suka fita.



Haka Anas da iyayensa suke kasancewa bashida aboki ko d'aya sai iyayenshi, duk wani abu in ya taso dasu yake tattaunawa, hakan ne ya haifar da gagarumar shak'uwa matsananciya a tsakanin shi Anas d'in da iyayen nashi.



Suna k'arasowa k'ofar gidan Anas ya ciro wayarshi ya kira Fatima.



Fatima tana kallon wayar tak'i d'agawa, a zuciyarta tace "oh dan kaga d'azu na d'aga shine ka sake kira, to ai ko zan d'aga sai ka gane ka yimin laifi tukun, danma ina k'aunarka ai da ban k'ara kulaka, duk a cikin zuciyarta take sak'a hakan.



Saida yai mata kira sau biyar bata d'agaba, Anas ya kalli Dad d'insa, idanunshi har sun ciko da k'walla, yace "Dad kaga tak'i d'agawa ko, na fad'a maka bata hak'ura ba."



Dadynsa ya dafashi yace "Anaas, wai kai sau yaushe zaka zama jarumi ne, a haka kakeso kai mata biyun, oya maza goge hawayen nan dake idanunka banson ganinsu, muje k'ofar gidan kai sallama nasan za'a fito ganin masu sallamar."



Suka fito daga cikin mota, suka k'arasa k'ofar gidan, Anas yai sallama, inda suka taki sa'a mai gidan yana nan, shi ya amsa musu sallamar ma.



Ba'a d'auki lokaci ba sai ga Tanimu ya fito, Anas ya fara hangowa yace "ah Anas kaine, ashema ba kai d'aya bane," Anas yace "eh ni da mahaifina ne."



Nan sukai musabaha da Daddy inda Tanimu yai musu kyakkyawar tarba, bayan sun gaisa sannan suka sanar dashi abinda ke tafe dasu.



Yace su d'an yi hak'uri yana zuwa, shiga ciki yayi ya sanar da Asabe cewa zai shigo da bak'i ciki sannan ya fito yai musu jagora har cikin gidan.



Bayan sun zazzauna, Asabe ta fito suka gaisa, sannan aka k'walawa Fatima kira, Fatima dake cikin d'akinta ta amsa, sannan ta fito ta shiga cikin d'akin d'auke da sallama, dukkansu suka amsa mata, Anas dama idanunshi suna kan k'ofa tunda yaji an kira sunanta, koda ta shigo kuwa idanunshi suna kanta.



Fatima ta zauna, sannan ta gaishe da mahaifin Anas cikin girmamawa, Dad ya amsa cikin farin ciki, tare da yaba halaye da d'abi'un Fatima, ta juya ta gaisheda Anas, Anas baki bud'e saboda tsabar murna ya amsa, sannan tai k'asa da kanta tana sauraren jin dalilin kiranta.



Tanimu yai gyaran murya yace "to Fatima ga mahaifin Anas nan yazo da kanshi domin nemarwa yaronshi afuwa akan laifin da yai miki, sedai nace musu bazance komai ba akai sai naji ta bakinki akan shin har yanzu kina sonshi, me kikace?"



Daddyn Anas yace "Fatima Anas ya sanar dani kina k'aunarshi, dalilin canzawarki saboda yace yanada matane, to indai wannan ne ki kwantar da hankalinki, matar Anas batada fitina, munada tabbacin zaku zauna lafiya da ita, amma in kin canza ra'ayi a kanshi kar kiji shakka ki sanar damu gaskiyar abinda yake ranki."



Anas yai caraf yace "kuma Daddy ka fad'a mata cewa nafi sonta, ita kad'ai nake k'auna."



Daddy yace "Anas kaimin shiru, inaso kaji tsoron Allah ka zama adalin namiji a tsakaninsu kaji koh," Anas yace 'to Dad insha Allah," Daddy ya kalli Fatima yace "ina jinki 'yata yi magana."



Fatima tai shiru, tana wasa da warwaron dake hannunta, ganin haka yasa Anas yai caraf yace "kawu tana sona har yanzu Allah ni na amsa mata."



Dad ya dafe goshi, sannan ya kalli Anas yace "Anas, kayi mana shiru muji ta bakinta mana."



Anas ya kalli Dad d'inshi yace "Dad tana jin kunya ne, ba zata iya magana a gabanku ba, shiyasa na amsa mata, ku k'yaleta kawai, Allah tana sona."



Daddy yace "to munji, tashi kaje waje ka jiramu mu gama magana da ita tukun sai ka dawo."



Anas yace "haba Dad ya zakace ni kad'ai in fita, ni kad'ai zaka kora banda ita, in hakane a koramu mana harda Fatiman," dukkansu saida sukayi dariya harda Tanimu.



Tanimu cikin dariya yace "Alhaji ka barshi yai zamanshi kawai, tunda tak'i magana hakan yana nuna mana bata sonshi ne yanzu, dan haka kawai sai su hak'ura da juna yaje ya nemi wata, cikin sauri Fatima ta d'ago kai, sai taga duk ita suke kallo, cikin sauri ta maida kanta k'asa.



Alhaji da Tanimu suka hau dariya, Tanimu yace "kaga ja'ira ko, gaskiyar Anas ne ashe har yanzu tana sonshi.



Fatima ta rufe fuskarta da hijabi a yayinda ta tashi tabar d'akin da gudu cike da jin kunya.



Nan dai su Dad suka tsayar da magana inda dama Daddy ya taho da sadaki, da kanshi ya mik'awa Tanimu, suka tsaida ranar aure nan da wata biyar masu zuwa.



Farin ciki a wajen Anas abun ba'a cewa komai, kafin su tafi yayi-yayi Fatima ta fito suyi sallama amma tace ita wallahi ba inda zata fito kunya takeji, hakanan sukayi sallama a waya.



Suka d'auki hanyar komawa gida cikeda jin dad'i, inda Daddy da kanshi yake yabawa da zab'in da Anas d'in yayi.




_To Allah ya kaimu ranar biki muci rangem mu kwaso shoki, kuma Allah yasa zatonsu akan uwar gida ya tabbata su zauna lafiya d'in._







Muje zuwa

          Page 85 to 90




Matar Anas wadda yake tare da ita a yanzu sunanta Fareeda, lokacin da iyayen Anas suka mallaka mishi company d'inshi ba dad'ewa a lokacin suka fara yi mishi maganar aure.



To gaskiya shi a lokacindai bashi da budurwa, hasalima be tab'a yin budurwa ba, kuma bayaso ya sab'a umurnin iyayenshi, hakan ne yasa ya basu dama akan su zab'ar mishi yarinyar da sukaga ta dace ya aura.



Cikin k'ank'anin lokaci kuwa Daddynshi ya nema mishi 'yar wani mak'wabcinsu.



Fareeda ba laifi tanada hali, ba kamar sauran kishiyoyin da Fatima tai zama dasu bane a baya, amma dai ance d'an  adam kamar wahainiya yake, bari muji ko nan gaba zata canza kala.



Duk da kasancewar Fareeda zab'in iyayenshi ce, amma be tab'a wulak'antata ba, ko ya nuna mata tsana ko wata alama da zata gane baya k'aunarta, kuma suna zaune lafiya, duk da ance yau da gobe sai Allah, kuma zo mu zauna dole wata rana a sab'a, amma dai zaman lafiyar shi yafi sab'anin yawa, wanan kenan.

...................................

A hanyar su ta komawa gida Daddy keta yaba natsuwar Fatima, inda yake yabawa da zab'en d'an nashi, shi kuwa Anas sai dad'i yakeji a ranshi yanata faman zubawa Dad d'in nashi uban surutu, yana k'ara yabon kayarshi.



Bayan sun k'arasa gida Daddy ya kalli Anas yace "to yaro hankali ya kwanta yanzu ko?"



Anas yace "eh wallahi Dad, bakaga bakina yak'i rufuwa ba saboda tsabar farin ciki."



Dad yai dariya, sannan yace "to tunda dare beyi sosai ba mu shiga ciki, inaso muyi magana da kai."



Anas yace "to Daddy nah muje," suka fito daga motar, suka jera suka shiga cikin falon d'auke da sallamarsu, Mummy dake zaune a falon ne ta amsa musu sallamarsu, tare da yi musu sannu da zuwa.



Dad ya kalli Anas yace "muci abinci tukun kafin muyi maganar ko?"



Anas yace "no Dad, abincin Fareeda yana can yana jirana, idan naci anan kaga bazan samu damar cin nata ba, kuma ba zataji dad'i ba."



Dad da Mummynshi sukai murmushin jin dad'i, sannan Dad yace "dakyau yarona, dama akan maganar Fareedan nakeso muyi magana, inaso kaji tsoron Allah yarona, kayi adalci a tsakanin matanka, rashin yin adalci babban bala'i ne maza suke jawowa kansu, domin Allah cewa yayi in kunsan zaku iya yin adalci to kuyi biyu, uku har zuwa hud'u, amma inhar kunsan kunaji a ranku ba zaku iya yin adalci ba to gwara kar kuyi, ku zauna da d'ayanku kawai yafi muku alkhairi, saboda rashin adalci wallahi babban bala'i ne, kuma babban zunubi muke kwasarwa kanmu wanda kuma Allah zaiyi mummunar sab'a mana a kanshi, dan haka ni na yarda da tarbiyar da muka baka, amma duk da haka ance in kanada kyau to ka k'ara da wanka,  dolenmu a matsayinmu na iyayenka mu k'ara tunatar dakai, kar kaga dan ba zab'inka bace Fareeda idan ka auri Fatima ka rik'a wulak'antata, kaji tsoron Allah idan kana ganin anan mu iyayenka ne zamu iya tsaya maka akan komai to a gaban Allah bamu da wannan damar, halinka da aikinka ne kawai zasu zama garkuwarka, dan haka yanda zaka kyautatawa Fatima haka nakeso itama Fareeda ka kyautata mata, kar ka sake koda wasa ka bambantasu akan komai, duk abinda ka yiwa d'aya tofa dole ka yiwa d'ayarsu kaji ko yarona?"



Anas yace "naji Dad, ku shaida ne akan ban tab'a nuna alamun cewa Fareeda ba zab'ina bace, ina nuna mata so da kulawa, kuma a yanzu ma nai k'arya ince bana sonta, tunda tana kyautatamin, amma dai gaskiya k'aunar da nakewa Fatima ya nunka wanda nake mata sau ba adadi."



Mummy ta kalleshi tace "to ai yaro hakan ne ba'a so ka rik'a fad'i, kabarshi a cikin zuciyarka kawai, karka fifita d'aya akan d'aya koda wasa, kabar komai a zuciyarka, karka nuna a fili kafi k'aunar d'aya akan d'aya, idan kai haka shine adalci, in kuwa kana fifita d'aya kana nuna kafi k'aunar d'aya akan idanun d'ayar tofa ba zataji dad'i ba, kuma hakan zai haifar da matsala, hatta kalmomin bakinka ya kasance iri d'aya akan kowacce kada ka zama munafikin mata ka rik'a kai tsegumin d'aya wajen d'aya ko ka rik'a sukar d'aya a lokacin da d'ayar batanan, idan fa suka ganoka to wallahi zasu rainaka kuma zasu had'e maka kai, yin hakan ma sam ba girma bane zubar da mutunci ne wajen mataye, masuyi ma Allah ya shiryesu su gyara, dan haka a kula yarona."



Anas ya numfasa sannan yace "naji duk abinda kukace iyayena, kuma insha Allah zanyi amfani da dukkanin nasihohinku da kuma shawarwarin da kuka bani, har kullum ina alfahari da samun iyaye na musamman wad'anda suka zamemin komai nawa, bayan kasancewarsu iyaye a gareni su suka zama aminaina, kuma abokan shawarata, sai kuma ya fara hawaye, ya cigaba da fad'in ina rok'on Allah ya fara d'aukar raina kafin ku, dan in kuka tafi kuka barni bansan ya zanyi da raina ba."



Dad dake kusa dashi ya share mishi hawaye, itama mahaifiyarsa ta taso ta dawo kusa dashi ta zauna a d'aya gefen, dukkansu suka dafashi, haka suke mishi a duk lokacinda ya shiga yanayi irin wannan sai su sakashi a tsakiya suita bashi dariya, yauma hakan ta kasance, saida sukaga ya dawo dai-dai sannan sukayi sallama dashi ya tafi gida.



A hanyarshi ta zuwa gidan nashi ne ya tsaya ya ciro wayarshi ya kira Fatima, Fatima har ta fara gyangyad'i a zaune tana zaman jiran kiranshi, ta wartsake ta d'auki kiran nashi.



Ji tayi yace "amincin Allah su tabbata a gareki MAI SONA," Fatima tai murmushi, sannan tace "ameen tare da kai hasken dake haskaka zuciyata."



Anas yace "kinga yau na makara wajen kiranki ko, wallahi ina wajen su Daddy suna yimin nawa nasihan ne irin wadda naga ana yi muku idan za'a kaiku d'akin miji."



Fatima tace "kai amma naji dad'i, gwara da suka yi maka nasiha akan kayi adalci a tsakaninmu, dan naga rawar kanka a kaina tayi yawa, harda fad'a a gabana wai kafi sona a kanta, to wallahi ba ruwana."



Anas ya kwab'e fuska kamar zaiyi kuka, sai kace tana kallonshi, yace "au hakama zakice, ai duk abinda nayi laifinki ne, ke kika maidani motsatstse, duk nabi na susuce, amma zaki shigo hannu na ai zaki sani."



Fatima tace "ai ba'a riga da an d'aura ba sai in fasa auren naka."



Cikin sauri a tsakiyar unguwa Anas ya zube yana cewa "dan Allah kiyi hak'uri guiwoyina a k'asa, in kika fasa aurena zanje in mutu ne kawai."



Fatima tai dariya sannan tace "to na yafe maka," sai yace  "to godiya nake gimbiyata, har naji sanyi a raina, laaaaa wai dama da gaske durk'ushewan nayi kamar kina gabana?"



Fatima tace "wai da gaske kake ko da wasa?" Anas yace "wallahi gani a k'asa na zuba uban kneel dwn, bari in tashi kar a fara taruwa a kaina," Fatima me zatai in ba dariya ba.



Anas yace "au dariya ma kikemin ko, Allah banda ina k'aunarki da nace Allah ya sakamin, to kawai dan nasan in na fad'a ya sakamin d'in in kika cutu ni zanfi shiga damuwane."



Fatima ta dad'e tana dariya har shima ya fara tayata, sannan tace mishi "kana inane?" Anas yace "gani nan a hanya zanje gida, zan shiga wajen uwargida," Fatima tace "to idan ka shiga ka gaishemin da ita."



Anas yace "to ina fatan ki kwanta cikin aminci, kuma ki kasance cikinsa daga yanzu har zuwa wayewar gari, kuma ki kulamin da kanki daga yanzu da zamuyi sallama har zuwa wayewar gari kulawarki ta dawo hannuna, kuma kiyi mafarkina, amma wallahi karki sake ki ganni a mafarkin na baki tsoro ko na saki kuka, kawai ki ganni ina saki dariya da kuma nishad'i da walwala."



Fatima ta sake yin dariya, sannan tace "an gama ranka ya dad'e, nima ka kulamin da kanka sosai dan Allah, duk da dai inada tabbas akan Antynah zata kulamana dakai sosai."



Anas yai dariya, sannan sukayi sallama Fatima ta kwanta bacci, inda shi kuma ya k'arasa cikin gidanshi.



Da sallamarshi ya shiga, inda ya tarar da ita zaune a falo tana jiran dawowarshi.



Fareeda ta amsa tare da yi mishi sannu da zuwa, Anas ya kalleta yai mata alamu da hannu akan tazo, tasowa tayi fuskarta d'auke da murmushi ya bud'e hannayenshi ta shige ciki ya rungumeta.


Anas yana rungume  da ita a jikinshi a haka suka k'arasa kan kujera suka zauna, Anas ya kalleta yace "yaranmu sunyi bacci ko?"



Fareeda tace "eh, sunata jiranka baka dawo ba sukayi bacci."



Anas yace "Allah sarki, wallahi Dad ne suka tsaidani muka tattauna bayan mun dawo."



Fareeda tace "yanzu dame zaka fara, wanka ko cin abinci?"



Anas yace "bari in fara yin wanka tukun sai inci girkin Uwargidana ko?" tai dariya tace "to tashi ka shiga, in ka fito abincinka yana jiranka."



Anas ya tashi yaje yai wanka, bayan ya fito be wani tsaya shiryawa ba ya saka doguwar riga sannan ya fito ya sameta a dining tana jiranshi.



Farida tana k'ok'ari wajen kulawa da mijinta ta fannin abinci da kuma tsaftar gida da jiki, sedai gaskiya tanada k'arancin kulawa ta fannin tararayar miji, kuma Anas yana son kulawa sosai a rayuwarshi, ko dan ya saba da yanayin rayuwar gidansu ne yanda ake tattalinshi da kulawa dashi sosai kamar jariri, to ita a fannin kulawar soyayya nata yai k'aranci. abun yana damunshi, yaso ya fahimtar da ita hakan amma ta kasa ganewa, sam ita irin 'yar soyayyar nan da irin su d'okin miji batayi, kuma akwai matan da suke yin haka tabbas, su babu ruwansu da bawa miji kulawa, kuma wasu mazan abinda suka fi so kenan.



Ya zauna yana cin abinci suna hira har ya gama, suka tafi d'akinshi domin su kwanta suyi bacci.



Bayan sun saka kayan bacci sun kwanta Anas ya rungumo matarshi, sannan yace "inason yin magana dake Fareeda."



Fareeda ta fuskanceshi, sannan tace "to ina jinka."



Anas ya sake cewa "ina fatan zakiywa maganar tawa kyakkyawar fahimta?"



Fareeda tace "eh insha Allah."



Anas yace "Fareeda aure nakeso in k'ara, ina fatan zakiymin kyakkyawar fahimta kuma zaki bani had'in kai ku zauna lafiya."


Ai Fareeda dake kwance bata san lokacinda tai wani irin tsalle ta tashi zaune ba, shima Anas d'in tashi yayi ya zauna ta kalleshi ta sake kallo tace "me kace, aure zaka sakeyi?"






_Ko Fareeda zata bada had'in kai?_

              Page 90 to 95




Shima Anas mik'ewa zaune yayi, kallonshi takeyi cike da mamaki tana hawaye, tace "fad'amin dame na rageka Anas da zaka yomin kishiya?"



Anas yace "babu abinda kika rageni dashi Fareeda, kina kyautatamin iya iyawarki, baki tab'a kamani da yi miki k'orafi akan gazawarki akan wani abu ba, kawai Allah ne ya hukunta yin auren bawai dan kin gaza a wani abu ba ko dan bana sonki, kuma................."



Hannu ta d'aga mishi, sannan tace "shikenan ya isa hakanan bana buk'atar dogon bayani daga gareka," tana gama fad'in haka ta tashi ta fice daga d'akinshi da gudu tana kuka.



Anas ya tashi ya bita domin ya cigaba da rarrashinta, sedai yana zuwa k'ofar d'akin ya tarar ta kulle ta ciki, jikinshi a sanyaye ya koma nashi d'akin ya kwanta, sai yaji har cikin zuciyarshi beji dad'in yanayin da ta shiga ciki ba wanda yasan tabbas shine sila, ya dad'e yana tunani,tare da rok'on Allah a cikin zuciyarshi akan Allah yasa dai kar Fareeda ta bashi matsala, be san lokacin da bacci ya d'aukeshi ba.



Da asuba ya fito ya tafi masallaci bayan yayi alwala, ya dawo ya sake dubawa d'akin Fareeda ko ta bud'e, sedai har yanzu a rufe, amma yaji alamun ta tashi tunda yaji motsi, sai ya wuce d'akinshi kai tsaye ya kwanta ya koma bacci.



Washe gari wajen k'arfe takwas yaji ana tashinsa, yana bud'e idanu yaga Fareeda ce take tashinsa, cikin sauri ya wartsake, mik'ewa zaune yayi, bakinshi d'auke da salati, Anas ya kalleta yace "my wife kin tashi?"



Fareeda ta gaisheshi ya amsa, sannan tace "na had'a maka ruwan wanka in ka fito break fast yana jiranka."



Yanayin yanda take magana sam ba walwala a tare da ita, gashi tasha kuka idanunta duk sun kumbura.



Anas har zai sake mata maganar jiya, sai ya fasa yace mata to kawai, sai ta juya ta fita, Anas ya tashi ya cire doguwar rigar jikinshi ya d'aura tawul ya shiga wanka.



yana fitowa ya shirya cikin shigar k'ananan kaya inda yai kyau sosai, sannan ya fito ya tarar an gyara ko ina  tsaf tsaf, ta b'angaren nan yana yabawa matarshi sosai, tana burgeshi matuk'a.



hangosu yayi ita da yaranshi Sudais, da Haneefa a dining table suna jiran fitowarshi.



Sudais ne babba, yana da shekara uku, inda Haneefa keda wata goma sha biyar a duniya, Sudais sai d'an karen wayau da surutu, inda ita kuma Haneefa take gwalamniyar magana har yanzu ba baki, amma tayi wayau kuma tana tafiya, ba inda bata zuwa a cikin gidan, yaran akwai kyau masha Allah, dan mahaifiyarsu itama ba baya ba a fagen kyau. 



yana tahowa wajen tun kafin ya taho yaran suka sauko daga kan kujerun suka taho da gudu, ya d'agasu d'aya bayan d'aya, sannan yaja hannunsu suka k'arasa suka zazzauna inda ya d'ora yaran akan jikinshi.



Fareeda ta zuzzuba musu abincin sannan ta ajiyewa kowa a gabanshi, yaran ne suka saka daru yau na Abbansu zasu ci, haka ya rik'a basu har suka k'oshi.



Fareeda tace "to tunda kun k'oshi ku k'yale Abbanku shima ya karya kar ya makara, kuje kuyi game, Sudais yaja hannun Haneefa suka tafi yin game, sannan Anas ya samu natsuwar fara cin nashi.



Yana cikin ci ne ya d'ago kanshi kenan yaga Fareeda ta k'ura mishi idanu k'walla cike da idanunta, cikin sauri ta goge kar ya gani, amma ina ta riga ta makaro ya rigada ya ganta.



Ajiye cokalin Anas yayi sannan ya k'ura mata idanu, kasa jurewa tayi ta fashe mishi da kuka.



Anas yai mata alamu da ta taso tazo inda yake, ba musu ta tashi taje,  hannunta ya rik'e tare da jawota ya zaunar da ita akan k'afafunshi, sannan ya rungume 'yar matarshi yana shafa bayanta alamun rarrashi akan tai shiru.



Fareeda kuwa ba abinda take banda ajiyar zuciya,  d'agota yayi suna fuskantar juna yace "Fareeda wallahi ba zanyi auren nan domin bana k'aunarki ba, kuma ba zanyi domin kin kasa dani ba, wallahi yin aurena babu abinda zai canza daga matsayinki a cikin zuciyata, Fareeda ina matuk'ar k'aunarki, silar auren nan ba zaisa in canza miki ba,  kuma wadda zan aura wallahi inada tabbas akan zaku zauna lafiya saboda halinta irin naki ne,  amma duk da an riga ankai sadaki an saka rana wata biyar zan iya hak'ura in fasa auren in har zuciyarki batayi na'am da auren ba.



Fareeda ta goge hawayen dake idanunta, sannan ta kalleshi muryarta na rawa tace "ni bazan zama sanadin rushewar aurenda zakayi ba, kawai dai kasan dole inji kishi a kanka musamman yanda na saba rayuwata dakai ni d'aya, ina mana addu'a Allah ya tabbatar da alkhairi, kuma Allah ya baka ikon yin adalci a tsakaninmu."



Anas yace "ameen matata, nagode da samun had'in kanki da kuma goyon bayanki, bari in tafi wajen aiki na kusa makara."



Fareeda tace "to," ya tashi ya shiga ya d'auko jakarshi ya fito, tana tsaye ya kalleta yai mata murmushi, itama ta mayar mishi da martani,duk da nata murmushin da ka gani kasan na yak'e ne, Allah kad'ai yasan me takeji a ranta, rungumeta yayi a jikinshi sannan ya saketa ya kira yaranshi suma ya rungumesu tare da mana musu kiss yai sallama dasu ya fita ya shiga motarsa, driver ya bud'e mishi gate ya fita.



saida ya bar unguwar sannan ya faka motar ya ciro wayarshi ya kira Fatima.



_Wannan halin Anas ne, baya iya yin waya da Fatima a gaban Fareeda kwata kwata a cikin gidanshi, ya d'auki yin hakan a gabanta matsayin cin fuska ne shiyasa ma baya yin hakan, sai kuma Allah ya taimakeshi itama Fatima ra'ayinsu yazo d'aya, bata kiranshi ko tura mishi sak'o a waya idan yana cikin gida har sai ya kirata ko ta tabbatar da yana wajen aiki, gaskiya da maza zasu gane su rik'a yin haka in zasu k'ara aure da sun rage rad'ad'i na farko da uwargida takeji, da kuma kawar da b'acin ran da zataji in taga hakan, kuma dama ance k'azantar da baka gani ba tsaftace, dan baka ganiba ma balle ta dameka, amma maza da dama a yanzu a gaban matansu suke waya da 'yan mata ko chatting wad'anda k'ila ma ba aurensu zaiyi ba, Allah yasa sauran maza suma suyi koyi da irin halin Anas, su zama masu gujewa duk abinda zai b'ata ran matansu._



ringing d'aya a na biyu ta d'aga da sallamarta, Anas ya amsa mata sallamar, sannan Fatima tace "har ka tashi hankalina na fara tunanin yau lafiya ba wayar ogana ba labarinta," sai Anas yai dariya sannan yace "to ke me ya hana da kika jini shiru ki kirani?"



Fatima tace "haba dai ai yin hakan bai dace ba, in matarka na kusa fa taji na kiraka ko kuma tazo wucewa taji kana waya, ko ta shigo ta  tarar dakai kana waya da ni? wallahi ko ni akaiwa haka bazan ji dad'i ba, kuma abinda bakaso ai maka to kar kaima wani ko kaso ai mishi."



Anas cike da farin ciki yace "ra'ayinmu ya zama iri d'aya dake, wallahi nayi dace da sa'a, har abada bazan daina farin ciki na had'uwa dake ba."



Fatima tace "nagode da yabo gwanina," Anas yace "tunda naji lafiyar amaryata itama to bari in tafi wajen aiki sai na dawo."



Fatima tace "to amma dai in ka tashi zaka biyo tanan ko? dan wallahi nai kewarka MAI SONA." Anas yace "zanzo mana inga sarauniyar dake zuba iko da mulkinta son rai a cikin zuciyata," Fatima tai dariya, sannan tace "to sai kazo ka kulamin da kanka dan Allah, Allah ya tsare mana kai, ya kare mana kai, ya dawo mana dakai lafiya."



Anas ya amsa da "ameen Amaryata, nagode da kyawawan addu'o'inki gareni." sukayi sallama, sannan ya tashi motarshi ya tafi wajen aiki.



Dama Anas gwanine wajen iya kyautatawa, to sai ya nunka kyautatawa matarshi Fareeda fiye da wadda yake mata a baya, so yake kawai ta kwantar da hankalinta, sannan kuma ya tabbatar mata da cewa babu abinda zai canza a tsakaninsu dan yayi aure.


Kuma duk wani abu da yasan in yayi zai sosa ranta baya yi ya kiyaye faruwar hakan, a yayin da lokaci yaketa k'aratowa, dole Fareeda ta saduda tare da rage damuwar da ta sakawa ranta tunda dai taga babu fashi dole sai anyi, sai da biki ya rage 'yan kwanaki ta sanar da 'yan uwanta itama, inda suma suka k'ara tausar zuciyarta, had'e da nunar mata da cewa su zauna lafiya muddin ita amaryar ta nemi hakan daga gareta, kada tace zata tayar da hankalinta tayi kishi irin na hauka, domin baya amfanar da komai sai koma baya a zuciyar miji, kawai k'ok'ari zatayi ta sake dagewa wajen kyautatawa mijinta, shine irin kishin da matan manzo s.a.w suka nuna na rige rige wajen kyautatawa mijinsu, da wannan shawarar ne suka k'ara kwantar mata da hankali.



Daddy ya baiwa Anas k'aton gida ya saka Amaryarshi a ciki ya karb'a tare da nuna farin cikinshi matuk'a, kuma yai mishi godiya, sedai yace shi bayaso ya rabawa matanshi gida, yafi so ya zauna dasu a gida d'aya yanaso ya had'a kansu, sannan yaranshi su taso waje guda kuma kansu a had'e, amma zasu koma wancan sabon gidan da ya bashi gaba d'ayansu bayan kwana biyu tunda yafi girma, iyayenshi sunyi na'am da kyakkyawan tunaninshi, inda suka bishi da addu'a akan Allah ya cika mishi burinshi

*BAYAN WATA BIYAR*

Rana bata k'arya sedai uwar d'iya taji kunya, yau ne dubbannin mutane suka shaida d'aurin auren Anas da Fatima akan sadaki dubu d'ari.



Yamma nayi motoci har guda goma suka jeru reras suna jiran fitowar Amarya domin a kaita gidan mijinta.



Asabe ita da kawunta sunyi mata nasiha sosai me matuk'ar ratsa zuciya, inda daga k'arshe sukai mata fatan alkhairi, aka fito da ita aka sakata cikin mota, inda k'annenta suke gefenta suma sunzo bikin su da 'yan uwan mahaifiyarta, sauran tawagar rakiyarta kowacce ta kama mota suka shishige aka jasu domin sada Amarya da gidan aurenta.



Bayan sun kaita suka juyo cike da farin ciki tare da yaba irin tarbar da abokiyar zamanta sukayi musu ita da 'yan uwanta.



To gadai Amarya an kai, muna yi musu fatan zaman lafiya mai d'orewa.






_Ayi hak'uri da wannan_👏

Bayan 'yan kai amarya sun tafi ba dad'ewa sai ga motar ango Anas bayan ya yi parking ya fito, ya nufi hanyar shiga cikin gidan, yana tafe yana jin wani nishad'i na musamman yana ziyartoshi, wai yau shine da mata tsala tsala guda biyu kuma duka babu ta yarwa a cikinsu, bakin nan nashi kamar gonar audiga sam yak'i rufuwa, daga wajen iyayenshi yake sun k'ara yi mishi nasiha akan yaji tsoron Allah yai adalci a tsakanin matanshi, kada ya rik'a nuna bambamci, bayan sun gama yi mishi nasihan ne sai yai sallama dasu ya garzayo gida wajen iyalenshi.



Da sallama a bakinsa ya shigo cikin d'akin hannunsa rik'e da,bak'ak'en ledoji guda biyu.



Ba kowa a falon, hakan yasa kai tsaye ya wuce d'akin uwar gidansa, yana shiga ya ganta zaune a gefen gado ta kifa kanta a jikin cinyoyinta, k'arasowa kusa da iya yayi, tare da ajiye ledojin a gefe, sannan ya zauna kusa da ita tare da dafata tare da kiran sunanta a hankali, yace "Fareeda."



Cikin hanzari ta d'ago kai, tare da sa hannu tana share hawayen da yaketa kai kawo akan fuskarta.



Ta kalleshi tare da k'ak'alo murmushin dole, tace "sannu da zuwa ango."



Anas be amsa ba, sai ma binta da idanu da yaketa faman yi, yana ganin yanda idanunta suka kumbura kuma sukayi ja, hannunta ya rik'e, sannan ya cigaba da kallonta yace "haba Fareeda, meyasa zaki tayar da hankalinki bayan da yardarki nai wannan auren kuma ban canza miki da komai ba, sanin kanki ne ina k'aunarki, kuma banyi wannan auren dan na gaji dake ba ko dan bakya kyautatamin, wallahi ki kwantar da hankalinki babu abinda auren nan zai canza a tsakaninmu insha Allah," nan dai yaita kwantar mata da hankali yana bata baki da kyawawan lafazi har sai da yaga alamun natsuwa ta fara ziyartota.



Anas yace "ina yaran nan ko har sunyi bacci？"



Fareeda tace "eh sunyi bacci tun d'azu."



Anas ya jawo ledojin nan ya bata duka biyun yace "gashinan ki duba ki zab'i d'aya," Fareeda taja leda d'aya tare da yi mishi godiya.



Ya dad'e a d'akin yana yi mata hira, harma saida yace ta kawo mishi abinci ya d'anci kad'an saboda kar yaje can ya kasa cin gasasshiyar kazar amarci kuma.😂



Hakan da yayi na rashin nuna zumud'inshi a auren da yayi harda tsayawa yaci abincinta, hakan ba k'aramin faranta ran Fareeda yayi ba, hakan yasa ta saki ranta ganin mijinta be canza mata da komai ba, saida yaga walaala akan fuskarta sannan yai mata sallama ya tafi d'akin Fatima wadda keta daman jiran shigowarshi.



Da sallamarshi ya shiga, amsa sallamar Fatima tayi cike da murmushi me d'auke da tarin farin ciki.



Fatima tace "barka da shigowa," fuskarta na lullub'e cikin mayafi ba tare da ta bud'e ba take gaisheshi.



Anas yai murmushi yace "bazan amsa ba har sai kin bud'emin kyakkyawar fuskarki na kalla," Fatima tace "nima bazan bud'e ba har sai ka fad'amin dalilin da yasa ka dad'e inata jiranka, da kuma dalilin da yasa ka tahomin kai kad'ai ba tare da abokanka ba."



Anas yai dariya tare da rik'e k'ugu yana cewa yau naga ikon Allah, can sai ya sake cewa "dad'in abun ai nima inada hannu," yana gama fad'in haka ya k'araso wajenta ya zauna tare da d'aga mayafin da ya rufe fuskarta.



Kallon juna sukayi tare da sakarwa junansu murmushi me cike da k'auna, Anas yace "kiyi hak'uri na dad'e da dawowa, amma na tsaya wajen yayarki ne na k'ara bata hak'uri akan kartai tunanin kin k'wace mata nine gabad'aya rabina ne yake wajenki rabi yana wajenta, daganan kuma mukayi hira."



Fatima tai dariya sosai, sannan tace "wato rabi rabi muke rik'e dashi ko, to gobe in Allah ya kaimu zan fad'awa Auntynah dan ta d'auko wuk'a mu rabaka gida biyu kawai kowa ta rik'e rabin a wajenta."



Gaba d'ayansu dariya sukai, sannan Fatima ta cigaba da cewa "nasan tun lokacin da ka shigo, kawai dai na tambaya ne domin inji me zakace, ina godiya ga mahaliccina Allah da ya had'ani da adalin namiji me k'ok'arin kiyaye hak'k'ok'in Allah a kansa," Anas yai murmushi, cike da jin dad'in yabon da ya samu wajen amaryarsa, Fatima ta cigaba da cewa "to su kuma abokanan naka me ya hanasu rakoka？"



Anas yace "ke wa yace miki ko inada abokai zan bari su shigo su kallemin matana kawai akan al'adar wofi, balle ni banda abokai, abokaina biyu ne kawai a duniya, sune mahaifiyata da mahaifina, kuma su suka yi mini duk wata nasiha da abokai sukeyi, daga k'arshe suka rakoni har bakin gate na shiga motata na taho gareku, amma yanzu abokaina sun k'aru, sun zama hud'u tunda ga Fareeda kuma gaki."



Fatima zatayi magana kenan ya katseta yace "wai haka dama kike da surutu, to gaskiya ki gimtse haka har sai munyi nafila mun godewa mahaliccinmu Allah, kuma munci kaji, in yaso sai kiyta zuba kamar kanya 'yan mata, amma yanzu tashi maza kiga."



Alwala suka d'aura sannan sukayi sallah raka'a biyu, bayan sun idar sukayi addu'a, sannan Anas ya dafa kanta tare da yi mata addu'ar da addini ya tanada.



Bayan sun kammala Anas ya jawo kaji sukaci, sannan bayan sun gama sukayi wanka suka sauya kayan jikinsu zuwa na bacci suka kwanta abinsu.


Da asuba suka tashi, sukayi wanka tare da yin alwala, Anas ya fito zai wuce masallaci amma saida ya tayar da Fareeda ya tabbatar da ta amsa mishi sannan ya wuce masallaci, ita kuma Fatima tayi tata a cikin d'aki, bata koma ta kwanta ba saida alfijir ya keto tana kwararo tasbihi gari yad'an fara haske sannan ta koma ta kwanta, Anas ma dama duk aka idar da sallah liman yana,yi musu karatu, hakan yasa shima be shigo gida ba har saida gari ya fara haske.



Koda ya shigo ya tarar Fatima har ta koma, hakan yasa shima ya kwanta.



K'arfe tara suka tashi suka shek'a wanka, sannan suka ci ado suka fito falo inda suka tarar da Fareeda zaune a falo.



K'arasowa sukayi, Anas ya zauna kusa da Fareeda, ta gaisheshi fuskarta d'auke da murmushi, Anas ya amsa, sannan ya kalli Fatima wadda take tsaye yace "kema k'araso ki zauna nan kusa dani," ba musu ta k'arasa ta zauna a gefenshi, suka sashi a tsakiya, Fatima ta gaishe da Fareeda, Fareeda ta amsa mata fuska a sake, sannan tace "abun karyawa ya kammala tun d'azu yake jiranku."



Fatima tace "ayya Aunty ai da kin kirani na tayaki aikin."



Fareeda tai murmushi, sannan tace "rufamin asiri wa yace miki amare suna aiki, zaki tayani ai k'anwata, amma ba yanzu ba," gabad'ayansu suka d'unguma kan dining inda suka tarar da yaransu suna cin nasu.



Bayan sun kammala cin abincin ne suka dawo falo suka zauna gabad'ayansu suna hira, ganin yaran nan sun mak'alk'ale Ftima kamar sun dad'e da saninta, gashi Anas yanaso yai magana da matan nashi, hakan ne yasa ya kora yaran yace suje wajen Tani 'yar aikin gidan su tayata hira.



Da gudunsu kuwa suka tafi, bayan sun tafi ya  rik'o hannayen matanshi duka, sannan ya  kalli Fareeda yace "Fareeda, ya juya b'angaren inda Fatima take itama yace Fatima, dukkaninku matana ne, kuma ke Fareeda nasha fad'i yanzu ma zan maimaita banyi auren nan dan kin tab'a sab'aminba, kuma bawai dan na daina k'aunarki ba, a'a kawai dai Allah ya rubutamin tabbas sai na zauna da mata biyu bawai dan naci zarafinki ko na wulak'antaki ba, kuma nasan ke macece mai ladabi da biyayya, baki da fad'a ko tashin hankali, ina fatan zaki cigaba ki d'ore akan wannan kyakkyawan halayen nan naki, ke kuma Fatima kema ganin natsuwa da tarbiyarki yasa na aureki, ina fatan zaki girmamata kuma zaki bada gudummawarki wajen cigaba da kawo zaman lafiya da kwanciyar hankali, ni kuma nai muku alk'awarin yin adalci a tsakaninku insha Allah tunda dukkaninku saboda ina k'aunarku ne yasa na zab'eku a matsayin abokan rayuwa, dan haka bazan yi sanadin raba kawunanku ba insha Allah."



Fareeda tace "insha Allah daga gareni,matsala ba zata taso ba, kuma ba za'a tab'a jin kanmu ba," Fatima ma ta amshe da fad'in "nima da yardar Allah ba abinda zai taso ta b'angarena sai kyautatawa da mutuntawa."



Anas yace "Alhamdulillah naji dad'in jin haka daga gareku, Allah ya zaunar damu lafiya," dukkaninsu suka amsa da "ameen" daganan kuma suka cigaba da hirarsu cike da nishad'i.



*Bari mu waiwaya b'angaren Mu'az da Amina muji*



Haka zaman su Mu'az da Amina ya cigaba da kasancewa na tsawon lokuta tare da cigaba da juya Mu'az kamar waina a tanda.



Kuma har yanzu Ameer yana can wajen kakarsa wato mahaifiyar Mu'az k'anwarsa tana kula masa dashi, sedai Mu'az yaje ya ganshi ya dawo ba damar ya maidashi cikin gidanshi.



'Yan uwansa kuwa tunda Fatima tabar gidan suka daina ziyartar gidan domin gudun cin mutuncin da Amina take musu.



Haka suka cigaba da shafe shekaru masu yawa tare, a tsakaninsu ba d'a ba ko b'atan wata sai uban mulki da take zubawa.



*BAYAN WANI LOKACI*

Mu'az ne zaune a kan kujera a cikin office d'inshi yana duba takardu sai aka k'wank'wasa k'ofa.



Mu'az ya amsa aka bud'e aka shigo, wani mutum ne tsaye bayanshi wata kyakkyawar mata ce take tsaye itama.



Mu'az ya d'ago kanshi ya kalli wannan mutumin yace "ya akayi ne？"



Mutumin nan yace "yallab'ai kan maganar sakatariyar da kace a nemo maka ne tunda sakatarenka ya rasu, to na kawo maka takardun wannan baiwar Allahn shine kace ta fara zuwa aiki yau, shine mukazo tare domin ka ganta ko akwai bayanin da zaka k'ara yi,mata game da tsari da kuma dokoki."



Mutumin nan yace Fa'iza matso mana ki gaishe da mai gida," tana k'ok'arin matsowa shi kuma Mu'az cike da takaici ya fara magana yace "dama ku bakuyi mata dukkanin bayanan dokokinmu ba kuka,kawota gaba........…………"



Kasa k'arasa zancenshi yayi sakamakon cin karo da wannan yarinyar me natsuwa, k'warjini, da cikar kamala.



Da yake da be hangota ba duk maganar da suketa yi da Mutumin nan wanda alamu ke nuna shima ma'aikaci ne a wajen.



Fa'iza ta gaisheshi, sannan Mu'az ya tambayeta sunanta ta fad'a mishi, sannan yace "to malama Fa'iza kuje ya nuna miki wajen da zaki zauna, da sauran abubuwan da ya kamata ki sani tunda kin amince zaki iya aiki a k'ark'ashinmu.



Tai mishi godiya cike da farin ciki, sannan suka tashi suka tafi idanun Mu'az yana kanta har suka fice.





_Topha, anya dai Mu'az baya nufin wani abu kuwa a ranshi, to inma wani abun ne ya zaiyi da oganniyarsa？_

                 Page 100 to 105




Sun dad'e zaune suna hira su uku, babu inda Anas yaje yana tare da matanshi suna hirarsu gwanin ban sha'awa.



Su biyu suka tashi suka shiga kicin suna girki, suna cikin aikin ne Anas ya shigo shima ya kama aiki wai zai tayasu, Fatima ta kalleshi tai dariya, tace "Aunty kinga ya shigo mana cikin kicin zai b'ata mana aiki ko, zo ki korar mana shi."



Anas yace "lallai ma yarinyar nan, danma kun samu zan taimaka muku ta yanda girkinku zaiyi dad'i kuma yai sauri sakamakon saka hannuna a cikin aikin shine kuke korata ko, to oya kuyi maza ku bani hak'uri kar kuja nai fushi nai tafiyata."



Fareeda ta kalleshi tace" ai mu tafiyar taka mukeso dama," Anas yace "to tunda korata kukeyi babu inda zanje inanan tare daku har ku gama."



Fareeda ta kalli Fatima tace "zo kiji," Fareeda ta rad'a mata abinda zasuyi su koreshi, sai suka tunkari,wajen da yake tsaye suna dariya, shi kuma ya dunk'ule hannu tare da ware k'afakamar irin me shirin yin damben nan, yace "an fad'a muku tsoronku zanji, ku shigoni kuga."



Cikin sauri suka k'arasa wajenshi suka fara yi mishi cakulkuli ta ko'ina nan da nan ya fara k'ok'arin guduwa, da k'yar ya samu Allah ya k'waceshi ya gudo falo, suka biyoshi, ya fad'a d'akinshi a guje, suma suka bishi yana shiga suka kulleshi suna fad'in "kai da fitowa sai mun gama."



Fatima ta kalli Fareeda tana nishi tace "Wash Allah, gaskiya Auntynah kina fama da wannan miji naki," dukkansu suka kwashe da dariya, sannan suka koma kicin suka cigaba da aikinsu.



_Tsakani da Allah banda k'arya da son kai a fad'i gaskiya a wannan zamanin mata nawa ne suke yin irin wannan da kishiyoyinsu？ ni har gobe inata tambaya akan wai shin menene ribar tashin hankali idan an yo miki kishiya, wace riba ake tsinta, har yanzu an gagara bani amsa, duk da dai nasan amsar bata wuce tarin nadama da danasani, dan Allah mu samarwa da mijinmu da yaranmu da abokan zamanmu kwanciyar hankali da farin ciki mara adadi, babu abinda yafi zaman lafiya dad'i a rayuwa, akasin haka ko bak'in ciki da b'acin rai da damuwa mara iyaka yake haifarwa, duk wanda zaice kiy tashin hankali a gidan da zaki samu aljannarki to wallahi Allah ba mai k'aunarki bane, ba mamaki ma haushin ganinku cikin kwanciyar hankali yasa yake k'ok'arin wargaza muku gida, muyi karatun ta natsu, in baku manta ba masu karatu labarin nan ya faru da gaske, kuma wadda ya faru a kanta ta bamu misali a rayuwa kan abubuwan da suka faru a kanta, tunda gashi a wannan zamanin ne dai da muke ciki an samu me hak'uri irinta, ita kanta Fareeda halinta abin koyi ne, domin kuwa tayi kishi kwatankwacin na matan manzo s.a.w, wallahi tashin hankali akan komai baida riba kuma wallahi baya kawo gyara sai b'arna, domin ba'a gyara kowane irin abu da b'arna, hak'uri ne ke kawo nasara a rayuwa, Allah kasa mu dace._


…………………………………………


*B'angaren su Mu'az*

Bayan kwana biyu a hankali shak'uwa ta fara shiga tsakanin Fa'iza da Mu'az, sai kuma soyayya me k'arfi ta shiga tsakaninsu, saboda yarinyar akwai natsuwa da kuma hankali, akwai alamun tarin tarbiyya a tare da ita.



Amma duk wannan neman auren a b'oye yaketa yi, dan kuwa matarshi Amina bata da labari.



Wasa wasa fa magana tai k'arfi har ya kaita ta gaisa da iyayenshi inda sukai matuk'ar yabawa da halaye da kuma d'abi'un Fa'iza, suna kuma ji a ransu insha Allah yanzu ne Mu'az zai san yayi aure.



*WACECE FA'IZA？*
Fa'iza 'ya ce ga wani malamin addini fitacce mai suna malam Habib, wanda yake koyar da maza da mata karatun addini, cikin garin ba inda ba'a b'ullowa domin neman ilimin addini a wajenshi, saidai Allah baya barin wani dan wani yaji dad'i, Allah ya d'auki ran mahaifin Fa'iza lokacin tana aure a gidan mijinta, bayan mahaifinta ya rasu ne shima mijinta ba dad'ewa Allah yai mishi rasuwa, wannan dalilin ne yasa ta yanke shawarar fara aiki tunda Allah ya bata ilimi da na bokon da na addinin, bayan haka idan ta tashi daga wajen aiki ta koma gida tana karantar da yara k'anana  mata da maza, inda ita kuma mahaifiyarta ke koyar da matan aure, a wannan gab'ar ne Mu'az ya shigo cikin rayuwarta suka fara sabo har ta kaisu ga sun fara soyayya, wannan kenan.



*Cigaban labarin*

Mu'az ya sanar da ita cewa shifa aurenta yakeso yayi, amma yanada mata d'aya da yaro d'aya wadda wata matar daban ta haifa mishi, idan ta amince zai turo magabatansa ayi magana domin tsaida ranar aure.



A nan itama Fa'iza ta shaida mishi cewa itama bazawara ce, ta tab'a yin aure mijinta ya rasu, shekararsu takwas a tare Allah ya d'auki ransa ba tare da ya basu d'a ko d'aya ba.



Mu'az yai mata gaisuwa, sannan ya k'ara tuntub'arta kan maganar turowan, kai tsaye ta amince, inda iyayen Mu'az sukaje aka samu kawunta wato wan mahaifinta aka tsaida ranar aure.



A yau lahadi ne aka tsayar da ranar aurensu, kuma a yau ne Mu'az ya yanke shawarar tunkarar Amina da maganar sa ranarshi da Fa'iza.



Yana shiga cikin gidan ya tarar da ita zaune a falo k'afarta d'aya kan d'aya tana kallo tana yi tana cin apple, sallamar mai gidan nata ne ta karkato da ita gareshi, da fara'arta ta kalleshi tace "wellcome sweet heart."



Da murmushi a kan fuskarsa shima ya k'arasa kusa da ita ya zauna, sannan ya amsa mata.



Bayan sun kammala cin abinci suna cikin hira mai dad'i sai Mu'az ya kalleta yace "akwai muhimmiyar maganar da nakeso muyi dake," Amina tace "to ina saurarenka me ya faru？"



Mu'az yace "kiyi hak'uri da abinda zan fad'i Amina, aure zan k'ara, yanzu haka ma maganar da nake miki ankai sadaki har an saka rana nan da wata uku masu zuwa."



Duk wata walwala da annurin da yake kan fuskar Amina gabad'aya d'aukewa yayi, ita ba damuwarta auren da yace zaiyi ba, domin tasan ko ta shigo sai taga dama zata zauna a gidan, damuwarta da fargabarta d'aya shine kar dai asirin da taiwa Mu'az ya karye ne, saboda ta tabbatar da cewa sai ta amince da magana ko wace irine kafin ya k'arfafata, amma a yau wai sai gashi kunnenta yana ji wai har ankai sadaki an saka ranar aure.



Amina ta juyo ta fuskanceshi, sannan tace "haka kawai naji zuciyata bata amince da wannan auren naka ba, dan haka ka janye banaso."



Mu'az ya kawar da kai dan bayaso ma su had'a idanu kar ta karyar mishi da zuciya, yace "Amina kiyi hak'uri kawai, amma ba yanda za'ai an riga ankai kud'in aure sannan ince aje a karb'o na fasa, bazai yiwu ba gaskiya," yana gama fad'in haka ya tashi ya shige cikin d'akinshi.



Yana shiga cikin d'aki Amina ta bishi da kallo cike da tsananin mamaki, ta dad'e a haka baki bud'e tana kallon ikon Allah, wai itace yau a karon farko Mu'az ke musa mata, tashi tayi ta fara kai kawo a falon, can sai tace "kai ina, wallahi da sake," tana gama fad'in haka sai ta doshi d'akinta da sauri.



Tana shiga ta d'auki wayarta ta kira Atine, ringing d'aya a na biyu ta d'aga, tana d'agawa ko gaisawa basuyi ba Amina tace "k'awata akwai matsala fa, Mu'az aure zaiyi wai," Atine tace "to menene abun tashin hankalin tunda kinsan komai a hannunki yake sai kinso ta shigo, inma ta shigo bakya son zamanta zaki iya turata waje, menene abun damuwar to sau nawa hakan ta faru kuma har yanzu ke kad'ai ce a gidan？"



Amina tace "ke wannan auren yasha bamban da sauran fa ahar rana akasa ban saniba sai yau ko ince ma sai yanzu nakeji," nan dai ta kwashe duk yansa sukayi da Mu'az d'in ta fad'a mata.



Atine tace "eh k'awata kinada gaskiya lallai akwai matsala, lallai dole hankalinki ya tashi, yanzu yaushe kike da lokaci muje wajen malam kai da aljanna sai hange domin a tari abun tun wuri？"



Amina tace "ni ko yanzu inada lokaci wallahi, bari in shiryo kawai inzo muje," Atine tace "yanzu kuma, ki bari dai zuwa gobe," Amina tace "dan Allah k'awata ki shirya kawai muje yanzu," Atine tace "to shikenan sai kinzo," nan sukayi sallama Amina ta hau shiri.



Bayan ta shirya tsaf ta fice ba tare da Mu'az ya sani ba, to waye shi dama da har zata nemi izinin fita a wajenshi, indai izini ne ai saidai ya nema a wajenta ita.



Cikin k'ank'anin lokaci Amina ta k'araso gidan k'awar tata, basu tsaya wani b'ata lokaci ba, dan ko zama Amina batayi ba suka fito suka d'auki hanya.



Da isarsu suka taki sa'a mutum d'ayane a ciki yau ba layi, yana fitowa suka shige, bayan sun gaishe da boka malam ko ince malum malum sai suka shiga kora mishi bayanin abinda ke tafe dasu, nan da nan boka wanda suke cutar kansu suke kiranshi malam amma inkiyarshi shine kai da aljanna sai hange, ya shiga bincike yana doka k'asa, can sai ya d'ago ya kallesu yace "gaskiya wannan auren ba makawa sai anyishi, saboda babu ta yanda za'ai mu iya yin nasarar aiki akan yarinyar da zai aura, domin kuwa ko kad'an bata wasa da addu'a, dan haka ki bari ayi auren zamu sake taimaka miki da maganin da indai kinyi amfani dashi kamar yanda akace to zaki cigaba da juya Mu'az ta yanda kikaso, ko kara kikasa kikace karya sake ya tsallaka be isa ya tsallaka ba, amma fa in har kika dage akan ja da wannan auren kinaso kiga be yiwu ba to tabbas asirinki duka zai karye kuma ba makawa sai anyi auren ba wanda ya isa ya dakatar da yiwuwar hakan sai Allah amma ba boka ko malam ba, in ta shigo a cikin gidan za'a iya fitar da ita, amma fa shigowa ba makawa sai ta shigo, dan haka in kin amince da ayi auren in yaso sai a mallake miki shi to ki fad'amin inji？"



Amina da Atine suka had'a kansu waje guda sukayi k'us-k'us, sannan suka d'ago a tare a lokaci guda, Amina ta kalleshi tace "na amince a bani maganin mallakar, in yaso in ta shigo ko da tuggu da bak'ar wahala tabar gidan."



Boka ya bata wani magani dake k'ulle a bak'ar leda, yace ta tabbatar ta saka mishi a abinci yaci har na tsawon kwana uku, in hakan ta faru to zata sha mamaki, sai laya da yace ta birne a k'ofar gidan, nan sukaita mishi godiya, daga k'arshe suka bashi kud'i yak'i karb'a yace sai buk'ata ta biya tukun kuma shi zai fad'a musu abinda zasu biya, sukayi mishi sallama suka kamo hanyar gida cike da farin ciki.







Muje zuwa

*IN KINASO KIFI K'ARFIN RAINI WAJEN KISHIYOYI TO KI TABBATAR DA KIN HAD'A ABUBUWA DA DAMA IRIN SU IYA GIRKI, TATTALI DA KULA DA MIJI, SALO SALO DABAN DABAN NA ZAMANTAKEWAR AURE, D'AUKAR WANKA DA KWALLIYA, UWA UBA TSABTA,  HAK'URI, DA KUMA TAUSAYI, IDAN HAR KIKA KULA DA WAD'ANNAN ABUBUWAN TO WALLAHI BA BOKA BA MALAM SAI KINFI KOWACE MACE FADA A WAJEN MAIGIDANKI.*





                 Page 105 to 110




Saida suka kammala girkinsu gabad'aya suka jajjera a diningsannan Fareeda taje ta bud'eshi ya fito yana zumb'ura baki alamun yayi fushi.


Sunata janshi da hira amma be sakko ba, yarinyarshi macen ne tace "Abba ka pata yai kamay jakaikuka, ko Umma ne ta daceka？"  Anas ya kalleta ya k'ara,tab'e fuska kamar gaske, yace "kin gansu nan Baby iyayenki sune sukaimin abinda banaso," tace "to kayi hakuyi idan ka cemun bickit ina bajancammushu ba," sai a lokacin ne ya saki fuska yanata magana da yaranshi ya share matan nashi, su kuwa sai dariya suke mishi suna tsokanarshi, harda cewa in ya k'ara binsu kicin sai sun k'ara yi mishi cakulkuli, Anas ya murgud'a musu baki sannan yace "sai an sake zuwa d'in," yanda yai maganar ne tare da murgud'a baki ya basu dariya.



Washe gari bayan zuwan amarya da kwana biyu kenan, suka sake shiga kicin domin su shirya lafiyayyen abinci, suna shiga ba dad'ewa Anas ya shigo kicin d'in, amma sai ya tsaya daga bakin k'ofa tare da rik'e k'ugu, basuma san ya shigo ba maganarshi suka jiyo yana cewa "gani na shigo yauma in an isa aimun wani abu aji ba dad'i," lokaci guda suka juyo, suna juyowa kuma sukayo kanshi, haba nan da nan ya danna a guje yabar bakin kicin d'in, Fatima da Fareeda sukayi dariya sannan suka cigaba da aikinsu, bayan sun gama karyawa an shirya yara sun tafi makaranta sai Anas ya d'aukesu duka ya kaisu gidan iyayenshi, inda iyayen nashi sukayi farin ciki matuk'a ganin kan matan yaronsu a had'e, anan ne kuma suka k'ara yi musu nasiha sosai.



Bayan sun baro gidan iyayen Anas sun dawo gida suka yada zango a falo suna hira, ya kallesu duka yace "kowacce daga cikinku ta fad'amin sunan da zata rik'a kirana dashi inji.



Haka gidan su Anas ya cigaba da kasancewa cikin farin ciki da walwala, yaran Fareeda sun zama na Fatima, domin kuwa wani irin sabo sukayi da ita, koma ince yanzu haka sun tare a wajenta.



*BAYAN WATA UKU*

Fatima ce take ta fama da ciwo kwana biyu har ya kaita ga bata ma iya yin aiki, hakan ne yasa abokiyar zamanta ta d'auke mata komai, duk da a cewarta ba wani ciwon azo a gani ba amma k'iri k'iri suka hanata yin komai har sai ta samu lafiya.



Yau Litinin, Fatima tana kwance a d'aki tana bacci, kwana biyun nan wani irin kasalallen bacci take fama dashi, Fareeda ta shigo ta tasheta domin tazo suci abinci.



Fatima na fitowa tana zuwa bakin dinin d'in k'anshin abincin ya taso mata da amai ba shiri ta ruga hanyar ban d'akin dake cikin d'akinta a daidai Anas ya shigo ya dawo daga wajen aiki, kuma yaga lokacin da Fatiman ta ruga a guje, k'arasowa wajen Fareedan yayi yace "meke damunta ne haka wai, kwana biyun nan duk Fatima ta canza？"



Fareeda tace "wallahi kwana biyun nan rashin lafiya taketa yi, yanzu ma da alama amai take," Anas yace "na fahimci akwai abinda ke damunta, amma dana tambayeta meke damunta sai tacemin ba komai, karb'i jakarnan ki kaimin d'aki bari inje in dubata," ta karb'i jakar ta wuce shi kuma cikin sauri ya nufi d'akin Fatiman, yana shiga ya tarar da ita a kwance, cikin sauri ya k'arasa wajenta tare da d'agota yana yi mata sannu ta amsa, Fareeda ce ta shigo da sallamarta, tai mata sannu ta amsa, yace "amai kikayi ko？" Fatima tace "eh wallahi dag fitowa cin abinci ina shak'ar k'amshin naji amai ya tasomin."



Fareeda tace "nifa ina tunanin cikine da ita," Anas ya kalli Fatima yace "to yanzu zaki iya tashi muje muci abincin ne？"



Fatima tace "bazan iya cin abincin ba, banson k'amshinsa," Anas ya kalleta yace "to haka zaki zauna bakici komai ba？ ya kalli Fareeda yace "to ko za'a daina wannan kalar abincin ne Fareeda har sai ta samu sauk'i？"



Fareeda ta b'ata rai, sannan tace "haba Anas kasan fa shine best food d'inka da nawa harma da yara, sannan a kanta kawai sai kace a daina har sai ta warke."



Anas yace "shikenan, Fatima tashi ki shirya muje asibiti a dubaki, Fatima cikin k'arfin hali ta tashi ta,shirya, suka fito suka yiwa Fareeda sallama dan tace ba zataje ba, da isarsu aka tabbatar mishi da cewa Fatima tana d'auke da ciki na wata biyu da sati biyu, bayyanar da farin cikin Anas ma b'ata baki ne, bakinsa sam yak'i rufuwa.



Bayan sun dawo gida da murnarshi ya fad'awa Fareeda, sedai sam bega alamun farin cikin da yai tunanin gani daga gareta ba.



Ita kuwa Fareeda haushi yake bata da kishi, a ganinta yanata rawar kai akan rashin lafiyar Fatima sai kace a kanta zai fara samun yara.



To dama haka rayuwar take, dole dama wata rana aga sauyi na mutum koda tsakanin mata da miji ne kad'ai to ballantana kuma da kishiya.



Haka Fatima ta cigaba da samun kulawa a wajen Fatima da Fareeda.



*BAYAN WATA TARA*

Ranar Asabar da asubar fari Fatima ta fara nak'uda inda Anas ya d'auketa suka tafi asibiti, acan ne ta haifo d'anta namiji kyakkyawa kamarshi da mahaifinsa har ta b'aci.



Cikin ikon Allah kuma Fatima ta samu kulawar likita sosai, Allah ya kawo mata ciwon da sauk'i, hakan yasa aka sallamesu suka dawo gida.



Suna dawowa Fareeda ta karb'i yaro tai mishi wanka ta shiryashi tsaf, sannan ta d'ora ruwan wankan mai jego a daidai Innarta Asabe tazo itace tai mata wanka, sannan tayi mata wasu abubuwan da baza'a rasa ba, tai mata sallama da cewar gobe in Allah ya kaimu zata dawo.



'Yan barka ne sukaita shigowa harda iyayen Anas, hakanne yasa Anas d'in be samu damar keb'ewa da Fatima suyi magana me tsawo ba har tsawon kwana uku.



Yau Talata yau ne ya samu damar ganinta ita kad'ai a d'aki, bayan ya zauna kusa da ita sai yace "dama shawara nakeso muyi dake akan sunan da kikaga ya dace a saka wa wannan yaron？"



Fatima tace "ina rok'on alfarma akan a saka mishi sunan mahaifina," Anas yai murmushi, sannan yace "tunaninmu ya zama iri d'aya kenan," nan tai mishi godiya sosai da amincewarsa, ranar suna aka sakawa yaro sunan mahaifinta inda suke kiranshi da Dady, muma muna addu'a Allah ya raya abinda aka haifa, Allah ya sashi cikin nagartattun bayi.


*B'ANGAREN SU MU'AZ*

Amina tana dawowa daga wajen boka sai ta shirya lafiyayyen girkin da tasan Mu'az yafi so, yana dawowa kuwa da murnarta ta tarbeshi, aka kawo mishi abinci k'amshi ya doki hancinsa, yasha mamaki matuk'a, saboda shidai yasan ba son yi musu girki take ba, yawanci sai ya dawo ya siyo musu a waje, ko kuma tasa 'yan aiki su girka, kuma ba yanda ya iya dole yaci ko ba dad'i.



Amma yau abun mamaki sai ga abincin da yafi so a gabanshi, amma da yake ya d'auko yunwa hakan yasa yai fatali da mamakinsa gefe ya fara jefa lomomin abincin da yake gauraye da magani.



Haka Amina taita yi har tsawon kwana uku, amma dama ance karta yi bashi kowane irin umurni har sai bayan kwana d'aya da kammala yi mishi amfani da maganin.


Cikin Asuba Amina ta tashi tahau d'akawa Mu'az duka, a gigice ya tashi ya durk'ushe a gabanta yana fad'in gani Hajiya me kike so？"



Amina ta kalleshi fuska a d'aure tace "tun d'azu ina kiranka sai yanzu kaga damar tashi," Mu'az yace "tuba nake Hajiya kiyi hak'uri ba za'a sake ba," tace ya tashi yaje ya girka mata abinci yai wanke wanke yai shara idan ya gama yazo tana son magana dashi.



Cikin sauri ya amsa ya tashi ya fara aiwatar da aikin da ta sakashi, bayan ya gama yazo ya fad'a mata ya gama, Amina ta kalleshi tace "magana nakeso muyi game da aurenka, na amince kayi auren amma kuma dole insan duk wani abu game da auren kafin ka aiwatar da komai kana jina？" Mu'az yace "eh naji."


Amina ta sake cewa "yanzu ankai kayan lefe ne ko ba'a kai ba？" yace "a'a bankai ba amma na siya kayan harda akwatuna suna ajiye," Amina tace "akwati nawa ka siya？" Mu'az yace "akwati goma ne," Amina ta sakashi ya d'ebo duka akwatinan ta gani, bayan ta gama duddubawa sai ta janye takwas gefe, ta ware biyu gefe, kuma ta rage kayan ciki tace "to guda biyun nan da na ware su zaka kai," ba musu yace mata to, to waneshi da yin musu ma.



*RANA BATA K'ARYA*

A yau Juma'a, yaune mutane da dama suka shaida aure tsakanin Mu'az da Fa'iza, yamma nayi aka zo da mota guda d'aya itama uwargida ta bada ikon azo da ita, aka d'auki amarya a ciki aka kaita d'akin mijinta, sai muyi musu fatan Allah ya basu zaman lafiya.



Tun ranar da aka kawo Fa'iza cikin gidan nan har yau wajen watanta d'aya amma kwata kwata Mu'az be shigo wajenta ba, hakan ba k'aramin damunta yake ba, gashi tunda tazo 'yan uwan Mu'az ba wanda taga yazo har sau d'aya, hakan yasa jikinta ya bata cewa tabbas akwai wata a k'asa, sedai ta rasa waye zai fahimtar da ita abinda ke faruwa.



Yau Laraba, Fa'iza ce zaune a falo tana kallo, Amina ta fito daga cikin d'aki ta tsaya akan Fa'iza rik'e da k'ugu, Fa'iza ta kalli Aminan ta gaisheta amma tak'i amsawa, ana haka sai ga Mu'az ya shigo, Fa'iza tace mishi "sannu da zuwa," Mu'az da sauri ya kalli Amina yana jiran umurnin ya amsa ko kuwa, Amina ta kalleshi tace "wuce kaje ciki ka jirani ganinan zuwa," da saurinshi ya wuce har yana cin tuntub'e kamar zai fad'i.



Fa'iza ta tashi ta koma cikin d'aki zuciyarta na suya, ita ba kanta take koka mawa ba, kuma ko kad'an bata tausayawa kanta tausayin Mu'az ne ya kamata, taya za'ai magidanci kamarshi ya zama kamar bawa wanda aka siya da kud'i a cikin gidanshi, ganin kukan ba mafita bane yasa ta tashi zaune tare da jawo wayarta dake kusa da ita ta lalibo lambar Hafsat k'anwar Mu'az d'in, tana ringing d'aya a na biyu aka d'auka, da fara'ar Hafsat take cewa "matar yaya yakike ya amarci, kina dai kula da yayana dakyau sosai koh？"


Fa'iza tace "Hafsat dan Allah kizo gidan nan yanzu inason ganinki, Hafsat jin yanayin muryar Fa'iza ya tabbatar mata da akwai matsala, sai tace mata kawai gatanan zuwa.



Bayan Hafsat ta katse wayar mahaifiyarsu take tambayarta lafiya, Hafsat tace "Umma akwai matsala, Fa'iza ce matar yaya ta kirani daga jin muryarta kuka tayi wai inje, Allah idan naje shegiyar matar nan tashi ta yimin rashin mutunci bazan k'yaleta ba."



Umma tace "a'a karki kuskura inji wani abu ya biyo baya wallahi, kije kiji kiran da take miki, idan wani abun akai mata ki bata hak'uri, amma bayan wannan bance kiyi wani abun ba."



Cikin sauri Hafsat ta shirya ta tafi gidan yayanta, tana zuwa ta tarar ba kowa a falo, taji dad'in hakan dan dama ta tsani ganin matar nan a idonta, kai tsaye d'akin Fa'izan ta wuce, bayan sun gaisa ta shaida mata abunda ya faru d'azu wanda ta ganewa idonta, ta cigaba da cewa "wallahi ba haka kawai Amina tabar Mu'az ba," Hafsat tace "tab' to ai indai wannan tsinanniyar matar ne zakiga abinda yafi haka."


Fa'iza ta kalleta da mamaki a fuskarta, tace "dama kunsan yana cikin irin wannan yanayin amma kuka kasa d'aukar mataki akai Hafsa？"


Hafsat tace "wallahi mun sani, shiyasa ta hanashi rab'armu,ai saboda kar mu samu damar karya sihirinta, a kwanakin baya yakan d'an lek'amu, amma yanzu abun ya dawo sabo."



Fa'iza ta numfasa, sannan tace "to wallahi tallahi sai nayi iyakar k'ok'arina wajen karya duk wani asiri nata.



Hirarsu Amina ta jiyo tazo d'akinta ta kalli Mu'az dake mata guga tace "kashe ayon d'in nan, ya kashe, ta sake cewa wato kai ka bawa k'anwarka damar zuwa gidan nan ta shige d'akin Fa'iza suna gulmata ko？"



Mu'az ya zazzaro idanu had'e da dafa k'irji yace "ni！！！wallahi bansan da zuwanta ba, kiyi hak'uri,"tace "to maza kaje ka koreta," ya tashi cikin sauri yana cewa to bari inje in koreta."



Suna cikin hira Mu'az ya fad'o d'akin kamar an jehoshi, wannan shine karo na farko da ya shigo d'akin Fa'iza, kallon Hafsat yayi yace "tashi ki fitarmin daga gida, kuma kar na sake ganin kinzo gidan nan in ba Amina ta kiraki ba."



Kuka sukeyi daga ita har Fa'iza, cikin kuka ta tashi ta fice daga gidan, saida yaga fitarta sannan ya dawo ya wuce d'akin Aminan ya cigaba da bata hak'uri.







MUJE ZUWA
