                 Page 110 to 115




Haka zama ya cigaba da wanzuwa a cikin gidan Anas cikin kwanciyar hankali.



Sedai ance dolen dole in zama yai zama sai an samu sab'ani a rayuwa, a cikin gidan, dole wani lokacin Fareeda ta b'ata mata rai, itama haka Fatiman dole wata rana ta b'atawa Fareedan rai haka zaman tare ya gada.


*B'ANGAREN SU MU'AZ*




Tun daga wannan ranar ne Fa'iza ta cire komai a ranta ta dage da addu'o'i ba dare ba rana, duk wasu addu'o'i na karya sihiri babu wanda bata sani ba, sedai a yanzu babbar matsalarta shine dole akwai,addu'ar da zatai mishi a cikin ruwa yasha kuma ta shafa mishi a jiki, sedai sam Amina bata bari Mu'az d'in ya nufo inda Fa'izan take, saboda bokanta yace mata kada ta kuskura ta bari su rik'a had'uwa da wannan matar tashi, duk wata hanya ta had'uwarsu tayi k'ok'ari ta toshe, saboda zata iya karya asirin da akai mishi ta hanyar addu'a, kuma karyewar sihirin dai-dai yake da tonon asirinta a gidan, wannan dalilin ne yasa take mugun taka tsan-tsan akai.



Ita kuma Fa'iza duk wani k'ok'ari da zatayi domin ta ganshi tayi amma abun yaci tura, sai su shafe tsawon wata ma bata ganshi ba, domin Amina batason ma suga juna.



Da safe wajen aiki yake tafiya, in ya dawo yana cikin d'akinshi ko na Amina, baya zaman falo shi da gidanshi amma an hana, ko unguwa zata tafi tare suke fita ya wuce wajen aiki itama ta wuce inda zataje, bayan ta jaddada mishi cewa kar ya kuskura ya sake koda wasa ya koma gida har sai ta dawo, hakan yasa ko ya tashi aiki baya tahowa gida har sai ta kira waya ta fad'a mishi ta dawo sannan zai rufo office d'inshi ya taho.



Babban abinda yafi damun Fa'iza shine yanda aka rabashi da iyayenshi da 'yan uwanshi, sannan ga d'anshi, wannan abun yafi komai d'aga hankalinta.



Yau Laraba, kuma a yauma Amina shiryawa tayi zata tafi anguwa, tare suka fito da Mu'az d'in, suka shiga mota yaja suka tafi.



Gidan k'awarta aminiyarta Atine zata tafi, har k'ofar gidan ya sauketa, bayan yayi parking sai ta bud'e k'ofa ta fito, ta kalleshi tace "zan kiraka kazo ka d'aukeni sai mu koma gida tare kaji koh？



Mu'az ya kalleta yace "to shikenan yanda kikace dole haka za'ayi," ya juya kan motar tashi ya tafi wajen aiki, inda ita kuma tuni ta dad'e da shigewa cikin gidan.



Fa'iza taga lokacin da suka fita, kuma kuma akan idonta suka shiga mota, kuma ta tabbatar in sukayi irin wannan fitar to sai yamma ko dare.




tashi tayi cikin sauri ta d'ebo ruwa ta zauna a gefen gadonta ta fara addu'o'i tana tofawa a cikin wannan ruwan, bayan ta kammala taje d'akin Mu'az d'in inda ta tarar dashi a haukace duk ya yamutse, ajiye ruwan tayi a gefe ta cire zanin gadon da filon ta d'auko wani me kyau ta shimfid'a, sannan ta kwashe wanda ta cire da kuma kayan sawanshi masu datti da suke baje a k'asa a d'akin ta sakasu a cikin injin d'in wanki, ta share d'akin ta d'auko turaren wuta a d'akinta ta turare d'akin, kafin kace me d'aki yayi matuk'ar kyau dashi.



bayan kayan sunyi fes sai ta shasshanya mishi, ta shiga ban d'akinshi shima ta wanke sannan ta dawo ta d'auki wannan kofin dake d'auke da ruwan addu'a ta fara yayyafawa a kan gadonshi da filallukanshi, sannan tabi duk wata kusurwar dake cikin d'akin ta faffesa.



duk wani abu dake d'akin saida ta saka mishi a jiki, hatta suturunshi dake cikin sip.



Bayan ta kammala ta jawo mishi ta rufe, tabi falon ma duk ta yarfa a ko'ina, taje zata saka a cikin d'akin Aminan ma ta tarar a rufe yake, hakan yasa ta hak'ura ta dawo falo ta zauna.



Wani ruwan ta sake d'ebowa ta zauna a d'aya daga cikin kujerun dake falon tayi wasu addu'o'in sannan ta tofa a cikin ruwan dake kofin, bayan ta kammala ta ajiye gefe tare da rufewa, sai bayan awa d'aya da fitarsu Amina wani dabara tazo mata a rai, amma tasan dole tana buk'atar taimako, hakan yasa ta tashi tai sauri ta shiga d'aki ta d'auki wayarta, lambar Hafsat ta lalibo ta danna mata kira, ringing d'aya tayi aka d'auka.



Bayan sun gaisa Fa'iza tace "Hafsat dan Allah kizo yanzu, a yau nakeso in kawo k'arshen duk wani tuggu da matarnan ta dad'e tana shiryawa."



Hafsat tace "hum ba inda zanzo sai kace kin manta wulak'ancin da yai mini ya fifita matarshi a kaina, Umma ma ta hanani zuwa gidan, tace bazan k'ara zuwa ba.



Suna cikin magana Fa'iza taga bak'in hayak'i yana fitowa daga d'akin Mu'az, saida ya gama jujjuyawa sannan yafita daga gidan ta k'ofar falon, Hafsat ce taketa Hello hello saboda shirun da taji Fa'izan tayi.



Fa'iza tace "Hafsat akwai alamun nasara wallahi," ta fad'a mata abinda tayi, sannan ta fad'a mata wanda zatai yanzu, ta cigaba da rok'onta tazo yanzu dan Allah bazata iya aikin ita kad'ai ba.



Hafsat tace "gaskiya ni bazanzo ba su sake tozartani," Fa'iza tace "nace miki basanan dukkansu tare suka fita ta tafi unguwa shi ya wuce wajen aiki," Hafsat tace "to naji, sai ki tambayi Umma in ta amince shikenan," Fa'iza tace "bani Umman da sauri," Hafsat ta mik'awa Umma waya, bayan sun gaisa Fa'iza ta rok'i Umma tace dan Allah tabar Hafsa tazo ta tayata aiki bata jin dad'i ne, sai ta b'oyewa Umma gaskiyar abinda yake faruwa, so take sai sun samu nasara ta ganewa idonta amma yanzu bataso ta sani, Umma ta numfasa sannan tace "shikenan bakomai ta shirya tazo amma kar ta bari Aminan ta dawo ta sameta dan bana son tashin hankali, Allah ya k'ara sauk'i."



Fa'iza tai mata godiya sannan sukayi sallama, Hafsat ta shirya sannan taiwa Umma sallama ta nufi gidan yayanta Mu'az.



Fa'iza kuwa suna gama waya da Umma sai ta lalibo lambar Mu'az ta kira sedai har ta katse ba'a d'aga ba, ta kira har sau uku sannan ya d'aga, yana d'agawa sai yaji muryar Fa'iza tana cewa "kayi sauri ka dawo gida matarka Amina ta dawo yanzu ba lafiya kai sauri," tana gama fad'in haka ta katse wayar, Mu'az cikin kid'ima ya mik'e tsaye hannunsa rik'e da waya, ya kira lambar Amina nan aka shiga fad'a mishi wayarta tana kashe, rashin sani yafi dare duhu, ita kuma Amina ta kashe wayarta ne sunata faman hira da Atine, duk dan kar a dameta da kira.



Ganin lambarta baya shiga yasa ya zari makullin motarsa ya fito cikin sauri ya fad'a motarsa ya tayar da ita ya figeta ya nufi hanyar gidanshi guje, wasu daga cikin ma'aikata suna tambayarsa lafiya amma be amsa ba, haka suka biyoshi da sauri sedai tun kafin su k'araso wajen ya figi motar yai gaba.



Kafin ya k'araso Gidan Hafsat ta iso, tana k'arasowa ba dad'ewa sai sukaji k'arar motarshi ya dawo, cikin sauri Fa'iza ta d'auki wannan ruwan,addu'ar ta jawo hannun Hafsat sukazo bakin k'ofa suka tsaya, Fa'iza ta kalli Hafsat tace "so nake da ya k'wank'wasa ki bud'e ni kuma in samu in watsa mishi ruwan addu'ar nan a jiki," Hafsat tace "nifa Anty tsoro nakeji kar yai mana duka," Fa'iza ta dafata tace "ke wallahi karki damu babu wani abu da zai faru wallahi, insha Allah zamu samu nasara," jin alamun tafiyarshi yasa sukayi shiru har ya k'araso bakin k'ofar ya fara knoking.



Hafsat cikin rawar jiki ta kalli Fa'iza, Fa'iza ta d'aga mata kai alamar k'arfafa mata guiwa akan ta bud'e k'ofar, Hafsat ta saka hannu jikinta da hannunta duk suna rawa a haka takai hannunta cikin sauri zata bud'e sakamakon dukan k'ofar da Mu'az yake da k'arfi.



Hafsat tana bud'e k'ofar Fa'iza ta kurb'i ruwan nan a cikin bakinta ta fesawa Mu'az a fuska, kallo ya bita dashi yana tsaye, can sai kanshi ya fara juyawa, ganin haka yasa Fa'iza ta sake kurb'a ta k'ara fesa mishi, nan da nan jiri ya d'ibeshi sai ya zube musu a k'asa, Fa'iza tace "Alhamdulillah akwai alamun nasara, bari in shafe mishi jiki da sauran, bayan ta gama shafe mishi jikin da ruwan addu'ar sai tace kamashi mu shigar dashi cikin d'akinshi, insha Allah lafiya zai tashi."







Muje zuwa

*Ayi hak'uri da jina shiru na kwana biyu masoyana, hakan ya farune sakamakon rashin lafiyar da naketa fama dashi, amma yanzu gani na dawo dominku kuma insha Allah ban direwa har sai na kammala, nagode da addu'o'inku a gareni👏😍😘*




*🇸🇦ZAMAN AMANA WRITER'S soyayyarku a gareni ribace, halinmu yazo daya hakan sa'a ce, ni na shirya zanta saka kuiyyo dariya, kuma ku tuna dani duk rintsi karmuyi wariya, ina k'aunarku irin k'aunarnan dai d'aya tilo, mutanena a cigaba da baaaaaaasu mamaki kawai🤜🤛😍😘*






               Page 115 to 120




Bayan Fa'iza ta gama shafe mishi dukkanin jikinshi da addu'o'i, suka kamashi suka kaishi d'aki suna jira suga farfad'owarshi.



Mu'az ya d'auki dogon lokaci yana bacci sai wajen bayan la'asar wuraren k'arfe hud'u da rabi sannan ya tashi.



Da salati d'auke a bakinshi ya bud'e idanu tare da mik'ewa zaune, yamutsa fuska yake sakamakon jikinshi da yai mishi tsami, Fa'iza da Hafsa ya gani, wad'anda suke tare dashi tun d'azu, sallah ce kad'ai ke tayar dasu daga wajenshi.



Mu'az ya kallesu duka, sannan ya sake maida kanshi wajen Hafsa yace "Hafsa yau kece a gidan namu, ya kika baro Umma?"



Fa'iza da Hafsa suka kalli juna tare da yin murmushin farin ciki, sannan dukkaninsu suka d'aga hannu suna godewa Allah mahalicci.



Shima Mu'az murmushin ya tayasu, sannan yace "lafiya, me ya faru naga kuna hamdala?"



Hafsat tace "Yaya Umma tananan lafiya, kuma ka kwantar da hankalinka, koma menene ke faruwa zakaji daga bakin Aunty Fa'iza, ni yanzu gida zan tafi nasan yarona Ameer yanacan yana cigiyata."



Mu'az ya yunk'ura zai tashi yace "ah to bari muje in kaiki da motata, daga nan in gaisheda Uwata, kuma in duba yarona."



Fa'iza tai sauri ta rik'eshi tana cewa "a'a koma ka zauna jikinka ba k'wari gobe maje tare mu gaisheta," Mu'az ya bita da idanu tamkar be santa ba ko be tab'a ganinta ba.



Hafsat ma tace "a'a Yaya baka jin dad'i ka bari ka huta gobe in Allah ya kaimu sai kuzo d'in."



Shi Mu'az kallonsu yake fuskarshi na bayyanar da mamaki k'arara na maganarsu, nan dai Hafsat tai musu sallama ta tafi.



Fa'iza ce taita d'awainiya dashi, ta zuba mishi ruwan wanka yai wanka sannan ta zuba mishi abinci yaci, sannan ya kalleta yace ta fad'a mishi abinda ya faru dashi suke mishi wannan tarairayar.



Fa'iza tace "rashin lafiya kayi fa ba wani abu ba, shiyasa kaga dole mu kula dakai ko?"



Mu'az dai ya bita da kallo yanaso ya gano gaskiyane abinda ta fad'a mishi ko akasin haka.



Mu'az ya tashi ya d'auro alwala yai sallah, inda Fa'iza ta k'arashe gyare ko'ina fuskarta d'auke da tsananin farin ciki.



Hafsat kuwa tana komawa gida ta fad'awa Ummansu komai, Umma ta cika da farin ciki mara iyaka, tare da godewa Allah, sannan ta d'ora da addu'ar Allah ya kawo k'arshen zaman d'anta da wannan matar wato Amina.



Amina kuwa bayan magrib ta kalli Atine tace "bari in kira Mu'az yazo mu wuce gida, sai yaushe ke zaki lek'o?



Atine tace "ina nan zuwa karki damu k'awata, kinsan had'uwarmu bata wahala ai, tunda munfi k'arfin masu hanamun, sukayi wata arniyar dariya suka tafa."



Amina ta zaro wayarta ta kira lambar Mu'az a kashe, taita kira ana fad'a mata abu guda wai tana kashe, nan fa ranta ya b'aci ta fara sababi "lallai wannan mutumin har ni zai kashewa waya yau, to wallahi sai ya fuskanci tsattsauran mataki a wajena."



Taita bambami Atine tana bata hak'uri tana nunar mata da ta sani ko k'ila wani abun ne ya sameshi ko ya samu wayar tashi, hakan ne yasa da taga har an fara kiran sallah sai ta yanke shawarar tahau taxi kawai ta koma gida domin ba yanda ta iya, amma a rayuwarta babu abinda ta tsana irin hawan taxi.



Sukayi sallama da k'awarta ta fito ta samu abun hawa ta tafi gida.



Har k'ofar gidan aka kawota ta biyashi sannan ta fito ta k'wank'wasa me gadi yazo ya bud'e mata ta shigo cikin fushi musamman ma da taga motar Mu'az d'in, juyowa tayi ta kalli me gadin gidan wanda yaketa antayo mata sannu da zuwa amma bata amsa mishi ba, tace "tun yaushe Mu'az ya dawo cikin gidan nan?"



Me gadi yace "ai Hajiya kuna fita tare bayan kamar awa guda ya dawo, daganan kuma bai sake fita ba."



Zuciyar Amina ta k'ara tafasa, wato idan batanan dawowa gida yake yana cin amanarta, kuji 'yar banza sai kace karuwa ya aje a ciki ba matarsa ba😂



A zuciye ta doshi cikin gidan kamar zata tashi sama, tana zuwa k'ofar d'akin ta fara k'wank'wasawa, Fa'iza ce tazo ta bud'e, tana bud'ewa Amina ta shigo ta bangaje Fa'iza har saida takai k'asa, ta wuceta tayi kan Mu'az wanda ke zaune akan kujera, tana k'arasawa ta d'aga hannu ta wankeshi da mari, tana fad'in "dama dalilin da yasa ka kashe wayarka kenan ashe kananan kana cin amanata, har ni zaka wulak'anta, to wallahi kayi kad'an, kuma ka tashi maza ka wuce muje ciki."



Fa'iza dake k'asa sakamakon bangazar da tasha sai ta tashi domin ganin yanda wannan diramar zata kasance, jingina tayi da k'ofar d'aki tare da kalmashe hannayenta a k'irji tana murmushi, a zuciyarta tana cewa ina tausaya miki yau Amina da da yanzu ai ba d'aya bane.



Fa'iza ta kalli Amina tace "baki isaba wallahi babu inda zai tafi saboda ba ke kad'ai kike da iko dashi ba, nima mijina ne, dan haka inada buk'atar abuna a kusa dani nima."



Amina ta kalli Fa'iza a wulak'ance tace "ke kuma a suwa kike magana akan abunda ya shafeni ni da mijina, ke kanki wallahi albarkacina kikeci kike zaune a cikin gidan nan, idan naso yanzu sai nasa ya sakeki."



Fa'iza ta shek'e da dariya, sannan ta kalleta a shek'e tace "in kin isa yanzu ba sai gobe ba kisa ya sakeni, in ke 'yar halak ce ba shegiya ba."



Amina tana huci ta kalli Mu'az tace "inaso ka saketa yanzu a gabana kuma saki uku nakeso kai mata."



Fa'iza ta kalli Amina tace "ki sashi ya rubuta ne saboda ki fi samun hujja, nima in samu na kaiwa iyayena."



Amina ta kalli Mu'az tace "d'auko biro da takarda ka rubuta mata saki uku yanzu a gabana, tashi mana ina magana kana zaune."



Mu'az ba musu ya tashi ya shiga cikin d'akinshi ya d'auko biro da takarda ya dawo ya zauna, sannan ya d'ora takardar a kan jikinshi ya fara rubutu, bayan ya gama sai ya nunke, Amina ta fuzge tace "sai na gani na tabbatar saki ukun kai mata tukun, danma kar ta sake saka ran dawo............................."



Kasa k'arasawa tayi sakamakon ganin abunda ke d'auke a jikin takardar.



Sunanta ta gani yasa _ni Mu'az na saki Amina saki uku_



 Amina ta kalleshi tace "bakada hankaline, sunana ka saka a jiki fa."



Mu'az ya mik'e tsaye, sannan ya d'aga hannu ya wanketa da kyawawan mari har guda biyar, sannan ya nunata da hannu yace "duk da basu yimin bayani ba amma yanzu na fahimci komai, na tsinci wasu abubuwa daga cikin maganganunki, kije Allah ya isa tsakanina dake dan bazan yafe tarin cutarwar da kikaimin ba."



Fa'iza ta kalleta tace "ranki ya dad'e tunda an sakeki zo ki fice ko kuma wallahi insa ayi mana waje dake, banza mushirika kawai."



Ko motsi ta kasa yi saboda anyi mata abinda bata tab'a kawowa a ranta akwai ranar zuwansa ba.



Mu'az ya kalli Fa'iza yace "Swthrt rabu da ita, zo muje ciki kiy mini tausa, Fa'iza tazo suka sak'ale hannun junansu suka fara tafiya sannan suka tsaya Mu'az yai magana ba tare da ya juyo ba, yace " ke kuma ki tabbatar da ban fito na sameki a gidan nan ba wallahi, in ba haka ba," be k'arasa ba yaja matarshi Fa'iza suka shige cikin d'akinta.



Amina duk tana kallo, ji tayi wani dunk'ulen abu yana mata yawo yana tokare mata k'irji da mak'oshi, ta dad'e a tsaye a falon tana tunanin mafita, daganan ta yanke shawarar tunkarar bokansu, da wannan shawarar ta fita tabar gidan ba tare da ko d'akinta ta lek'a ba.



Ko da isar Amina wajen boka ta tarar da boka a kwance ya mutu harshe a waje kamar kare, bukkarshi ma ta k'one k'urmus.



_Ikon Allah me bada taimako kuma shine yau a mace🤔 ya akai be hanawa kanshi mutuwa ba, tunda yanada ikon yayewa mutane damuwa?🤔 wallahi muji tsoron Allah mu saki mutum mu kama Allah shine kad'ai in ka dogara gareshi baka tab'ewa kuma zaka samu waraka akan komai, amma boka bai isa yai maka komai ba, ko kanshi be isa karewa ba balle al'umma, in ya isa kuma meyasa ya bari ya mutu?_



Nan da nan ta k'wala wani irin k'ara, sai gani tayi wani bak'in abu yazo ya shige jikinta nan ta fad'i k'asa a sume.



Washe gari da safe kafin su Mu'az suje gaisheda Umma sai gasu ita da Hafsa harma da d'anshi Ameer sunzo gaisheshi.



Anan ne suka sanar dashi irin rayuwar da yayi a baya da kuma irin halinda ta jefashi, duk suka bashi labarin komai har rabashi dasu da d'anshi da tayi duk suka fad'a mishi, daga k'arshe Umma ta sake yi musu nasiha dukkansu.



Suna cikin magana sai ga Amina ta fad'o kai da ka ganta kasan ba hankali a tare da ita, tana shigowa ta fad'i k'asa ta fara surutai inda taita bankad'e abubuwan da sukaita shukawa ita da k'awarta Atine.



Tana gama fad'i kuma ta shek'e da dariya, sannan tasa kuka, sai kuma wata murya ta cigaba da cewa "ni sunana Moses, binda yasa nake muku bayani shine, itace ta jawo sanadin da na rasa yan'uwana sannan ta jawo sanadin da jikina ya k'one domin mune aka turo mu shiga jikin Mu'az muyi aiki a kanshi shiyasa nake bayyana muku komai duk da dama kun sani, dan haka mun tafi." yana gama fad'in haka Amina ta tashi a haukacenta sukayi waje.



_Wannan sai ya zama izina ga mazanda suke biyewa kyau kawai ko kuma sha'awa kawai, ga irin illolin da suke haifarwa nan, hakama matan duk muyi hattara aure ba ado bane da zamu dage sai me kyau, aure bauta ne domin aljanna ake yinsa, Allah kasa mu dace._



Amina tana fita daga  gidan gidan Atine ta dosa yara na binta a baya suna mata ihu aka, tana zuwa k'ofar gidan a dai-dai Atinen ta fito da alama unguwa zata tafi, daga nesa ta hango Aminan da gudu ta nufo gidanta yara suna biye da ita, ai ba shiri a guje ta juya ta fad'a cikin gidan, inda tasa me gadi ya dannawa k'ofar makullai, sannan tace karya sake ya barta ta shigo idan ta shigo to a bakin aikinsa.



Atine ta koma cikin gida tunda ba maganar fita unguwa kuma yau.



Me gadi ya koma wajen zamansa ya zaune inda yaketa mamaki wai yau Atine ke korar k'awarta aminiyarta wadda ya sansu tare, koda ya fara gadi a tare ya gansu.



_Ai dama haka rayuwar ta gada, wanda ka biye mawa ya kaika ya baro to idan abu ya sameka shine na farkon wanda zai fara guje maka, dan haka muyi hattara musan irin mutanen da zamuyi huld'a dasu, irin wad'anda zasu d'oramu kan hanya madaidaiciya, kuma ko menene ya samemu ba zasu gujemu ba, gadaishi Atine takai Amina ta barota daga k'arshe tana gudunta sakamakon lalurarta, ya Allah ka gyara zukatanmu ka bamu ikon yin kyakkyawan k'arshe._



Haka Amina ta cigaba da zama a haukace, kuma kasancewar ba yar garin bace ba'asan iyayenta bama, kuma zamanta cikin jama'a akwai hatsari saboda duka da take kaiwa, hakan yasa aka kamata aka kaita gidan mahaukata, anan ta cigaba da rayuwa.



Mu'az kuwa sabuwar rayuwa yake ginawa shi da amaryarsa Fa'iza, kuma ya dawo da yaronshi Ameer gidanshi, kullum cikin neman gafarar mahaifiyarsa da 'yan'uwansa yake duk da kuwa baya cikin hayyacinshi lokacin da abubuwan sukaita faruwa, matarshi Fa'iza kuwa tana d'auke da ciki na wata uku. 



To kaima fa Mu'az kaga rayuwa, sedai muce Allah ya kiyaye gaba ya sauki matarka Fa'iza lafiya.




Wannan kenan, b'angaren su Fatima kuwa.







Muje zuwa

*SUUREEYY@DSTAR*

*ALLAH YA JIK'AN MAHAIFINKI, ALLAH YASA MUTUWAR TA ZAMTO HUTU A GARESHI, ALLAH YASA ALJANNA CE MAKOMA AGARESHI, KI DAGE DA YI MISHI ADDU'A ITACE SOYAYYAR DA TAI SAURA A TSAKANINKI DASHI.*





           LAST PAGE 120 to 125




Bayan wata tara da 'yan kwanaki Fa'iza ta haifo d'iyarta mace kyakkyawa.



Ko da ranar suna ta zagayo aka sakawa yarinyar sunan Mahaifiyar Mu'az wato Ummul khulsum uwa me hak'uri da kawaici, inda suke kiranta da Ameera.



A washe garin ranar suna ne Atine tazo gidan inda ta gana da Mu'az tare da neman yafiyarsa akan irin abubuwan da sukai mishi, inda ta shaida musu itama yanzu haka aurenta ya mutu sakamakon asirin da taiwa mijinta ya karye, kuma saida akai da gaske iyayenta suka samo lafiyarta, domin duk asirin da ake musu an shaida musu cewa idan ya karye zai dawo jikinsu kuma hakanne ya faru.



Anan ne kuma ta shaida musu cewa sati d'aya kenan da rasuwar Amina bayan doguwar jinya da tayi a cikin asibitin dake gidan na mahaukata saboda wasu manya manyan k'uraje masu girma da shegen wari kuma marasa kyan gani da suka cika mata jiki, inda kuma aka kasa gano na menene, wad'anda har tsutsotsi ke fitowa daga cikinsu, an kaita asibiti daban daban, inda daga k'arshe aka dawo da ita asibitin dake cikin gidan mahaukatan aka cigaba da kulawa da ita, amma daga k'arshe zuba mata idanu akayi saboda sunyi iya yinsu, d'akin da aka ware mata kanshi bai shiguwa saboda tsabar d'oyin da yake yi.

Kuma a ranar ne dai Mu'az yake jin ashe ba 'yar garin bace a wani gari daban take, bayan shi kuma ce mishi tayi iyayenta sun rasu, kuma batasan danginta ba sakamakon tun kafin a haifeta suka bar garin suka dawo nan garin da zama, wanda itama Atinen tace batasan ko wane gari take ba, dalilin barinta garin kuwa shine mijinta ne yai mata kishiya kuma tayi asirin har ta gaji yak'i kamashi saboda baya wasa da addu'a hakama amaryar tashi, gashi mijin nasu irin tsayayyun mazajen nan ne wad'anda basa d'aukar zuga shiyasa duk makirci da tuggunta ta kasa samun nasara, sanadin hakan ne yasa Aminan ta kashe mijin ta kwashe dukiyarshi ta gudo nan garin shine kasuwanci ya had'asu da Atinen har suka zama aminai.



Mu'az da Fa'iza sun dad'e suna jimamin wannan lamari, Mu'az yace "nima Allah ne ya kareni da ta kasheni, amma ai koma menene ta shuka zataje ta girbi abinta, inda ta tura shi mijin nata na farko ai gashinan shima ta bishi."



Nan dai sukayi sallama bayan ya amsa cewa ya yafe mata sai ta cigaba da neman gafara wajen mahaliccinta, tai mishi godiya sannan tai sallama dasu ta tafi.



_K'alubale a gareku maza da matan ma, sai asan irin abokan rayuwar da za'a rik'a aura, hikimar yin bincike kenan a aure domin gujewa fad'awa hannun baragurbi a cikin mutane, bayan haka mu daina sakaci da addu'a da kuma neman zab'in Allah akan komai, na tabbata kuma makaranta kun tabbatar cewa da ace Mu'az baya sakaci da Azkar to da ba abinda zai sameshi, koda ya sameshi to komai zaizo mishi da sauk'i addu'ar nan da muke sakaci da ita wallahi garkuwace akan komai babu abinda garin maganin malamai zasuyi maka a rayuwa, ba zasu tare maka komai ba illah su saka kayi dakon azabar Allah,  Allah yasa mu dace._



Rayuwar Mu'az da iyalansa abun burgewa, duk abinda ya faru a baya a yanzu ya zama tarihi, Ameer kuwa an fara girma, yanzu yanada shekara goma, inda tuni ya fara zuwa makaranta ta bokon da ta islamiyya,  ana kaishi wajen mahaifiyarsa Fatima akai-akai yana yi mata kwana biyu musamman ma idan sun samu hutu.



B'angaren Anas da matansa kuwa suna zaune lafiya, inda yaran anas suka zama uku kenan, Fareeda nada biyu sai tsohon ciki, ita kuma Fatima tanada guda d'aya.



Fatima ta fi Fareeda hak'uri da kauda kai akan abu.



Ita kuma Fareeda kishinta baya b'oyuwa tana bayyanar dashi komai k'ank'antar abu.



Tabbas Fatima taga rayuwa, kuma itadai tata k'addarar rayuwar kenan, tunda kowa dai ya sani indai akwai kishiya tofa sai hak'uri kawai, domin duk yanda kakai ga son a zauna lafiya tofa dole wata rana sab'ani sai ya shigo.



Suna zaune lafiya amma hakan ba yana nufin wata rana abokiyar zamanta bata cuta mata ba, a'a sedai kawai tana hak'urine da rayuwa da kuma rungumar k'addararta, tana lallab'a rayuwar a yanda tazo mata domin samun dace a aljanna, itama kuma Fatiman dole watarana ta b'ata mata hakan zaman taren ya gada.



Yau Asabar babu aiki, Anas ne zaune a gida shi da matanshi a falo suna hira, sun sakashi a tsakiya kowacce ta d'an kwanto a jikinshi.



Anas ya kallesu yace "dallah ku d'agani karku b'allamin kafad'a," dukkansu suka had'a baki wajen cewa "munk'i."



Anas yace "yauwa kowaccenku ta fad'amin sunan da zata rik'a kirana dashi dan gaskiya banason baban wance baban wanen nan, a sakamin sunan soyayya kawai me dad'i."



Fareeda ta kalleshi tace "to ni na saka maka gishiri, " Fatima tace "ni kuma nasa maka yaji," dukkansu suka kwashe da dariya, Anas yace "wallahi banso, ku canza wani kawai haba wad'annan sunaye sai kace ana filin yak'i," dariya sukaita yi mishi, sannan Fareeda ta sake cewa to nasa maka Darling."



Fatima tace "ni kuma Honey"



Anas yace "to ya na iya nayi maneji hakanan, ni kuma sunan da zan sa muku sunane guda d'aya zan raba muku shi ku biyu," sukace wane sunane haka, Anas yace "ke Fareeda zan rik'a ce miki Sweet, ke kuma Fatima Heart, kunga swthrt ne na raba muku shi ku biyu, " nan suka sa mishi daru akan su wallahi basu yarda ba.



Ashe babbar d'iyarshi ta shigo tanata doka sallama basu jita ba, sai ji sukayi ta rugo da gudu tana cewa Daddy nima wallahi ban yadda ba cai ka camun cunan coyayya," dukkaninsu suka saka mata dariya.









ALHAMDULILLAH
